We find that no candidate satisfies all four axioms simultaneously, that the representations distinguish task type reliably but cannot distinguish between two questions within the same task, and that the representations encode little information beyond what is already present in the input embedding. The failure is consistent across dense, reasoning-distilled, and RL-trained model families, indicating that the gap is structural rather than a property of model size or training procedure.

</details>


### [92] [Position: The Term "Machine Unlearning" Is Overused in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27379)
*Sangyeon Yoon,Yeachan Jun,Albert No*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，"机器遗忘"一词在LLM研究中被过度滥用，应严格限定为"数据集定义下的删除"——即消除指定遗忘集的训练影响，使模型近似等价于重新训练。许多当前被标为"遗忘"的任务实质上属于对齐、抑制、编辑等不同目标，术语混淆导致评估指标被误用。作者呼吁建立更严格的术语体系，使术语与显式保证和参考模型挂钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM研究中，"机器遗忘"(machine unlearning)这一术语被广泛用于描述多种不同目标和任务（如有害请求拒绝、知识移除、行为抑制等），但这些任务的本质并非真正意义上的"消除训练数据影响"。这种术语混淆并非表面问题，它导致不同论文在同一标签下做出不同的隐性保证，评估指标（如ROUGE、遗忘准确率）被跨范围误用，即使未测试"重新训练等价性"也能获得表面良好的分数。作者旨在厘清概念，防止研究社区的混乱加剧。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文(position paper)，采用概念分析和论证的方法：(1) 对"机器遗忘"给出严格定义——数据集定义下的删除，要求模型近似等价于在排除遗忘集后重新训练的结果；(2) 将当前被标为"遗忘"的任务分类映射到不同的范畴：对齐(alignment)、抑制(suppression)、知识编辑(editing)、混淆(obfuscation)等；(3) 分析现有评估指标与真实遗忘目标之间的错配；(4) 提出术语规范化的呼吁与框架。

Result: 论文揭示了当前LLM遗忘研究中的核心问题：多数工作未验证"重新训练等价性"这一遗忘的根本保证，却使用相同的术语和评估基准。表面指标（如低ROUGE分数、高遗忘准确率）可能仅反映模型的表面非披露能力，而非训练影响的真正消除，衍生能力仍然保留。作者将这些发现凝聚为对术语严格性和评估匹配性的呼吁。

Conclusion: "机器遗忘"应被限制用于数据集定义的删除场景，并伴有明确的等价性保证。其他以策略为导向的任务应使用对应术语（对齐、抑制、编辑等），并配套设计匹配的评估方法。术语的严格化绝非"表面工程"，而是确保研究目标、方法、评估三者一致性的关键前提。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly face demands to "forget" training data, knowledge, or behaviors due to regulatory deletion obligations, copyright/licensing disputes, and safety or product-policy requirements. This position paper argues that machine unlearning is overused as a term in LLM research and should be reserved for dataset-defined deletion: removing the training influence of a precisely specified forget set such that the resulting model is approximately indistinguishable from retraining without that data. We contend that many tasks currently labeled "unlearning" (e.g., refusal for harmful requests, entity/knowledge removal, or targeted suppression) pursue different, often policy-dependent objectives and therefore require different terminology and baselines (e.g., alignment, suppression, editing, obfuscation). We further argue that this confusion is not cosmetic: because papers make different implicit guarantees under the same label, metrics and benchmarks are frequently reused outside their intended scope, rewarding surface-level non-disclosure (e.g., low ROUGE/forget accuracy) even when retraining-equivalence is not tested and derived capabilities remain. We conclude by calling for stricter terminology tied to explicit guarantees and reference models, and for evaluations that match the claimed objective.

</details>


### [93] [A Survey of Automated Presentation Coaching: Systems, Methods, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27380)
*Wen Liang,Li Siyan,Zackary Rackauckas,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对自动化演讲口语辅导系统进行了系统综述，沿发音训练、韵律建模和语音合成三个维度对现有系统进行分类，提出五维任务分类法（音段发音、词汇重音、超音段韵律、语速节奏、内容忠实度），并梳理了基于TTS的示例生成与诊断方法，最后指出标注语料稀缺、口音公平反馈及低延迟实时诊断等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动化演讲辅导系统处于CAPT、韵律建模和语音合成的交叉领域，但此前缺乏对这些系统沿上述维度进行系统综述和比较的工作，存在研究空白。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法：(1) 对现有系统按发音导师、流利度与韵律教练、多模态训练器、会议问答练习工具进行分类；(2) 提出五维任务分类法（音段发音、词汇重音、超音段韵律、语速节奏、内容忠实度），并将各系统映射到该框架以揭示覆盖缺口；(3) 梳理核心技术方法，包括基于TTS的示例生成和发音/韵律/流利度诊断方法。

Result: 综述揭示了现有系统在五维任务分类法上的覆盖缺口；梳理了TTS示例生成与诊断方法这两大类核心技术；明确了三个关键开放挑战：标注演讲语料稀缺、面向不同母语背景的口音公平反馈、实时排练所需的低延迟诊断。

Conclusion: 自动化演讲辅导是一个多学科交叉但尚未被充分综述的领域。本文通过系统分类和差距分析，为该领域提供了首个统一的结构化概览，并指明了未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Automated coaching for oral presentations sits at the intersection of computer-assisted pronunciation training (CAPT), prosody modeling, and speech synthesis, yet no prior work has systematically surveyed and compared existing systems along these dimensions. This survey reviews and categorizes automated presentation coaching systems, spanning pronunciation tutors, fluency and prosody coaches, multimodal trainers, and conference Q&A practice tools. We introduce a five-dimensional task taxonomy - covering segmental pronunciation, lexical stress, suprasegmental prosody, pacing, and content faithfulness - and explicitly map surveyed systems onto it to reveal coverage gaps. We further review the core technical methods these systems employ: TTS-based exemplar generation and diagnostic methods for pronunciation, prosody, and fluency assessment. Key open challenges include the scarcity of annotated presentation corpora, achieving accent-fair feedback across diverse L1 backgrounds, and delivering low-latency diagnostics for real-time rehearsal.

</details>


### [94] [Causal Connections: Leveraging Multilingual Fine-Tuning for Financial QA@FinCausal 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27446)
*Akash Kumar Gautam,Serhii Hamotskyi,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了团队 HSA_CORAL 在 FinCausal 2026 共享任务中的参赛系统，任务目标是通过抽取式问答从金融文本中提取因果关系。团队比较了三种模型家族：编码器-only 标记（mBERT）、编码器-解码器生成（mBART）以及解码器-only 大语言模型（Llama 3.1 和 GPT 变体），并探索了提示工程、少样本示例和监督微调等策略。最佳系统 GPT-4.1 Mini 在英西联合数据上微调后，英文子任务并列第一（4.8140），西班牙文子任务排名第三（4.7753）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本中的因果关系抽取对于决策支持至关重要，但现有方法在多语言场景（英语和西班牙语）下的表现仍有待提升。团队旨在系统比较不同模型架构（编码器-only、编码器-解码器、解码器-only LLM）与不同训练/适配策略（提示、少样本、微调）在金融因果问答任务上的效果，并探索多语言微调对跨语言迁移的价值。

Method: 团队构建了三类系统：(1) 基于多语言 BERT 的编码器-only 序列标注模型；(2) 基于多语言 BART 的编码器-解码器生成模型；(3) 基于 Llama 3.1 和 GPT 变体的解码器-only LLM。在 LLM 上，依次尝试了提示词优化、少样本示例演示以及监督微调（SFT）。最终将英文和西班牙文训练数据合并进行多语言微调，以 GPT-4.1 Mini 作为骨干模型，在统一的因果抽取问答框架下进行训练和评估。

Result: 提示工程和少样本示例已能带来有竞争力的表现，而监督微调带来了最大幅度的性能提升。最佳系统——在英西联合训练数据上微调的 GPT-4.1 Mini，在英文子任务中取得并列最高分 4.8140，在西班牙文子任务中以 4.7753 排名第三（均采用 LLM-as-a-judge 指标）。多语言微调有效促进了跨语言迁移。

Conclusion: 任务特定的适配（尤其是监督微调）和多语言联合训练对于金融因果抽取问答系统至关重要。GPT-4.1 Mini 经过英西联合微调后，在跨语言场景中展现出强大的性能，证明了将解码器-only LLM 与多语言微调相结合是处理金融领域因果抽取任务的有效范式。

Abstract: This paper describes team HSA_CORAL's submission to the FinCausal 2026 shared task on extracting cause-effect relations from financial narratives via extractive question answering in English and Spanish. We compare three modeling families: (i) encoder-only token tagging with multilingual BERT, (ii) encoder-decoder generation with multilingual BART, and (iii) decoder-only LLMs (Llama 3.1 and GPT variants) using prompt refinement, few-shot demonstrations, and supervised fine-tuning. Across settings, prompting and few-shot examples yield competitive performance, while supervised fine-tuning provides the largest gains. Our best system, GPT-4.1 Mini fine-tuned on combined English and Spanish training data, achieves a tied highest score on the English subtask (score 4.8140) and ranks third on Spanish (score 4.7753) under the shared task's LLM-as-a-judge metric. Overall, the results highlight the value of task-specific adaptation and multilingual fine-tuning for cross-lingual transfer in financial causality QA.

</details>


### [95] [Developmental approach reveals the statistical learning of Neural Language Models: Transformers generalize from the most abstract statistical patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27460)
*Wang Bojun,Holly Jenkins,Elizabeth Wonnacott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在不同训练阶段对生成式Transformer模型的内部表征进行分析，研究发现神经语言模型先习得抽象的全局统计知识，后习得局部的统计依赖关系，且早期存在大量过度泛化，后期逐步被约束。基于此提出解释NLM统计学习与语言认知的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经语言模型（NLM）的统计学习过程和心智表征机制，尤其是模型如何在训练过程中逐步构建语言知识——是从全局到局部还是反之，以及过度泛化如何产生和被修正。

Method: 采用发展性方法：在人工语法上训练一系列生成式Transformer模型，在训练的多个阶段保存模型状态，分析其内部表征随训练进程的动态变化轨迹。

Result: NLM在学习初期首先习得最抽象的全局统计知识，随后才习得相对局部的统计依赖关系；学习路径从一开始就包含大量过度泛化，这些过度泛化在后期被逐步约束。

Conclusion: 提出了一种“先全局后局部、先泛化后约束”的NLM统计学习框架，为理解神经语言模型的语言认知机制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: In this study, we use a developmental approach to investigate the statistical learning and mental representation of neural language models (NLM). A series of Generative Transformer models are trained on a synthetic grammar. The model states are saved at multiple stages in the course of training. Through analyzing how the internal representations of these models change in the developmental path, we found that NLMs acquire the most abstract global statistical knowledge at the beginning of learning and later acquire the relatively local statistical dependencies. This learning path contains many over-generalizations from the very beginning and these over-generalizations are gradually constrained in the later stage of learning. Based on this observation, we propose a new framework to explain the statistical learning and language cognition of NLMs.

</details>


### [96] [Supersede: Diagnosing and Training the Memory-Update Gap in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27472)
*Vedant Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM智能体在多轮对话中因事实更新而频繁使用过时信息，长上下文下的记忆维护（而非理解能力）构成核心瓶颈。该问题随对话增长而恶化且不随模型规模缓解；作者发布强化学习环境Supersede，首次证明该"事实替代"能力可通过GRPO微调显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在长程、多轮交互中，面临事实动态变化（如用户搬家、价格更新、计划修订）的挑战。现有研究多关注长上下文理解，但忽视了"识别并使用当前有效事实、丢弃已被替代的旧事实"这一独立能力。作者意图量化这一能力缺口，探明瓶颈究竟在于理解还是记忆维护，并探索是否可通过训练加以修复。

Method: 1）在LongMemEval的知识更新子集上，将智能体的完整上下文替换为有界自维护记忆，测量准确率下降（配对McNemar检验）；2）通过将对话长度放大24倍并等比扩容记忆，检验瓶颈是否为记忆容量不足；3）发布Supersede强化学习环境（基于verifiers/prime-rl技术栈），以"使用当前值回答"作为奖励信号、使用过时值作为惩罚；4）对Qwen2.5-3B进行GRPO微调，在真实未见对话上评估替代准确率的提升。

Result: 1）记忆替换后准确率从92%降至77%（GPT-5.4），下降显著（p<0.005），且随模型规模增大，完整上下文准确率趋于饱和（~92%），但记忆条件下的差距持续存在；2）对话增长24倍后准确率从68%降至28%，等比扩容记忆无恢复（28%→28%，n=25），失败与对话长度成正比而非压缩比；3）GRPO微调Qwen2.5-3B后，在真实未见对话上的替代准确率从9.0%提升至16.7%（单次运行），检查点曲线呈单调递增，表明收益来自学习到的策略。

Conclusion: 事实替代（supersession）能力是LLM智能体的一项独立且尚未解决的关键缺陷，瓶颈在于记忆维护而非理解。该问题无法通过扩大模型或记忆容量缓解，但可通过专门设计的强化学习训练环境（Supersede）进行有效训练。这是首个以时序事实时效性为奖励目标的训练环境，也是事实替代差距可被训练缩小的首个证据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents operate over long, multi-session interactions in which facts change: a user moves, a price updates, a plan is revised. Acting correctly requires using the current value of a fact and discarding values that have been superseded. We isolate this ability on real conversational data and show that it is a distinct, unsolved failure. On the knowledge-update subset of LongMemEval, replacing an agent's full context with a bounded, self-maintained memory drops accuracy from 92% to 77% even on a frontier model (gpt-5.4), a gap that is statistically significant (paired McNemar p<0.005) and persists across model scale while full-context accuracy saturates near 92%. The bottleneck is therefore memory maintenance, not comprehension, and is not closed by a stronger model. We then ask whether this is merely an undersized memory, and find it is not: as the conversation grows 24x, accuracy falls further (from 68% to 28%), and granting the agent proportionally more memory yields no detectable recovery (28% to 28%, n=25). The failure scales with the length of the conversation, not the compression ratio. We release Supersede, an open reinforcement-learning environment (on the verifiers / prime-rl stack) that turns this measurement into a training signal: agents are rewarded for answering from the current value and penalized for stale ones. Finally, we close the loop and show the gap is trainable: GRPO fine-tuning a small open model (Qwen2.5-3B) on this environment nearly doubles its held-out supersession accuracy on real, unseen conversations (9.0% to 16.7%, a single run), along a monotonic checkpoint curve indicating the learned policy, not the harness, carries the gain. To our knowledge this is the first trainable environment whose reward targets temporal fact-currency, and the first evidence the supersession gap can be trained down, not only measured.

</details>


### [97] [The Context-Ready Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27538)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Context-Ready Transformer，通过在token进入Transformer Block之前利用修正网络（correction network）融合上一位置的缓存上下文，将其从标准Transformer改造为适用于高效串行推理的循环神经网络架构。训练时以K步展开并行处理，推理时串行生成且速度显著提升，性能可匹配甚至超越更深的常规Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer在自回归生成时每个token需重新计算所有历史上下文，导致推理效率低。作者希望设计一种既能像RNN一样高效串行推理、又能保留Transformer并行训练优势的架构，实现推理加速与性能的兼顾。

Method: 提出Context-Ready Transformer：由一个D层Transformer block加一个修正网络（correction FFN）构成。生成时，修正网络将上一位置的block输出（历史上下文缓存）与当前token embedding融合，使token在进入block前已完成语境化。训练时在完整序列上展开K次修正过程进行并行训练；可将预训练Transformer通过添加零初始化修正FFN并微调来转换。评估涵盖不同宽度、深度、block大小及两个数据集，并与标准Transformer及其变体对比。

Result: D=5模型性能优于12层Transformer，且在A100上生成速度提升1.7倍。K=10时，单层模型（D=1）性能优于6层Transformer，推理加速2.6倍，且串行推理与并行K=10的PPL差异在0.01以内。架构在宽表示和长上下文场景下收益最大。在指针追踪任务上，D=1模型配合BPTT训练可解决全部10个组合层级，而标准Transformer呈现阶梯状深度依赖。

Conclusion: Context-Ready Transformer成功将Transformer改造为高效的循环推理架构，在保持并行训练能力的同时实现了显著的推理加速，尤其适合宽模型和长上下文场景，并在需要复杂组合推理的任务上展现出优于标准Transformer的深度效率。

Abstract: We introduce the context-ready transformer, a new recurrent neural network architecture built from a D-layer transformer block that pre-contextualizes each token before it enters the block. During left-to-right generation, a correction network combines the previous position's block output -- a cached summary of past context -- with the current token embedding, so the tokenenters the block already contextualized rather than as a raw embedding. At sequential inference, the correction chain makes the architecture a recurrent neural network. For training, we unroll the correction process K times over the full sequence, processing all positions in parallel at each step. A pretrained transformer can also be converted to a context-ready model by adding a zero-initialized correction FFN and fine-tuning. We evaluate across widths, depths, block sizes, and two datasets, with all comparisons against standard transformers, variants, and ablations. A D=5 model beats a 12-layer transformer while generating 1.7x faster on an A100. With K=10, a single-layermodel (D=1) beats a 6-layer transformer with a 2.6x inference speedup, and sequential inference matches parallel K=10 to within 0.01 PPL. The architecture benefits most from wide representations and long contexts. On a pointer-chasing task, D=1 trained with BPTT solves all 10 composition levels, while standard transformers exhibit staircase-like depth dependence.

</details>


### [98] [EntMTP: Accelerating LLM Inference with Entropy Guided Multi Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27550)
*Carrie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EntMTP 是一种无需训练的调度器，根据局部生成熵动态切换多token预测的树状注意力拓扑，使推测深度与上下文可预测性相匹配，在保持生成质量的同时最大化吞吐量，在多个基准上相比 Hydra 和 Medusa 分别实现 1.15 倍和 1.36 倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有使用多token预测（MTP）头的模型在整个生成过程中采用固定的树状注意力拓扑，推测深度恒定。这与自然语言的熵模式不匹配：低熵区域可支持激进的多步推测，高熵区域则需要保守策略。这种错位导致算力浪费和效率低下。

Method: 提出 Entropy-guided Multi-Token Prediction (EntMTP)，一种免训练的调度器。核心思路是基于局部生成熵的运行估计，从一组任务特定的帕累托最优树集合中动态切换树状注意力拓扑，使推测深度随上下文可预测性自适应调整，从而最大化预期接受token吞吐量。

Result: 在 Humaneval、ShareGPT、GSM8k 和 Litbench 基准上评估，EntMTP 相比 Hydra 基线一致实现 1.15 倍加速，相比 Medusa 基线峰值加速达 1.36 倍，且不牺牲生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过根据局部熵动态调整推测深度，EntMTP 在不改变模型权重的前提下，实现了跨不同文本分布的稳健加速，证明了熵引导的自适应推测策略在提升多token预测效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction has been shown to increase data density during training, improve downstream text-generation quality, and serves as the defacto approach for self-speculative decoding. Existing foundation and open source models that use MTP heads commit to a static tree-based attention topology throughout the entire generation sequence, meaning the speculation depth, and thus the compute required during verification, stays constant regardless of the context. This is fundamentally misaligned with the entropy patterns of natural language where low-entropy regions often support reliable multi-step drafting, while high-entropy regions require more conservative speculation. To address this, we propose Entropy-guided Multi-Token Prediction (EntMTP), a training-free scheduler that toggles between tree-based attention topologies from a set of task-specific pareto-optimal trees conditioned on a running estimate of local generation entropy. By matching speculation depth to context predictability, EntMTP maximizes expected accepted-token throughput across the full distribution of generated text without sacrificing generation quality. When evaluated across Humaneval, ShareGPT, GSM8k, and Litbench benchmarks, EntMTP consistently achieves a 1.15x speedup against Hydra and peak speedup of 1.36x against Medusa baselines respectively.

</details>


### [99] [Ko-WideSearch: A Korean Breadth-Search Benchmark for Exhaustive Set Enumeration by Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27595)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ko-WideSearch 是一个韩语广度搜索基准，包含 228 张表格、190 个实体、16 个类别，通过自动化合成-验证流水线构建。评估 20 个 web agent 后发现：智能体能恢复集合成员但难以填充完整行数据（Row-F1 仅 53.7），且增加搜索量或预算无法弥合差距；开放式自由文本单元格是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 web agent 基准测试过度衡量"深度"（在约束链中寻找单一答案），几乎不评估"广度"（穷举封闭集合并填充每个项目的属性），尤其在非英语场景中。广度基准构建成本极高，因为验证黄金集合的完整性和每个单元格的正确性远比检查单一答案昂贵。

Method: 提出自动化 synthesize-and-verify 流水线构建 Ko-WideSearch 基准。每个任务指定一个集合父实体（如电视剧季、王朝、联赛等），要求输出完整成员列表及逐项属性表。通过两个独立调节的结构性旋钮控制难度层级：表格宽度和二维复合键，使交叉积成员比例从 0% 到 100% 跨越三个难度层级。评分采用 Item-F1、Column-F1 和 Row-F1。黄金集构建和评分共用同一个归一化感知比较器，避免仅因格式差异错误丢弃稳定的日期和计数列。

Result: 评估 20 个 web agent 发现：智能体能恢复集合但无法恢复行（Item-F1 92.8 vs Row-F1 53.7）；随旋钮硬化准确率持续下降；更多搜索和更多预算均无法弥合差距。按单元格分析，困难在于找到正确值而非格式化：开放式自由文本单元格失败最多，而日期或名称等有标准答案的单元格通常正确。

Conclusion: 当前 web agent 在广度搜索任务上存在系统性缺陷，核心瓶颈是信息检索而非格式化。基准揭示了现有智能体从"找集合"到"填全表"之间存在显著能力鸿沟，呼吁社区更多关注广度评估维度。

Abstract: Web-agent benchmarks overwhelmingly measure depth -- pinning one obscure answer behind a chain of constraints -- while breadth, exhaustively enumerating a closed set and filling each item's attributes, is barely evaluated, especially outside English. Breadth is also hard to build: certifying that a gold set is complete and every cell correct is far costlier than checking a single answer. I introduce \textsc{Ko-WideSearch}, a Korean breadth-search benchmark built by an automated synthesize-and-verify pipeline. Each task names a set-parent entity -- a TV season, a dynasty, a league, an administrative region, an election -- and asks for its full membership plus a per-item attribute table, graded by Item-, Column-, and Row-F1. It spans 228 tables over 190 entities and sixteen categories across three difficulty tiers, set by two structural knobs I dial independently -- table width and a 2-D composite key -- so cross-product membership climbs from 0\% to 100\% across the tiers. A single normalization-aware comparator is shared between gold construction and grading, so stable date and count columns are not over-dropped on formatting alone. Across twenty web agents, the failure is consistent: agents recover the set but not the rows (e.g.\ Item-F1 92.8 against Row-F1 53.7), accuracy falls steadily as the knobs harden, and neither more search nor more spend closes the gap. Broken down by cell, the hard part is finding the right value, not formatting it: open-ended free-text cells fail most, while cells with a standard answer such as a date or a name usually come out right.

</details>


### [100] [Narrative-UFET: Narrative Generation for Ultra-Fine Entity Typing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27598)
*Mreedul Gupta,Advait Deshmukh,Ashwin Umadi,Matt Pauk,Maria Leonor Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Narrative-UFET，通过在超细粒度实体类型任务中引入自动生成的叙事级上下文（而非仅依赖句子级上下文），验证了跨句语篇信息对长尾类型识别的关键作用，并发现合成叙事甚至优于自然文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前UFET方法依赖句子级上下文，但消歧证据往往分散在多个句子中，导致长尾类型识别效果不佳。由于现有UFET资源均为句子级，此前难以验证这一假设。

Method: 构建Narrative-UFET数据集：为每个实体提及自动生成简短连贯的叙事文本。设计两种对照变体——Maintain（实体类型在叙事中保持一致）和Change（实体类型在叙事中发生转换），以隔离特定语篇属性的影响。同时与自然语境进行对比实验。

Result: 叙事上下文在长尾类型上相较句子级基线取得一致提升，Change变体提供更强信号。合成叙事的增益超过自然文本，表明受控的语篇构造可以显式化自然文本中隐含的信号。

Conclusion: 语篇建模和叙事构造是UFET任务中有前景的研究方向，但当前方法仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Ultra-fine entity typing (UFET) assigns highly specific types to entity mentions, but current approaches struggle with types in the long tail. We hypothesize that a key limitation is the reliance on sentence-level context, since disambiguating evidence is often spread across multiple sentences. Testing this has been difficult because all existing UFET resources are sentence-level. We present Narrative-UFET, a controlled extension of UFET in which each entity mention is paired with an automatically generated short, coherent narrative. Synthesizing narratives lets us isolate the effect of specific discourse properties. We experiment with two paired variants: one in which the entity's type is held constant across the narrative (Maintain) and one in which it shifts (Change). We show that narrative context yields consistent improvements on long-tail types over sentence-level baselines, with the Change variant providing the stronger signal. A comparison against naturally occurring contexts shows that synthetic narratives yield stronger gains, indicating that controlled discourse construction can surface signals that real text leaves implicit. Substantial room for improvement remains, suggesting open directions in both discourse modeling and narrative construction.

</details>


### [101] [Masked Language Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27617)
*Iskander Azangulov,Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Leo Zhang,Simon Vary,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Masked Language Flow Models (MLFMs)，将掩码机制融入流语言模型，结合连续流与离散去掩码，并设计交替采样器以支持多步推理，首次证明基于流的语言模型可扩展至推理与指令跟随任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型(MDMs)在少步采样时因跨位置的因子分解近似而退化；流语言模型(FLMs)虽可通过蒸馏实现单步生成，但在需要多步推理的复杂任务中表现不佳，因其必须在生成时解码每个token。本文旨在融合两者优势，克服各自局限。

Method: 提出MLFMs，通过连续随机插值(continuous stochastic interpolant)连接部分掩码序列与干净序列，将掩码引入FLMs。该设计支持通过连续流进行条件生成，并可将预训练MDMs轻量适配转换为MLFMs。进一步提出交替连续去噪与基于置信度的离散去掩码的采样器，以支持多步推理。

Result: 在GSM8K和MT-Bench上评估，首次证明基于流的语言模型可扩展至下游推理和指令跟随任务。

Conclusion: MLFMs成功融合掩码机制与连续流模型，通过交替采样策略有效支持多步推理，为流语言模型在复杂语言任务中的应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) promise fast, parallel language generation, but their reverse transition factorises across token positions -- an approximation that breaks down in the few-step sampling regime where parallel generation ought to provide the greatest efficiency gains. Flow Language Models (FLMs) sidestep this limitation by learning a continuous flow that transports noise toward clean sequences represented in Euclidean space, inducing a flow map that can be distilled for single-step generation. However, this makes complex tasks requiring multi-step reasoning problematic for FLMs, as FLMs are forced to decode every token during generation. To address this, we introduce Masked Language Flow Models (MLFMs), which incorporate masking into FLMs using a continuous stochastic interpolant to bridge partially masked and clean sequences. This design enables conditional generation via continuous flows and allows pretrained MDMs to be converted into MLFMs through a simple, lightweight adaptation. Leveraging this flexibility, we propose a novel sampler that alternates continuous denoising with the discrete unmasking of confident tokens to better support multi-step reasoning. We evaluate our approach on GSM8K and MT-Bench and find, for the first time, that flow-based language models can be scaled to solve downstream reasoning and instruction-following tasks.

</details>


### [102] [Cross-Platform Chinese Offensive Comment Detection via Dual-Threshold Hard Example Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27629)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Fangfang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对中文社交媒体攻击性评论检测在跨平台部署时性能下降的问题，提出了一种双阈值难例挖掘方法。通过在源域微调RoBERTa建立基线，构建多平台测试集并量化域间距离以揭示性能退化瓶颈，最终利用预测置信度双阈值筛选难例进行二次微调，实现低成本跨平台域自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文社交媒体攻击性评论检测模型在跨平台部署时面临显著的性能退化问题（域偏移），而已有方法通常依赖大量目标平台标注数据，成本高昂。因此，需要一种低成本、高效的跨平台域自适应方法。

Method: （1）基线段：在COLD数据集上微调clean-Chinese-base RoBERTa，建立二分类基线。 （2）域偏移量化：构建覆盖微博、小红书、贴吧、知乎的三分类精细化标注测试集，使用Jaccard距离和Proxy-A Distance量化源域与各目标域之间的域距离，系统揭示域偏移下的性能退化瓶颈。 （3）双阈值难例挖掘：利用基线模型对无标注语料进行预测，通过高置信度和低置信度双阈值筛选易错样本（高置信度误判与低置信度不确定样本），仅需少量人工标注的难例，即可在隐式语境下对模型进行二次微调，实现低成本跨平台域自适应。

Result: 实验表明，经过双阈值难例挖掘策略优化后的模型在微博、小红书、贴吧、知乎四个平台上均取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的双阈值难例挖掘方法能够以极低的人工标注成本有效缓解跨平台域偏移问题，显著提升中文社交媒体攻击性评论检测的跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-platform deployment of offensive comment detection for Chinese social media suffers performance degradation. The paper proposes a dual-threshold hard mining method to address this. First, the clean-Chinese-base RoBERTa is finetuned on COLD to establish a binary baseline for fair comparison. Second, a three-class fine-labeled test set covering Weibo, Xiaohongshu, Tieba, and Zhihu is constructed, domain distances from the source are quantified using Jaccard and Proxy-A Distance, as well as the degradation bottleneck of the baseline under domain shift is systematically revealed. Herein, a dual threshold hard example mining strategy is proposed. High- and low-confidence error-prone samples are filtered from unlabeled corpora by prediction confidence. The model is secondarily finetuned under implicit contexts with merely a small set of manually labeled hard examples, realizing low-cost cross-platform domain adaptation. Experiments reveal significant performance gains of the optimized model across four platforms.

</details>


### [103] [Yuvion LLM: An Adversarially-Aware Large Language Model for Content And AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27632)
*Ting Ma,Xiufeng Huang,Benlei Cui,Xiaowen Xu,Shikai Qiu,Ruijie Jian,Hongxing Li,Guanghui Wang,Longtao Huang,Haiwen Hong,Haolei Xu,Wenjing Jiang,Ziwen Xu,Zhaoyu Fan,Shaoxuan He,Chuxi Xiao,Yujian Li,Xinyue Chen,Chunyang Chai,Wenxuan Liu,Ziheng Wang,Dongjie Zhang,Yangfan Zhou,Libin Dong,Yupeng Cao,Xiaoqian Xia,Jing Wang,Zhe Jiang,Zhenan Ye,Guang Yang,Bin Liu,Wei Peng,Ziqiang Zhu,Meihui Lian,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Haidong Ding,Bingyu Zhu,Yan Wang,Hai Zhao,Xuan Jin,Wei Zhao,Pengfei Sun,Wei Wang,Huiming Zhang,Bin Li,Hui Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Yuvion LLM 是一个专为对抗性鲁棒内容安全设计的大语言模型，将对抗鲁棒性和智能体能力作为核心目标，通过对抗感知数据构建、知识增强持续预训练、策略导向多任务安全后训练（含风险感知SFT和RL策略优化）以及安全感知的智能体强化学习，在93项安全基准上展现出领先的对抗鲁棒性，Yuvion-8B在多项安全任务中甚至超越GPT-5.4和Qwen3-MAX等更大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用模型开发忽视了安全的对抗本质——安全失败往往源于对模型策略的战略性绕过，而非自然输入。当前方法不足以应对涉及规划、工具使用和多步推理的现实安全场景，导致测量的安全性能高估了真实部署鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Yuvion LLM，其训练流程包括：(1) 对抗感知数据构建；(2) 知识增强持续预训练；(3) 策略导向多任务安全后训练，包含风险感知监督微调(SFT)和基于强化学习的策略优化；(4) 安全感知的智能体强化学习，用于处理工具使用和多步推理等复杂安全场景。同时构建了YLRE评估体系，包含93个基准、四类评估类别。

Result: Yuvion LLM在安全基准上展现出明显优势，在对抗条件下鲁棒性尤为突出，同时保持扎实的综合能力。Yuvion-8B在多项安全任务上超越包括GPT-5.4和Qwen3-MAX在内的大多数SOTA基线模型。

Conclusion: 将对抗鲁棒性和智能体能力作为大语言模型安全的一等公民目标加以优化是有效的，Yuvion LLM证明了中等规模模型通过针对性训练可在安全性和对抗鲁棒性上超越更大规模的通用模型。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in real-world systems, safety failures can still lead to harmful outputs and dangerous misuse. We argue that the essence of safety is adversarial: many failures arise not from natural inputs alone, but from strategic attempts to evade model policies and safeguards. However, existing general-purpose model development largely overlook this adversarial nature, and often remain insufficient for realistic safety scenarios involving planning, tool use, and multi-step reasoning, causing measured safety performance to overestimate real deployment robustness. To address this gap, we present Yuvion LLM, a large language model built for adversarially robust content safety and broader AI safety. Yuvion LLM treats adversarial robustness and agentic capability as first-class objectives. Its pipeline combines adversarially aware data construction, knowledge-enhanced continued pretraining, and policy-grounded multi-task safety post-training, including risk-aware supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning-based policy optimization, together with safety-aware agentic reinforcement learning for tool use and multi-step reasoning in complex safety scenarios. We further introduce the Yuvion LLM RiskEval (YLRE), a collection of 93 benchmarks across four evaluation categories, covering diverse open and internal evaluations with a focus on safety, adversarial robustness, and real-world capability requirements. Across these evaluations, Yuvion LLM demonstrates clear advantages on safety-focused benchmarks and particularly strong robustness under adversarial conditions, while maintaining solid overall capability. Notably, Yuvion-8B outperforms most state-of-the-art baselines, including substantially larger models such as GPT-5.4 and Qwen3-MAX, on several safety tasks.

</details>


### [104] [When Search Agents Should Ask: DiscoBench for Clarification-Aware Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27669)
*Yiling Tao,Shihan Deng,Meiling Tao,Pengzhi Wei,Zhichao Hu,Zhihao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoBench 是一个面向"澄清感知深度搜索"的评测基准，包含211个样本、463个模糊实例，覆盖11个领域和4种模糊类型。实验表明，当前LLM搜索代理在歧义检测与有效澄清方面存在明显短板，反复搜索而不主动澄清的表现甚至不如直接猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理评测基准假设用户查询是完整且明确的，但真实场景中用户查询常存在模糊、欠指定甚至事实错误。在深度搜索的多步推理链中，这种模糊性会传播并导致错误的搜索轨迹。当前缺乏专门评估搜索代理是否能够主动识别模糊、提出有效澄清问题并通过用户交互恢复正确推理路径的基准。

Method: 提出 DiscoBench 基准，包含211个样本和463个模糊实例，横跨11个真实世界领域，覆盖4种模糊类型（模糊、欠指定、事实错误等）。设计了多轮交互的用户模拟器，从四个维度评估模型性能：任务效用（task utility）、模糊检测（ambiguity detection）、交互策略（interaction strategy）和成本效率（cost efficiency）。

Result: 在代表性LLM上的实验揭示：(1) 模糊检测与有效澄清是两种独立的能力；(2) 反复搜索而不请求澄清的表现通常比直接猜测更差；(3) 当前搜索代理在检索能力与交互式问题解决之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: DiscoBench 揭示了当前LLM搜索代理在澄清感知深度搜索中的关键不足：模糊检测与澄清能力相互独立，且盲目搜索策略有害无益。这为未来具备交互式问题解决能力的搜索代理研发指明了方向。

Abstract: Search agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to solve complex information-seeking tasks, requiring multi-step retrieval and reasoning to fulfill user goals. However, existing benchmarks often assume that user queries are complete and explicit, overlooking the fact that real-world search requests are frequently vague, underspecified, or even factually incorrect. In deep search scenarios, such ambiguity can propagate along multi-step reasoning chains and lead agents toward incorrect search trajectories. To address this gap, we introduce DiscoBench, a benchmark for clarification-aware deep search, designed to evaluate whether search agents can proactively identify ambiguity, ask effective clarification questions, and recover correct reasoning paths through user interaction. DiscoBench contains 211 samples and 463 ambiguity instances across 11 real-world domains, covering four ambiguity types. We further design a user simulator for multi-turn interaction and evaluate model performance from four perspectives: task utility, ambiguity detection, interaction strategy, and cost efficiency. Experiments on representative LLMs show that ambiguity detection and effective clarification are distinct capabilities, and that repeatedly searching instead of asking for clarification often performs worse than direct guessing, highlighting a critical gap between retrieval ability and interactive problem-solving in current search agents.

</details>


### [105] [From Signals to Transfer: A Factorised Study of Probe-Based Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27679)
*Ponhvoan Srey,Xiaobao Wu,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Quang Minh Nguyen,Duc Anh Vu,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对基于探针的大语言模型不确定性估计方法进行了因子化研究，系统比较了特征设计、训练数据构建和评估设置等因素对性能的影响，发现域内性能不足以衡量进展，分布偏移下结构化压缩特征更鲁棒，并提出了可迁移到开放式生成的预训练探针基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于探针的不确定性估计方法在特征设计、训练数据构建和评估设置上各不相同，导致无法明确哪些因素真正驱动性能提升，阻碍了该领域的实质性进展。

Method: 在匹配条件下对探针式不确定性估计进行因子化研究，系统分离并比较不同特征（原始隐藏状态、注意力特征、结构化压缩特征）、训练数据构建方式（提示策略、标签构造）以及评估设置（域内 vs. 分布偏移）对性能的影响。

Result: 域内条件下，原始隐藏状态和注意力特征难以被超越；但在分布偏移下，结构化和压缩特征表现出更强的鲁棒性。提示策略和标签构造对探针行为有显著影响。基于最佳实践训练的预训练探针能够合理迁移到开放式事实生成任务。

Conclusion: 域内性能单独不足以衡量不确定性估计方法的进展，应更多关注面向部署的评估，特别是分布偏移下的鲁棒性。预训练探针可作为一种稳定的现成基线供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Probe-based uncertainty estimation (UE) has emerged as a prominent approach to detect hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) by learning uncertainty from internal model signals. Yet, recent methods vary simultaneously across feature design, training data construction, and evaluation setting, obscuring what actually drives performance. To address this issue, we propose a factorised study of probe-based UE under matched conditions. Our results show that raw hidden states and attention features are difficult to outperform in-domain. However, under distribution shift, structured and compressed features are more robust, suggesting that in-domain performance alone is insufficient to measure progress. Furthermore, prompting and label construction significantly affect probe behaviour. Building on these best-practice findings, we train benchmark-based pretrained probes that transfer reasonably well to open-ended factual generation, providing a stable off-the-shelf baseline. Our work encourages more deployment-oriented evaluation of probe-based uncertainty estimators. The code repository is available at https://github.com/ponhvoan/ProbeUE.

</details>


### [106] [Mitigating LLM-based p-Hacking by Preregistering for the Next LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27687)
*Maria Thomas,Kristina Gligoric,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究者提出一种预注册协议来缓解LLM研究中的p-hacking问题：在预注册之后发布的首个合格LLM上运行确认性分析，因为该模型在承诺时不存在，无法被针对性操纵。在两个真实值已知的任务上评估，协议分别阻止了73.9%和72.7%的p-hacking迁移，且预注册实验证实了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于生成、分类和标注数据，这些输出会馈入下游假设检验。然而，研究者可以通过调整提示词、解码参数或输出格式来轻易实现p-hacking，即人为操纵以达到统计显著的结果。这种自由度威胁了基于LLM研究的科学严谨性和可复现性，亟需一种机制来约束这种自由度。

Method: 提出预注册协议：研究者先在当前模型上确定完整分析流程，然后预注册该分析计划以及一组合格未来模型的入选标准；确认性分析必须在预注册之后发布的首个合格LLM上运行。由于该模型在预注册时尚未存在，无法被针对性地黑入（hack）。作者在20个模型、4个供应商、11种LLM分析配置下，于两个真实值已知的任务上评估协议效果，并进行多项压力测试，最终通过自身预注册实验验证协议。

Result: 在两个任务中，协议分别阻止了73.9%和72.7%的p-hacking成功迁移。压力测试表明缓解效果仍然显著。作者实际执行预注册后，在预注册后发布的首个合格模型上，7个黑入前序模型的配置中有6个未能成功迁移，确认了协议有效性。

Conclusion: 预注册协议能有效缓解LLM研究中的p-hacking问题。由于黑入配置在不同模型间迁移性差，且预注册后发布的新模型在承诺时不可得，该方法从机制上切断了研究者针对性操纵的可能，为LLM-based研究的科学规范性提供了实用保障。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate, classify, and annotate data whose outputs feed downstream hypothesis tests. However, LLM-based research is easy to p-hack: a researcher can tune the prompts, decoding parameters, or output format until a desired result is reached. We propose a protocol to mitigate p-hacking in LLM-based research: preregistering the experiment and eligible models, and then running it on the first eligible LLM that is released after the preregistration. The researcher finalizes the procedure on current models, preregisters the analysis plan together with a set of eligible future models, and runs the confirmatory analysis on the first eligible model released afterward. Because this model does not exist at commitment time, it cannot be hacked against; furthermore, configurations that hack one model frequently do not transfer to the next. We evaluate the protocol on two tasks whose true values are known. Across 20 models from four providers and 11 LLM-analysis configurations, the protocol would have blocked successful transfer of the p-hack in 73.9% and 72.7% of cases in the two tasks. Additional analyses reveal that mitigation remains substantial under several stress tests. Finally, putting money where our mouth is, we followed our own protocol and preregistered our experiment. The preregistered experiment confirmed the protocol's effectiveness: out of the 7 configurations that hacked the prior model, the hacking failed to carry over in 6 configurations on the first eligible model released afterward.

</details>


### [107] [Mitigating Position Bias in Transformers via Layer-Specific Positional Embedding Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27705)
*Changze Lv,Zhenghua Wang,Yiran Ding,Yixin Wu,Tianlong Li,Zhibo Xu,Muling Wu,Tianyuan Shi,Shizheng Li,Qi Qian,Xuanjing Huang,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层特定位置嵌入缩放方法（LPES），通过遗传算法和贝塞尔曲线为每层分配不同的RoPE缩放因子，无需微调或增加推理延迟，有效缓解LLM长上下文中的“中间丢失”问题，在关键值检索任务上实现最高11.2%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在处理长上下文时存在“lost-in-the-middle”问题，即位于输入中间位置的关键信息容易被忽视或丢失。现有基于多尺度RoPE的方法存在推理延迟高或依赖次优的手工缩放策略等缺陷，亟需一种更高效、自动化的解决方案。

Method: 提出LPES方法，核心思路是为Transformer每一层分配不同的RoPE缩放因子，以平衡注意力分布。采用遗传算法自动搜索最优的逐层缩放因子，并引入贝塞尔曲线参数化缩放因子的变化轨迹，大幅压缩搜索空间，提升搜索效率。该方法无需微调模型参数，也不增加推理延迟。

Result: 在多个长上下文基准测试上，LPES有效缓解了位置注意力偏差，取得了持续稳定的性能提升，尤其在关键值检索数据集上准确率提升高达11.2%。

Conclusion: LPES通过层特定的位置嵌入缩放策略，结合贝塞尔曲线引导的遗传算法，以零额外推理开销的方式显著改善了LLMs的长上下文理解能力，为解决“中间丢失”问题提供了一种简洁且高效的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with the ``lost-in-the-middle'' problem, where critical information located in the middle of long-context inputs is often underrepresented or lost. While existing methods attempt to address this by combining multi-scale rotary position embeddings (RoPE), they typically suffer from high latency or rely on suboptimal hand-crafted scaling strategies. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a layer-specific positional embedding scaling~(LPES) method that assigns distinct scaling factors to each layer. LPES achieves a more balanced attention distribution without fine-tuning model parameters or increasing inference delay. A specially designed genetic algorithm is employed to efficiently select the optimal scaling factors for each layer by incorporating Bézier curves to significantly reduce the search space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LPES effectively mitigates positional attention bias and delivers consistent improvements across multiple long-context benchmarks, yielding up to an $11.2$\% accuracy gain on the key-value retrieval dataset.

</details>


### [108] [Low-Agreeableness Persona Conditioning for Safe LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27709)
*Austin MY Cheung,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在温暖微调数据中引入低宜人性（low agreeableness）的用户角色条件，同时配以温暖、缓和冲突的助手回应，可以在保持对话温暖度的前提下，显著降低模型的越狱敏感性和有害输出率，表明通过数据设计本身即可实现更安全的共情微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，为增强社交温暖度而对LLM进行微调会损害事实可靠性并增加谄媚倾向。本文关注一个相关但未被充分探索的失败模式：温暖微调也会削弱模型的对抗安全性，使其更容易被越狱攻击并生成有害内容。作者试图探明这究竟是共情适应的固有结果，还是数据构建方式造成的人为偏差。

Method: 提出一种基于角色驱动的重写流水线（persona-driven rewriting pipeline）：将对话中的用户轮次条件化为低宜人性人格，同时为助手生成温暖且具有缓和冲突（de-escalating）风格的回应。在4个模型上开展3组实验，比较该方法与通用温暖微调基线在越狱敏感性和有害输出率上的差异，并通过表征探测（representational probing）分析温暖方向与服从方向在潜在空间中的几何对齐程度。

Result: 相较于通用温暖微调基线，所提方法显著降低了越狱攻击成功率和有害输出比例，同时保持了对话温暖度。表征探测提供了提示性证据：该条件化策略降低了潜在空间中"温暖"方向与"服从"方向之间的几何对齐程度。

Conclusion: 更安全的共情微调可以仅通过数据设计实现，无需依赖安全标签、有害内容检测器或修改训练目标。这表明温暖微调带来的安全脆弱性并非共情适应的固有结果，而是可以通过精心构造训练数据来缓解的。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) for social warmth degrades factual reliability and increases sycophancy. We investigate a related but distinct failure mode: warmth fine-tuning also weakens adversarial safety, making models more susceptible to jailbreaks and harmful output generation. We examine whether this reflects an inherent consequence of empathetic adaptation or an artifact of data construction. To address this, we introduce a persona-driven rewriting pipeline that conditions user turns on low agreeableness and pairs this with warm, de-escalating assistant responses. Across three experiments on four models, our approach reduces jailbreak susceptibility and harmful output rates relative to generic warmth fine-tuning baselines, while preserving conversational warmth. Representational probing provides suggestive evidence that this conditioning reduces the geometric alignment between warmth and compliance directions in latent space. These results show that safer empathetic fine-tuning is achievable through data design alone, without safety labels, harm detectors, or changes to the training objective.

</details>


### [109] [Do Speech Emphasis Models Generalize across Languages and Emotions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27717)
*Megan Wei,Deepali Aneja,Jiaqi Su,Yunyun Wang,Haonan Chen,Zeyu Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 引入MMEE多语多情感强调语料库（7种语言、34种情感/风格，10,000句，三层感知标注），系统基准测试显示多语言训练显著提升跨语言鲁棒性，高低唤醒度情感间可稳健迁移，合成与感知基准间存在共享韵律结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韵律强调检测模型主要基于单语中性朗读语音训练和评估，缺乏对跨语言、跨情感、跨说话风格泛化能力的系统研究，需要构建多语多情感标注语料库来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建MMEE语料库（7种语言、34种情感/风格类别、10,000句专业录制表达性语音，三层感知强调标注，每样本10人标注）；在单语、跨语言、多语言、跨情感、跨数据集及不同数据规模下，对两种SOTA架构（强调检测模型）进行系统基准测试。

Result: 单语模型零样本跨语言迁移能力有限，语言类型距离越大性能越差；多语言联合训练显著提升鲁棒性；高低唤醒度情感间模型可稳健双向迁移；合成数据基准与感知标注基准之间的双向迁移表明存在共享的韵律结构；较小训练数据规模下性能仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 多语言多情感联合训练是提升强调检测模型泛化能力的有效策略，跨情感和跨数据集的稳健迁移揭示了韵律强调中普遍存在的共享声学-感知结构，为构建通用强调检测系统提供了数据资源和方法参考。

Abstract: Prosodic emphasis varies across languages, emotions, and speaking styles, yet existing emphasis detection models are largely trained and evaluated on monolingual neutral read speech. We introduce MMEE (Multilingual Multi-Emotion Emphasis), a corpus of 10,000 professionally recorded expressive utterances (14.13 hours) across 7 languages and 34 emotion/style categories, with three-level perceptual labels (10 annotations per sample). We benchmark two state-of-the-art architectures under monolingual, cross-lingual, multilingual, cross-emotion, cross-dataset, and data-scale settings. Monolingual models show limited zero-shot transfer, degrading across typologically distant languages, while multilingual training substantially improves robustness. Models transfer robustly between high- and low-arousal emotions; bidirectional transfer between synthetic and perceptual benchmarks suggests shared prosodic structure; and performance stays robust even at smaller training scales.

</details>


### [110] [Enhancing Numerical Prediction in LLMs via Smooth MMD Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27731)
*Zhuo Zuo,Li Yue,Wenhao Zheng,Chenpeng Wang,Xianggen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM在数值精确输出上的不可靠性，本文提出SMMD损失函数，通过在数值子词表上引入值距离核与图平滑性，将预测分布与目标分布进行核匹配，并在核图上平滑残差，从而在数学推理、算术计算、时钟识别和图表问答等任务上持续提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失将数值token视为无结构类别，忽略了数值之间的度量结构（如大小关系、距离），导致LLM在需要精确数值输出的任务上表现不可靠。

Method: 提出SMMD（Smooth Maximum Mean Discrepancy）损失：(1) 在数值子词表上定义值距离核（value-distance kernel），捕捉数值token间的度量关系；(2) 通过MMD（Maximum Mean Discrepancy）将预测数值分布与目标分布进行核匹配；(3) 在核诱导图上对预测-目标残差进行平滑，鼓励局部一致性。

Result: 在数学推理、算术计算、时钟时间识别和图表问答四个数值目标任务上，跨多个开源LLM和VLM骨干网络进行评测，SMMD在精度上持续优于交叉熵损失和近期的数值目标损失函数。消融分析表明MMD与平滑性具有互补效应，并验证了基于距离的核设计的重要性。

Conclusion: SMMD通过结合MMD核匹配和图平滑性，有效弥补了交叉熵损失忽略数值度量结构的缺陷，为LLM在数值精确输出任务上提供了一种通用且有效的训练目标。

Abstract: Despite their strong general capabilities, large language models (LLMs) often remain unreliable when outputs must be numerically precise. A key reason is the training objective: standard cross-entropy treats numeric tokens as unstructured categories and ignores the metric structure of their values. We address this mismatch with Smooth Maximum Mean Discrepancy (SMMD), which builds on the classic MMD by incorporating value-distance kernels over numeric tokens and graph-based smoothness. With this kernel defined over a numeric sub-vocabulary, SMMD aligns the predicted numeric distribution to the target via kernel matching and smooths the prediction-target residual over the induced kernel graph to encourage local consistency. We evaluate SMMD on four numeric-target tasks: mathematical reasoning, arithmetic calculation, clock-time recognition, and chart question answering, across multiple open-weight LLM and VLM backbones. SMMD consistently improves accuracy over both cross-entropy and recent numeric-target losses; analyses show complementary effects between MMD and smoothness and underscore the importance of distance-based kernel design. Code is available at https://github.com/Zuozhuo/smmd-loss.

</details>


### [111] [KG2Cypher: Data-Centric Pipeline for Building Enterprise Text-to-Cypher Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27742)
*Minjun Choi,Yerin Kim,Junghyuk Seo,Sujin Mo,Hyemin Lee,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KG2Cypher 提出一种数据驱动管线，通过从企业知识图谱自动生成 Text-Cypher 训练对，结合 LoRA 微调与类别条件提示，在韩语企业场景中将执行结果 F1 提升至 0.964。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为私有企业知识图谱构建自然语言接口（Text-to-Cypher）成本高昂，且韩语短查询、模式转述等特点使语言 grounding 尤为困难，亟需一种低成本、自动化的高质量训练数据生成方案。

Method: KG2Cypher 采用三阶段管线：（1）从图谱事实反向构造可执行 Cypher 查询，再用 LLM 生成其对应自然语言问题，形成 Text-Cypher 对；（2）通过 LLM 评判器与人工校验双轮验证，转换为候选感知的 SFT 数据；（3）在推理端采用类别条件模式提示、实体检索和 LoRA 推理服务训练后的生成器。

Result: 在韩语企业场景中，LoRA SFT 将广播节目查询的执行结果 F1 从 0.806 提升至 0.950，公司查询从 0.70 提升至 0.92。在 11 分类设定下，达到 95.2% 精确匹配率、99.9% 执行率和 0.964 的执行结果 F1。

Conclusion: KG2Cypher 验证了从现有知识图谱自动生成高质量微调数据以构建企业级 Text-to-Cypher 系统的可行性，数据驱动方案在韩语复杂企业查询场景中表现出显著性能优势。

Abstract: Enterprise Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are increasingly used for internal search, analytics, and question answering, but building natural-language interfaces for private enterprise graphs remains costly. We present KG2Cypher, a data-centric pipeline for building enterprise text-to-Cypher systems from existing KGs. KG2Cypher first constructs an executable Cypher query from observed graph facts and then uses LLMs to generate its associated natural-language question. The resulting Text-Cypher pairs are validated with an LLM judge and human validation, and are converted into candidate-aware SFT data. The trained generator is served with class-conditioned schema prompting, entity retrieval, and LoRA-based inference. We evaluate KG2Cypher in Korean enterprise settings, where short search-style queries and schema paraphrases make language grounding difficult. LoRA SFT improves execution-result F1 from 0.806 to 0.950 on broadcast-program queries and from 0.70 to 0.92 on company queries. In an 11-class setting, KG2Cypher achieves 95.2% exact match, 99.9% execution rate, and 0.964 execution-result F1.

</details>


### [112] [Output-Space Allocation Costs for Calibration-Guided LLM Compression: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27785)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM压缩中分配目标函数的影响。针对ROCKET方法中权重空间Frobenius误差与输出空间重建目标不一致的问题，提出ROCKET-ActCost，将MCKP分配代价与输出空间目标对齐。实验发现：高压缩率下存在accuracy-perplexity权衡，但两种误差高度相关（>0.99）限制了效果；低压缩率下差异可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练LLM压缩方法ROCKET存在目标不一致问题：其稀疏字典分解使用输出重建目标，但MCKP层间分配却使用权重空间Frobenius误差。作者探究将分配代价与输出空间目标对齐是否能提升压缩模型保真度。

Method: 提出ROCKET-ActCost，将MCKP层间分配的代价函数从权重空间Frobenius误差替换为输出空间重建误差（基于校准数据激活值计算），使分配目标与分解目标保持一致。实验在Qwen3-8B（50%压缩率）和Llama-3.2-1B（20%压缩率）上进行对比评估。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B 50%压缩率下，ROCKET-ActCost在8个零样本基准上平均准确率提升0.8个百分点（53.1% vs 52.3%），但WikiText困惑度恶化16%（61.46 vs 52.98），呈现accuracy-perplexity权衡。权重空间与输出空间误差的相关性超过0.99，限制了分配差异。在Llama-3.2-1B 20%低压缩率下，两者结果几乎一致（准确率53.3% vs 53.5%，PPL 14.45 vs 14.66），说明低压缩率下代价函数影响微弱。

Conclusion: 对齐MCKP分配代价与输出空间目标可小幅提升下游任务准确率，但会损害困惑度，揭示了不同分配目标偏好不同下游指标。然而由于权重空间与输出空间误差高度相关（>0.99），实际分配差异有限，效应量不大。低压缩率下代价函数选择几乎无影响。

Abstract: Training-free compression methods for large language models (LLMs) often use calibration data to guide compression decisions. ROCKET, a recent method combining sparse-dictionary factorization with multi-choice knapsack problem (MCKP) allocation, derives its per-layer factorization from an output reconstruction objective but uses weight-space Frobenius error as the MCKP allocation cost. We investigate whether aligning the allocation cost with the output-space objective improves compressed model fidelity. On Qwen3-8B at 50\% compression, our ROCKET-ActCost achieves +0.8 percentage points higher average accuracy across 8 zero-shot benchmarks (53.1\% vs 52.3\%), but increases WikiText perplexity by 16\% (61.46 vs 52.98). This accuracy-perplexity tradeoff reveals that different allocation objectives favor different downstream metrics. The high correlation ($>$0.99) between weight-space and output-space errors limits allocation divergence, explaining the modest effect size. On Llama-3.2-1B at 20\% compression, the two methods produce near-identical results (53.3\% vs 53.5\% accuracy, 14.45 vs 14.66 PPL), suggesting that the effect of the cost function is minor at lower compression ratios.

</details>


### [113] [SHIFT: Gate-Modulated Activation Steering for Knowledge Conflict Mitigation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27786)
*Ruochang Li,Pengcheng Huang,Zhenghao Liu,Yukun Yan,Huiyuan Xie,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHIFT 提出了一种可学习的门控调制框架，用于解决 RAG 系统中检索上下文与模型参数知识之间的冲突，通过冻结骨干网络、仅训练不到 0.01% 参数的门控模块，实现自适应调节内部激活，避免传统神经元编辑方法对模型通用能力的损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决 RAG 知识冲突的方法主要在神经元层面识别并编辑与知识相关的内部神经元，但这些修改可能产生级联效应，因为被修改的神经元往往与模型的广泛行为和功能高度纠缠，从而损害 LLM 的通用能力。因此需要一种更优雅、低侵入性的方案。

Method: SHIFT 将神经元级别的修改重新形式化为可学习的门控调制（learnable gate modulation）。具体做法是：在冻结骨干模型的前提下，为 LLM 配备一个轻量级门控模块，优化参数占比不到 0.01%。生成过程中，该门控模块调整模型的内部表征，使模型能够自适应地利用上下文知识和参数化知识。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验验证了 SHIFT 的有效性，与多种竞争基线方法相比表现出优越性能。数据集和代码已在 GitHub 上开源。

Conclusion: SHIFT 通过极低参数量的门控调制，成功实现了 RAG 场景下的知识冲突自适应消解，在保持模型通用能力的同时有效提升了对上下文证据的依赖能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances LLMs by incorporating external knowledge to support response generation. However, conflicts between retrieved context and parametric knowledge have emerged as a critical challenge in RAG systems. To mitigate such conflicts, numerous studies have attempted to identify and edit knowledge-related internal neurons, aiming to improve the ability of LLMs to rely on contextual evidence during generation. However, these neuron-level approaches may introduce unintended cascading effects that compromise the general capabilities of LLMs, as the modified neurons are often entangled with broader model behaviors and functionalities. In this paper, we introduce SHIFT, a novel framework that reformulates neuron-level modification as learnable gate modulation, allowing LLMs to adaptively regulate internal activations for knowledge conflict resolution. Technically, our SHIFT equips LLMs with a lightweight gate module and optimizes fewer than 0.01% trainable parameters while keeping the backbone model frozen. During generation, the gate module adjusts the model's internal representations to adaptively leverage contextual and parametric knowledge. Extensive experiments on six datasets validate the effectiveness of our SHIFT in comparison with various competing baselines. All datasets and code are available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/SHIFT.

</details>


### [114] [NLL-Guided Full-Attention Layer Selection for Training-Free Sliding-Window Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27791)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的层选择方法NLL-guided layer selection，通过计算每层使用滑动窗口注意力替代全局注意力时答案token的负对数似然退化量，直接衡量各层对下游任务准确度的重要性，从而优化混合注意力模型中全局注意力层的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力模型（混合全局注意力和滑动窗口注意力）是高效长上下文推理的有前景方案，但关键问题——哪些层应保留全局注意力——尚未解决。现有方法采用固定周期模式或基于注意力的启发式规则，无法准确捕捉对下游准确度至关重要的因素。

Method: 提出NLL-guided layer selection方法：对每层逐一将全局注意力替换为滑动窗口注意力，衡量答案token的负对数似然（NLL）退化量，NLL退化越大表明该层对全局注意力需求越高，据此选择保留全局注意力的层。该方法无需训练，仅需约15分钟一次性校准。

Result: 在LongMemEval基准上使用Qwen3-4B模型：仅用1/4全局注意力层即达到64.6%准确率，匹配1/2全局注意力层的周期基线（65.0%），同时计算量减半；比SWAA报告的1/4-FA周期基线高10.4个百分点，比匹配的LightTransfer式基线高26.4个百分点。去混杂分析表明该信号与长程注意力需求一致，而非通用层敏感性。

Conclusion: NLL-guided layer selection有效推进了长上下文LLM部署的效率-准确率帕累托前沿，以极低的校准成本实现了对关键层的精准识别。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models that mix full and sliding-window attention across layers offer a promising approach to efficient long-context inference, but the critical question of \emph{which layers} should retain full attention remains unsolved. Existing methods use either fixed periodic patterns or attention-based heuristics that may not capture what matters for downstream accuracy. We propose NLL-guided layer selection, a training-free method that directly measures each layer's importance by computing the negative log-likelihood degradation on answer tokens when that layer uses sliding-window instead of full attention. On LongMemEval with Qwen3-4B, our method achieves 64.6\% accuracy using only 1/4 full-attention layers, matching the 1/2-FA periodic baseline (65.0\%) while halving the computational budget. NLL-guided selection outperforms the SWAA-reported periodic 1/4-FA baseline by 10.4 percentage points and a matched LightTransfer-style baseline by 26.4 percentage points. De-confounding analysis shows the signal is consistent with long-range attention needs rather than generic layer sensitivity. The method requires only $\sim$15 minutes of one-time calibration, advancing the efficiency-accuracy Pareto frontier for long-context LLM deployment.

</details>


### [115] [Position Bias Correction is Insufficient for One-Pass Attention Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27793)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了长上下文语言模型中的位置偏差问题。作者假设位置偏差是主要瓶颈，提出了"去偏单次注意力排序"方法，通过估计位置偏差曲线来修正原始注意力分数，实现单次排序。然而实验结果表明，在LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct上，去偏与未校准单次排序效果相同（94.83%包含准确率）；在YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k上虽有8.67个百分点提升，但仍落后迭代排序14.84个百分点，仅缩小了37%的差距。结论是位置偏差修正不足以匹敌迭代排序，重复重排序带来了偏差修正之外的额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型存在位置偏差（position bias），即中间位置的信息利用不足。现有方法"Attention Sorting"通过多轮"排序-生成"循环来缓解此问题，但部署成本高。作者希望探究：位置偏差是否是唯一瓶颈？能否通过简单的去偏矫正，用单次排序达到迭代排序的效果，从而降低部署成本？

Method: 提出Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting（去偏单次注意力排序）。核心思路：从注意力较低的"多数文档"中估计每条提示（per-prompt）的位置偏差曲线，然后通过减法或除法用该曲线修正原始注意力分数，从而实现单次排序，避免多轮迭代。在LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct和YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k两个模型上进行对比实验，与未校准单次排序和迭代Attention Sorting进行比较。

Result: 1. LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct上，去偏方法产生与未校准单次排序完全相同的结果（94.83% containment accuracy）。2. YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k上，去偏方法比未校准单次排序提升8.67个百分点，但仍落后迭代排序14.84个百分点，仅弥补了37%的差距。

Conclusion: 实验结果推翻了"位置偏差是主要瓶颈"的假设。单纯的位置偏差修正不足以匹敌迭代Attention Sorting的性能，表明重复的"排序-生成"循环提供了超越偏差修正的额外收益（如迭代精炼、上下文重组等更深层的机制）。

Abstract: Long-context language models suffer from position bias, where information in middle positions is underutilized. Attention Sorting addresses this by iteratively reordering documents based on attention patterns, but its multiple sort-and-generate cycles increase deployment cost. We hypothesize that position bias is the primary bottleneck and propose Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting, which estimates a per-prompt position-bias curve from the low-attention majority of documents and uses it to correct raw attention scores (via subtraction or division) to enable single-pass sorting. Our experiments on two models refute this hypothesis in the tested setting: on LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct, debiasing produces identical results to uncalibrated single-pass sorting (94.83\% containment accuracy), while on YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k, debiasing improves accuracy by 8.67 percentage points but remains 14.84pp behind iterative sorting, closing only 37\% of the gap. These results suggest that position-bias correction is insufficient to match iterative sorting, and that repeated reordering provides additional benefits beyond bias correction.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Complementary Action Modeling from Automotive Maintenance Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27808)
*Jiaqi Wu,Bai Li,Jochen Hartmann,Martin Gaedke,Sander Stuijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文定义了"互补动作建模"（CAM）任务，即在保持句子其余部分不变的情况下，仅通过修改动作短语来识别或生成维护指令的程序性对立指令。研究表明，互补指令由细微词汇线索决定，不应被视为普通的句子相似性或同义词释义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到，在汽车维修指令中，微小的词汇变化即可逆转指令的程序含义，而实体、修饰语和上下文保持不变。现有方法易将此类互补指令误判为相似或同义表达，因此需要一个专门的任务来捕捉这种由动作短语决定的程序性对立关系。

Method: 使用德语汽车维修数据集，通过候选匹配（candidate matching）和受控的Seq2Seq生成两种方式对CAM任务进行研究。评估结合了检索指标、重叠度指标和人工评估，从区分互补性与表面相似性、动作短语级别的受控生成、以及关系正确性三个维度进行考察。

Result: 互补维护指令最好被建模为基于细微词汇线索的程序性关联，传统的句子相似性或同义词释义方法不适用于此类任务。

Conclusion: 互补动作建模是一个独立且有价值的NLP任务，需要超越表面相似性，关注由动作短语触发的程序性语义反转。该任务对精准理解技术指令具有重要意义。

Abstract: A minute lexical variation can reverse the procedural meaning of an instruction even when the rest of the sentence remains unchanged. In automotive maintenance instructions, this pattern often appears when an action phrase turns an instruction into its procedural counterpart. The entities, modifiers, and surrounding context remain largely invariant, while the action phrase determines the procedural relation. We define this task as Complementary Action Modeling (CAM). Given a maintenance instruction, the goal is to identify or generate its procedural counterpart by modifying the action phrase while preserving the remaining sentence context. This task focuses on three aspects: distinguishing complementarity from surface similarity, controlling generation at the action-phrase level, and evaluating relational correctness using retrieval, overlap-based, and human evaluation. Using a German automotive maintenance dataset, we examine these questions through candidate matching and controlled Seq2Seq generation. The results show that complementary maintenance instructions are best modeled as procedural associations grounded in subtle lexical cues. They should therefore not be treated as ordinary cases of sentence similarity or synonym-based paraphrasing.

</details>


### [117] [A Study of Temporal Fusion Strategies for Named Entity Recognition in Historical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27881)
*Emanuela Boros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了将时间元数据通过轻量级融合策略（交叉注意力、适配器、拼接）嵌入NER模型的方法，发现在历史文本NER中，后期融合策略在时间泛化性上表现更优，尤其在早期和噪声时期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文本中的命名实体识别面临时间变化带来的独特挑战：实体的表面形式和显著性会随时间漂移。语言模型在时间推理尤其是历时性语境方面的能力有限或存疑，因此需要探索如何将时间信息结构化地注入NER模型。

Method: 系统研究将时间元数据结构化嵌入NER模型的轻量级融合策略：使用绝对和相对两种时间表示方式，通过早期或后期融合机制（交叉注意力、适配器、拼接）注入到基于Transformer的架构中。在法语和德语历史数据集上进行评估。

Result: 后期融合策略（late fusion）在时间泛化性上表现更稳健，尤其在早期时期和高噪声时期优势明显。相对时间表示与绝对时间表示的效果因融合策略和数据集而异。

Conclusion: 将时间元数据通过后期融合策略注入NER模型能够有效提升模型在历时性场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为历史文本NER中的时间建模提供了实用的设计指导。

Abstract: Temporal variation poses a unique challenge for named entity recognition (NER) in historical texts, where entities drift in surface form and salience across time. While language models (LMs) have made progress in various NLP tasks, their ability to reason about temporality, especially in diachronic contexts, remains limited or at least, questionable. In this paper, we systematically study how temporal metadata can be structurally embedded into NER models using a range of lightweight fusion strategies. We experiment with both absolute and relative temporal representations, injected into Transformer-based architectures via early or late fusion mechanisms such as cross-attention, adapters, and concatenation. Our evaluations on French and German historical datasets reveal that late fusion strategies yield more robust and temporally generalisable performance, particularly in early and noisy periods.

</details>


### [118] [Triadic Werewolf: A Jester Role for Multi-Hop Theory of Mind in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27909)
*Avni Mittal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在狼人杀游戏中引入"小丑"（Jester）这一第三方角色（其获胜条件为被投票出局），构建三元激励结构来评估大语言模型的心智理论能力。实验表明，传统二元推理游戏会掩盖模型在多智能体博弈中的真实推理水平，而三元结构能有效暴露模型在模拟对手效用函数方面的深层缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的心智理论评估多采用二元社交推理游戏，模型仅凭强语言先验即可获得高分，而无需真正模拟对手的激励结构。作者试图通过引入三元激励结构，揭示当前模型在多智能体递归推理层面的真实能力与局限。

Method: 在经典狼人杀游戏中引入第三方角色"小丑"——该角色因被投票淘汰而获胜，其效用函数与其他阵营完全相反。在GPT-4.1、DeepSeek-V3.1和Llama-3.3-70B上运行60局游戏，分别开启和关闭小丑角色的自我学习（self-learning）机制，分析各阵营胜率及具体投票行为。

Result: 小丑胜率达60-70%，狼人胜率从未超过20%。GPT-4.1扮演的狼人在60-70%的局中于第一天即投票淘汰小丑，属于严格自我挫败行为。自我学习对DeepSeek和Llama有正向帮助，但对GPT-4.1产生负面影响，且代价主要由村民承担。仅DeepSeek学会了"看起来可疑但又不显得刻意"的微妙策略，并从自我学习循环中获益最多。

Conclusion: 三元激励结构暴露了二元推理游戏所无法揭示的多智能体推理层面问题。当前顶级大语言模型在处理对立效用函数的递归推理时仍存在显著缺陷，且模型间的推理能力和学习行为存在本质差异。

Abstract: Theory-of-mind evaluations of large language models typically use dyadic social-deduction games, where every observable cue points to a single hidden side, so a model with strong language priors can score well without ever simulating opponents' incentives. We extend the Werewolf game with a Jester, a third faction whose utility on peer suspicion is inverted because it wins by being voted out, so optimal play requires reasoning across three opposing utility functions. Across 60 games on GPT-4.1, DeepSeek-V3.1, and Llama-3.3-70B with Jester self-learning on and off, the Jester wins 60-70% of games while Werewolves never exceed 20%, and GPT-4.1 wolves vote the Jester out on day 1 in 60-70% of games, a strictly self-defeating action. Self-learning helps DeepSeek and Llama but hurts GPT-4.1, with the cost landing on Villagers rather than Werewolves. Only DeepSeek learns the subtle strategy of looking suspicious without looking intentionally suspicious, and it gains the most from the loop. Triadic incentive structure exposes a layer of multi-agent reasoning that dyadic deduction games leave invisible.

</details>


### [119] [VASAE: Naming SAE Dictionary Directions with Vocabulary-Aligned Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27941)
*Kairui Zhang,Ziwen Yu,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VASAE 提出了一种词汇对齐的稀疏自编码器，在训练期间将每个 SAE 特征锚定到与最近 token 嵌入对应的内在 token 名称上，在不牺牲重建质量的前提下实现约 90% 的特征对齐率，为字典学习提供内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SAE 学到的特征通常需要事后（post hoc）人工命名，缺乏与 Transformer 词汇表的直接联系。作者旨在在训练过程中就为每个特征赋予内在的 token 名称，实现特征与词汇的系统性对齐。

Method: 提出 VASAE，在标准 SAE 训练中引入词汇对齐锚定（vocabulary-aligned anchoring）机制，将每个特征与最近的 token 嵌入绑定，从而赋予其内在 token 名称。通过计算最近 token 对齐分数并以 0.8 为阈值评估对齐质量。实验在 GPT-2-small 和 Llama-3.1-8B 上开展，并引入减去句子级均值稀疏编码的案例分析。

Result: 在 GPT-2-small 上，0-10 层约 90% 特征实现对齐；Llama-3.1-8B 浅层字典对齐率达 92.8%，中间层也表现良好，但末层对齐有限。减去句子均值稀疏编码后，剩余的内在 token 名称与邻近输入 token 语义相关。

Conclusion: 词汇对齐锚定能在训练期间将 SAE 特征与内在 token 名称连接，作为事后解释字典的有力补充，为 Transformer 表征的可解释性研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide useful decompositions of Transformer residual streams, but their learned features are usually named post hoc rather than directly connected to the Transformer's token vocabulary. We introduce Vocabulary-Aligned Sparse Autoencoder (VASAE), a method that trains SAE features under vocabulary-aligned anchoring and assigns each feature an intrinsic token name: the token string whose embedding is nearest to that feature. Without reducing reconstruction quality compared with a standard SAE, VASAE produces dictionaries with vocabulary-aligned features. Using a 0.8 cutoff on the nearest-token alignment score, dictionaries trained on GPT-2-small post-residual streams align about 90% of features in layers 0--10. In Llama-3.1-8B, representative shallow and middle-layer dictionaries contain strongly aligned features, including 92.8% in the shallow layer, while the representative final-layer dictionary shows limited alignment. After subtracting the sentence-level mean sparse code, case studies show that many remaining intrinsic token names are relevant to nearby input tokens. These results suggest that vocabulary-aligned anchoring can connect learned features to intrinsic token names during training, complementing post hoc interpretation of learned dictionaries.

</details>


### [120] [An Empirical Analysis of Factual Errors in Human-Written Text and its Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27959)
*Kazuma Iwamoto,Kazumasa Omura,Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出大语言模型时代下，人类撰写文本的事实错误检测（FED）被忽视。作者通过分析报纸勘误，提炼出人类事实错误的分类体系（如汉字误转换、量词错误等），并基于此构建评估基准。实验表明，即使GPT-5.4等高性能LLM在合成数据上仅取得52%的词级F1分数，揭示了该任务的挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的兴起，研究焦点转向检测LLM生成文本中的幻觉（hallucinations），而人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测（FED）被相对忽视。本文旨在填补这一空白，重新关注人类写作中的事实错误问题。

Method: 首先，通过分析报纸文章勘误（保证为人类撰写且语法错误少），提炼出人类事实错误的分类体系，识别出汉字误转换（kanji misconversions）、数量词分类器错误（numeral classifier errors）等特有类别。其次，基于该分类体系构建合成和真实的测试用例。最后，使用包括GPT-5.4在内的多种LLM在词级别上进行FED能力评估，并按检测难度进行细粒度分析。

Result: 实验结果显示，即便是GPT-5.4等高性能LLM，在合成评估数据上的词级F1分数仅有52%，表明该任务难度较大。按难度分层的分析进一步揭示了当前FED能力的现状与不足。

Conclusion: 人类撰写文本的事实错误检测仍是一项极具挑战性的任务，现有高性能LLM表现远未令人满意。该领域亟需更多关注，论文构建的分类体系和评估基准为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Factual Error Detection (FED), which is the task of identifying factually incorrect spans in a given text, has long been recognized as an important research problem. However, with the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), research attention has shifted toward factual errors specific to LLM-generated text (hallucinations) and their detection. As a result, the detection of factual errors in human-written text has been relatively neglected. To address this gap, we first distill a taxonomy of human-induced factual errors by analyzing corrections of newspaper articles, a representative source of text that is guaranteed to be human-written and contains few grammatical errors. Our analysis revealed that there are characteristic categories such as kanji misconversions and numeral classifier errors, which are not focused in existing hallucination benchmarks. Based on the taxonomy, we then evaluate the FED capability of vanilla LLMs on synthesized realistic test cases and real corrections. Experimental results demonstrated that even high-performance LLMs such as GPT-5.4 achieved only word-level F1 score of 52% on the synthetic evaluation data, highlighting the task difficulty. Furthermore, a detailed analysis by detection difficulty revealed the current state of FED.

</details>


### [121] [From Black-Box to Clinical Insight: A Multi-Stage Explainable Framework for Speech-Based Cognitive Impairment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27973)
*Yasaman Haghbin,Sina Rashidi,Ali Zolnour,Fatemeh Taherinezhad,Ali Fartoot,Hossein Azadmaleki,James M Noble,Maryam Dadkhah,Maryam Zolnoori*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多阶段可解释性框架，将基于Transformer的认知障碍语音检测模型的黑箱预测，通过SHAP归因、语言学特征和LLaMA-3.1-70B推理管线转化为临床可理解的叙述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语音的认知障碍检测虽具非侵入性和可及性优势，但Transformer模型的预测缺乏临床可解释性，阻碍了其在实际医疗场景中的部署和医生对其决策的信任。

Method: 提出多阶段可解释性框架：(1) SHAP-based token归因，识别对预测贡献最大的语音/文本片段；(2) 融合理论驱动的语言学特征（词汇丰富度、句法复杂度、语义连贯性等四个认知-语言维度）；(3) 基于LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct的四阶段LLM推理管线，将模型输出映射为临床叙述。底层模型为SpeechCARE-Adaptive Gating Network多模态筛查模型。

Result: 在NIA PREPARE基准上底层模型F1达72.11%；医生对70个分层英语样本的评估表明框架输出与患者级认知画像高度一致；系统可用性量表（SUS）得分82/100，显示较高的临床工作流整合潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将黑箱Transformer预测转化为临床可理解的叙述，实现了与医生评估的良好对齐，并展现出临床部署的可行性。

Abstract: Speech-based cognitive impairment detection offers a noninvasive, accessible alternative to costly biomarker assays, yet transformer-based models remain clinically uninterpretable. We propose a multi-stage explainability framework that translates black-box transformer predictions into clinically grounded narratives by integrating SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based token attribution, theory-informed linguistic features, and a four-stage LLM reasoning pipeline using LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct. Built on the SpeechCARE-Adaptive Gating Network multimodal screening model (F1 = 72.11% on the NIA PREPARE benchmark), the framework maps model outputs to four cognitive-linguistic dimensions, including lexical richness, syntactic complexity, and semantic coherence. Physician evaluation on 70 stratified English samples demonstrated strong alignment with patient-level cognitive profiles, and a System Usability Scale score of 82/100 indicated high potential for clinical workflow integration.

</details>


### [122] [ToxiREX: A Dataset on Toxic REasoning in ConteXt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27981)
*Stefan F. Schouten,Ilia Markov,Piek Vossen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToxiREX是一个多语言、上下文感知的有毒推理数据集，包含六种语言的Reddit评论线程及其结构化标注，用于捕捉隐式、上下文依赖的毒性内容。数据集包含约12.5万条LLM标注的训练样本和近3000条人工标注的测试样本，并提供了基线模型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毒性检测数据集存在三个主要局限：(1)多聚焦于单语言，缺乏多语言覆盖；(2)忽略对话上下文，无法捕捉隐式毒性；(3)标注粗糙，难以解释毒性背后的推理逻辑。该工作旨在填补这一空白，通过结构化推理模式同时解决多语言、对话上下文和隐式毒性三个维度的挑战。

Method: 基于先前工作中开发的"toxic reasoning schema"（有毒推理模式），对Reddit评论线程进行结构化标注；收集与特定重大事件相关的六种语言（英、阿、土、西、德、荷）评论；进行保留上下文的线程预处理；使用商业LLM标注约12.5万条训练数据，由母语者人工标注约3000条测试数据；通过提示和微调语言模型建立基线，并开发针对层次化、基于模式预测的评估策略。

Result: 测试集中标注者之间的分歧往往反映了合理且可辩护的替代性解读，而非单纯的标注噪声；现有语言模型虽优于随机基线，但整体表现仍有很大提升空间，表明该任务具有较高的挑战性。

Conclusion: ToxiREX是首个同时整合多语言、对话上下文和隐式毒性，并使用有毒推理模式进行丰富结构化标注的数据集，为毒性检测研究提供了一个更具现实复杂性的基准。

Abstract: We introduce a new, contextual, multilingual dataset called ToxiREX: Toxic REasoning in ConteXt. The dataset consists of threads of Reddit comments and structured characterizations of what the comments imply, following a systematic toxic reasoning schema developed in a previous paper. Using the schema allows us to capture and explain implicit and context-dependent toxicity, while supporting mappings to existing toxicity taxonomies. The dataset includes comments in six languages (English, Arabic, Turkish, Spanish, German, and Dutch), collected from posts connected to specific major events (e.g. the 2023 Turkey earthquakes; the Russian invasion of Ukraine). We describe the context-preserving preprocessing of the threads. We create a training set of 125 thousand comments which is annotated by a commercially available LLM, and a test set of just under three thousand comments that is annotated by native speakers. We show that apparent disagreements in the test set annotations often reflect defensible alternative interpretations rather than noise. Finally, we provide baseline results by prompting and fine-tuning language models. To produce these results, we develop evaluation strategies for our hierarchical, schema-based predictions. While models perform better than random, there remains a lot of room for improvement, showing the task to be challenging. ToxiREX is the first dataset to simultaneously incorporate multiple languages, conversational context, and implicit toxicity, while using the toxic reasoning schema for rich, structured annotations. Dataset available at: https://github.com/cltl/toxirex

</details>


### [123] [Dialogue to Detection: A Multimodal Hybrid NLP Pipeline for Insurance Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28002)
*Muhammad Shakeel Akram,Amal Htait,Abdul Hamid Sadka,Emma Meisingseth,Karishma Jaitly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种合成多模态框架，模拟车险FNOL场景，结合ASR、说话人识别、NER、LLM-RAG检索和规则评分，实现跨案例语音复用、叙事不一致等欺诈信号的多模态检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险欺诈在FNOL阶段早期检测仍面临挑战，现有方法多依赖私有的纯文本数据集，难以融合语言、行为和说话人等多模态线索，限制了研究进展。

Method: 构建合成多模态FNOL框架：生成客服-客户对话文本及双人音频，进行ASR与说话人分割聚类；下游融合NER、正则特征提取、LLM-RAG检索及说话人嵌入，形成基于规则的风险评分，识别叙事复用、结构不一致及跨案例声纹重复。

Result: 数据集验证与组件级评估表明框架具有稳定性和迁移潜力，在平衡敏感度与误报的同时，为超越纯文本欺诈检测提供了可复现基线。

Conclusion: 该合成多模态框架有效整合文本与声纹等多维度指标，为FNOL阶段的保险欺诈早期检测提供了可复现、可迁移的新范式，弥补了纯文本方法的不足。

Abstract: Insurance fraud imposes substantial financial losses and operational inefficiencies, raising premiums and impacting trust among legitimate policyholders. Early detection at FNOL remains a persistent challenge. Existing approaches rely largely on private, text-only datasets, limiting progress on multimodal methods that integrate linguistic, behavioural, and speaker-based indicators. We introduce a synthetic multimodal framework that replicates FNOL conditions. It generates agent-customer dialogue transcripts and two-speaker audios, performs ASR and diarisation. Downstream modules combine NER, regex-based feature extraction, LLM-RAG retrieval, and speaker embeddings in a rule-based risk score to flag narrative reuse, structural inconsistencies, and cross-case voice repetition while balancing sensitivity and false positives. Dataset validation and component-level evaluations show stability and transfer potential, offering a reproducible baseline beyond text-only fraud detection.

</details>


### [124] [The Signal-Coverage Matrix: Stratifying Type and Semantic Errors in Statement Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28013)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Zhanhui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种信号-覆盖矩阵，将LLM自动形式化的输出按Lean精化器通过/失败和语义等价判断交叉分为四类（TS/TO/SO/BF），揭示了标量TC%指标掩盖的错误修复细节，并量化了不同反馈方法的层级恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动形式化的类型正确率（TC%）从~53%提升至~76%，但标量指标无法揭示各类方法具体解决了哪些错误。需要更细粒度的分析框架来解构这一进步。

Method: 构建信号-覆盖矩阵（signal-coverage matrix），将Lean精化器的通过/失败信号与语义等价判断交叉，输出四种结果：真成功（TS）、仅类型正确（TO）、仅语义正确（SO）、双双失败（BF）。在ProofNet#和MiniF2F-test数据集上，用DeepSeek V4-Pro对比Vanilla、Lean-Retry、Sample-Filter和分层自动形式化（SAF）四种方法。

Result: （1）三种精化反馈方法的TS增益+34至+36中，约64%来自类型层级恢复，SO基本持平（87.5%的原始语义错误被修复，仅新增8个）；（2）每种方法的TO→TS转化率为23/61（95%CI [26.6%,50.3%]），该层级恢复率可预测留出方法的ΔTS，误差在2/186以内，且ΔTC在六个（模型，数据集）单元中与Vanilla精化失败率线性相关（R²=0.96）；（3）两个评判器在精化反馈输出上分歧达26-37个百分点（Vanilla仅7pp），其中30-56%的符号评判器假阴性可追溯至精化器强制重写。最终残差归结为两个黄金形式化错误。

Conclusion: TC%的增益应归因于具体哪些单元发生了移动，而非仅关注标量提升。四分类矩阵提供了比单一标量更丰富的诊断信息。

Abstract: Headline type-correctness (TC\%) of LLM autoformalization has climbed from $\sim$53\% to $\sim$76\% in two years, yet this scalar conceals which errors each method resolves. We propose a signal-coverage matrix that crosses the Lean elaborator (pass/fail) with a semantic-equivalence judgment (equivalent/not), sorting every output into one of four cells: true success (TS), type-only (TO), semantic-only (SO), or both fail (BF). On ProofNet\# and MiniF2F-test with DeepSeek V4-Pro across Vanilla, Lean-Retry, Sample-Filter, and Stratified Autoformalization (SAF): (1) the +34 to +36 TS gain across the three elab-feedback methods is $\sim$64\% type-stratum recovery, with SO flat on net (87.5\% of original semantic errors rescued, 8 newly created). (2) The TO-to-TS rate is 23/61 for each method (Wilson 95\% CI [26.6\%, 50.3\%]), and this stratum-level recovery rate predicts $Δ$TS on held-out methods to within 2/186 and renders $Δ$TC linear in the Vanilla elab-fail rate across six (model, dataset) cells ($R^2=0.96$). (3) The two judges disagree by 26 to 37 pp on elab-feedback outputs (vs. 7 pp on Vanilla), with 30 to 56\% of symbolic-judge false negatives traceable to elaborator-forced rewrites. The persistent residual reduces to two gold-formalization errors. TC\% gains should be credited by which cell moved, not by the scalar alone.

</details>


### [125] [A Tree-of-Thoughts Inspired Hybrid Approach for Legal Case Judgement Summarization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28044)
*Aniket Deroy,Kripabandhu Ghosh,Saptarshi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受"思维树"启发的抽取-生成混合式法律判决摘要方法，使用DeepSeek和LLama两种大模型进行实验，结果表明该混合方法优于纯抽取或纯生成式摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律判决摘要领域现有研究多集中于传统抽取式或生成式方法，而抽取-生成混合技术尚未得到充分探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出一种受"思维树"(Tree-of-Thoughts)启发的抽取-生成混合式摘要方法(extractive-abstractive summarization)，并基于DeepSeek和LLama两种大语言模型，系统对比抽取式、生成式以及混合式三种摘要提示策略。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的抽取-生成混合式提示(extractive-abstractive prompt)相比其他类型的LLM提示策略，能够生成更优的法律判决摘要。

Conclusion: 在LLM法律判决摘要任务中，受思维树启发的抽取-生成混合方法优于传统单一范式，为法律文本摘要提供了一种更有效的技术路径。

Abstract: In recent times, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for legal case judgement summarization. Most prior works have tried traditional extractive and abstractive summarization of case judgements. However, hybrid or extractive-abstractive techniques have not been explored much. In this work, we propose a novel tree-of-thoughts inspired extractive-abstractive summarization approach for legal judgement summarization. We conduct experiments using two popular LLMs, DeepSeek and LLama, and compare among extractive, abstractive and extractive-abstractive summarization. Our experiments show that the proposed extractive-abstractive prompt provides better summaries compared to other types of LLM prompts.

</details>


### [126] [Can LLMs Judge Better Than They Generate? Evaluating Task Asymmetry, Mechanistic Interpretability and Transferability for In-Context QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28050)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验检验了"评估比生成更容易"这一核心假设，发现在上下文问答场景中，生成准确率在四个基准中的三个上超过自我评估，只有多跳推理任务例外，注意力分析揭示了评估时对上下文关注度显著低于生成的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-Judge和自我评估流程（如自我验证、自我修正）隐含假设"评估比生成更容易"。但这一假设缺乏严格的实证检验，尤其是在排除参数化知识干扰的受控场景中。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用受控的上下文问答设置：给定一段上下文段落作为唯一信息源，模型需先生成答案，再对该答案进行自我评估（判断正确性）。在SQuAD 2.0、DROP、HotpotQA、MuSiQue四个基准上测试两个模型。通过注意力权重分析比较生成与评估阶段对上下文和候选答案的关注模式，并利用LoRA微调实验排除训练偏差的混淆。

Result: （1）在四个基准中的三个上（SQuAD 2.0、DROP、HotpotQA），生成准确率超过自我评估准确率；仅多跳推理MuSiQue例外；（2）注意力分析显示评估阶段对上下文的关注度仅为生成阶段的1/5至1/3，且几乎不读取候选答案；（3）LoRA微调实验证实该不对称性非训练伪影：针对生成微调会诱发过度接受，针对评估微调则降低生成质量。

Conclusion: "评估比生成更容易"的假设在多数上下文QA场景中不成立，这对依赖自我评估的LLM流程（如自我修正、推理时验证）构成根本性挑战，需重新审视这些方法的理论基础。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge and self-evaluation pipelines implicitly assume that evaluation is easier than generation. We test this in a controlled in-context QA setting where a context passage is the sole information source and each model judges the answer it generated, removing the parametric-knowledge confound of open-domain comparisons. Across four benchmarks (SQuAD 2.0, DROP, HotpotQA, MuSiQue) and two models, evaluation is not uniformly easier: generation accuracy exceeds self-evaluation on three of four, with multi-hop MuSiQue the exception. Attention analysis reveals why: evaluation attends to context 3--5x less than generation does and barely reads the candidate answer. LoRA fine-tuning confirms the asymmetry is not a training artifact: generation fine-tuning induces over-acceptance and evaluation fine-tuning degrades generation. These findings challenge core assumptions in self-evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [127] [MultiHashFormer: Hash-based Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28057)
*Huiyin Xue,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiHashFormer 提出一种基于哈希的自回归语言模型框架，通过多个独立哈希函数将每个 token 映射为唯一的哈希签名（离散哈希 ID 序列），再由 Hash Encoder 压缩为潜在向量供 Transformer 解码器处理，Hash Decoder 则生成下一个 token 的哈希签名。在 100M、1B、3B 参数规模上，该方法在多个基准上一致优于标准 Transformer，且能以恒定参数量支持多语言词汇扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语言模型的嵌入矩阵参数量随词表大小线性增长，成为参数瓶颈。此前针对 encoder-only 模型的哈希嵌入方案虽能提升参数效率，但多对一的哈希碰撞使其无法用于因果语言模型（自回归生成）。本文旨在解决这一限制，设计一种既能保持参数效率又支持自回归生成的哈希表示方法。

Method: 提出 MultiHashFormer 框架：(1) 使用多个独立哈希函数为每个 token 生成唯一的哈希签名——短序列离散哈希 ID；(2) Hash Encoder 将该签名压缩为单一潜在向量；(3) 标准 Transformer 解码器在该潜在空间上进行自回归处理；(4) Hash Decoder 预测下一个 token 的哈希签名；(5) 将哈希签名映射回文本 token。

Result: 在 100M、1B、3B 三个参数规模下进行评测，MultiHashFormer 在多个基准上一致超越标准 Transformer 语言模型。此外，该模型无需任何修改即可在恒定参数量的前提下处理多语言词汇扩展。

Conclusion: MultiHashFormer 成功将哈希表示扩展到自回归语言模型中，突破了此前哈希嵌入仅适用于 encoder-only 模型的局限，在保持参数效率的同时实现了有竞争力的生成性能，并天然支持多语言场景下的词表扩展。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) represent tokens using embedding matrices that scale linearly with the vocabulary size. To constrain the parameter footprint, prior work proposes hashing many tokens into a single vector within encoder-only models. While this offers parameter efficiency, many-to-one collisions prevent its use in causal LMs. In this paper, we propose MultiHashFormer, a new framework that allows hash-based autoregression. Each token is represented as a unique hash signature, a short sequence of discrete hash IDs, generated by multiple independent hash functions. A Hash Encoder compresses this signature into a single latent vector for processing by a Transformer decoder. Then, a Hash Decoder generates the hash signature of the next token, which is then mapped back to text. We evaluate our approach at the 100M, 1B and 3B parameter scales, demonstrating that MultiHashFormer consistently outperforms standard Transformer LMs across multiple benchmarks. Furthermore, we show that our model handles multilingual vocabulary expansion with a constant parameter footprint without any modifications.

</details>


### [128] [Mechanism-Driven Monitors for Preemptive Detection of LLM Training Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28116)
*Ruixuan Huang,Yipei Wang,Wenyi Fang,Hantao Huang,Yifan Huang,Ansheng You,Zhenxing Zhang,Shuai Wang,Fan Wu,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于机制的LLM训练不稳定性检测方法，通过从注意力模块的QK频谱熵和MoE路由器的专家选择功能中推导内部监控信号，在损失函数崩溃前数千步即可检测到故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型训练消耗大量计算资源，训练不稳定导致的故障代价高昂。现有的数值故障或超参数故障在训练动态已失稳后，损失和梯度范数可能仍表现正常，持续数千步后才显现，因此亟需更早期的机制驱动检测方法。

Method: 作者从各关键模块的功能角色出发，在故障最早可能产生可测量信号的计算位置推导内部监控指标：对于低精度Flash Attention，监控QK双线性分解的频谱熵，其一阶项在损失崩溃前即出现异常；对于MoE路由器，从专家选择的功能角色推导指标。通过故障注入实验（低精度注意力故障、大学习率故障及组合故障）验证这些信号的有效性。

Result: 不同的故障类型在内部监控信号上产生明显不同的特征签名。这些机制驱动的监控指标能在损失函数发散前数千步触发警报，远超传统基于损失和梯度范数的检测方法。

Conclusion: 基于模块功能机制的内部监控方法能够实现训练不稳定性的早期检测，为大规模LLM训练提供了高效、可解释且具有故障区分能力的稳定性保障手段。

Abstract: Frontier large language model training consumes massive accelerator fleets and long wall-clock computation, making stability failures costly when they occur.
