After a numerical or a hyperparameter fault has already destabilized the training dynamics, it may continue for thousands of steps while loss and gradient norms still appear normal. We study mechanism-driven detection of training instability by deriving internal monitors from the functional role of each critical module and from the earliest computational sites where failures are expected to produce measurable signatures. For low-precision flash attention, we monitor the spectral entropy of a QK bilinear decomposition, whose first-order term becomes abnormal before the loss fully collapses. For MoE routers, we derive indicators from their role in expert selection. Our fault-injection experiments on low-precision attention, large learning-rate, and combined faults show that these signals provide distinct signatures for different failures, triggering thousands of steps before loss divergence.

</details>


### [129] [From Tokens to States: LLMs as a Special Case of World Models and the Continuous Path Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28127)
*Paul Dubois*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文反驳了LLM与World Model的二元对立，论证LLM是世界模型的退化特例，并提出从Next-Token Prediction到JEPA存在一条连续的频谱，沿此方向推进需解决数据与架构两大开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Yann LeCun等人将LLM与世界模型对立的主流叙事，指出这种二分法过于简化，阻碍了对两者关系的深入理解和技术路径的渐进式探索。

Method: 采用概念分析与论证方法：首先通过形式化定义（状态空间为token序列集合、动作为追加一个token）证明LLM是世界模型的退化特例；其次构建从NTP到JEPA的连续频谱，以多token预测、未来摘要预测、下一隐变量预测为中间节点，展示约束逐步放松的过程；最后对数据获取与架构泛化两大开放问题进行结构化审视。

Result: 核心论点：(1) 世界模型是LLM的严格泛化而非替代；(2) 存在可渐进放松LLM约束的频谱路径，但每放松一个约束就会牺牲LLM的两大可扩展优势——互联网规模的自监督数据和为离散token预测协同设计的Transformer架构。

Conclusion: LLM与世界模型并非对立，后者包含前者。沿频谱向JEPA推进面临"数据悬崖"（从自监督文本到需标注的动作-环境交互数据）和"架构适配"（Transformer能否泛化至连续状态预测）两个关键开放问题，决定该路径的可行性。

Abstract: The AI community has framed the relationship between large language models (LLMs) and world models as a dichotomy: LLMs predict tokens; world models simulate reality. Yann LeCun argues in 2022 that reaching general intelligence requires abandoning autoregressive token prediction in favour of latent-space architectures. This framing is unnecessarily binary. Two claims will be defended. First, LLMs are a degenerate special case of world models: the state space is the set of all token sequences, the only action is appending one token, and world models are therefore a strict generalisation of LLMs, not a replacement. Second, there is a natural continuous spectrum from NTP to JEPA, with multi-token prediction, future-summary prediction, and next-latent prediction as intermediate stations already populated by current research. Moving along this spectrum relaxes the LLM constraints one by one. It also progressively surrenders the two practical advantages that make LLMs trainable at scale: internet-scale self-supervised data, and a transformer architecture co-designed for discrete token prediction. Both are examined as open research questions: the data question (the cliff from self-supervised text to instrumented action-labelled environments) and the architecture question (whether the transformer generalises to continuous-state prediction, or whether a new primitive is needed).

</details>


### [130] [Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28186)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Xinyue Zeng,Zhuochun Li,Hong Jiao,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Epi2Diff 框架将大型推理模型的推理轨迹映射为认知片段序列（episodes），从中提取紧凑的片段动态特征并结合语义表示，用于预测人类题目难度。在四个真实数据集上一致优于强基线，并揭示困难题目会引发更多努力型、迭代式和以实现为中心的认知动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有题目难度预测方法依赖昂贵的人工校准或仅使用题目的表层文本表征，缺乏对题目所引发认知过程的证据。作者认为难度不仅关乎题目文本属性，更是题目所诱发问题解决负担的可观察结果。

Method: 提出 Epi2Diff（Episode to Difficulty）框架：(1) 将大型推理模型（LRM）的推理轨迹映射为功能性的问题解决状态片段（episodes），如理解、规划、实现、验证等；(2) 从片段序列中提取反映推理规模、努力分配和状态转换的紧凑动态特征；(3) 将片段动态特征与题目语义表示融合，用于人类难度预测。

Result: 在四个真实人类难度数据集上，Epi2Diff 一致优于微调小语言模型、LLM 上下文学习、监督 LLM 适配等强基线；在 SAT 衍生分类基准上，相较监督 LLM 微调基线平均相对提升 8.1%。

Conclusion: LRM 推理轨迹中的认知片段为人类题目难度提供了可预测且可解释的过程表征。更难题目并非简单导致更长回复，而是引发更努力、更迭代、更以实现为中心的片段动态。这为教育测量提供了基于推理模型的新视角。

Abstract: Predicting human item difficulty is central to educational assessment, where reliable estimates support fairness and effective test construction. Existing methods often depend on costly human calibration or item-level textual representations, providing limited evidence about the cognitive processes that make items difficult. We argue that difficulty should be viewed not only as a property of item text, but also as an observable consequence of the problem-solving burden an item induces. Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) offer scalable process evidence through reasoning traces, but such evidence must be structured to support interpretable modeling. To this end, we introduce Epi2Diff (Episode to Difficulty), a framework that maps LRM reasoning traces into cognitively grounded episode sequences. These episodes group trace segments into functional problem-solving states, enabling difficulty to be modeled through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions. Epi2Diff extracts compact episode-dynamic features and combines them with semantic item representations for human difficulty prediction. Experiments on four real-world human difficulty datasets show that Epi2Diff consistently outperforms strong baselines, including fine-tuned small language models, LLM in-context learning, and supervised LLM adaptation. On SAT-derived classification benchmarks, Epi2Diff achieves an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised LLM fine-tuning baselines. Further analyses show that harder items induce more effortful, iterative, and implementation-centered episode dynamics, rather than merely longer responses. These results demonstrate that cognitive episodes in LRM reasoning traces provide a predictive and interpretable process representation for human item difficulty, offering a new lens for educational measurement with reasoning models.

</details>


### [131] [Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28273)
*Niclas Lietzow,Danielle Bitterman,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman,Michal Golovanevsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过激活修补和消融实验，揭示了VLM中处理感知-知识冲突的稀疏因果电路：视觉接地默认激活，而先验知识接地依赖网络后半层少量注意力头（2.5-4.8%），这些头分为路由头和写入头，结构在模型家族和尺度间一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在视觉证据与记忆中的世界知识冲突时如何调和二者，决定了多模态系统的可靠性。此前研究仅从行为层面描述该冲突，缺乏组件级因果解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 跨越三种VLM家族，结合三个粒度（残差流、注意力头、MLP子层）的激活修补技术、模型组件消融实验和机制分析，定位并分析因果相关的注意力头。

Result: 视觉接地默认出现，先验知识接地依赖网络后半层2.5-4.8%的因果必要注意力头。消融这些头可在68-96%的先验知识提示下将预测从知识接地翻转为视觉接地，但仅改变0.8-7.5%的视觉接地预测，呈不对称因果结构。这些头分解为调节信息流的路由头和直接投射答案token到残差流的写入头。

Conclusion: VLM中感知-知识冲突由一个稀疏、不对称的因果电路解决，该结构在模型家族和尺度间高度一致，为理解多模态系统的可靠性提供了组件级因果框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Causal Inference for Functional Treatments with Stochastic Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27518)
*Martha Barnard,Jared D. Huling,Julian Wolfson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对函数型处理（如可穿戴设备记录的连续时间体力活动数据）的随机政策因果效应估计方法，通过单一基函数修改处理分布，避免了正定性假设，并具备双重稳健性，应用于NHANES数据研究体力活动对死亡率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备产生的高维函数型数据（如加速度计记录的体力活动曲线）与健康结局（如死亡率）之间的因果推断面临两大挑战：一是定义有科学意义且满足正定性假设的估计目标很困难；二是连续时间域上存在时变混杂的反馈环路。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 作者提出：1）定义针对函数型处理的随机政策（stochastic policies），通过改变处理分布而非固定处理值来估计因果效应，从而规避正定性要求；2）开发一种基于单一基函数修改处理的新方法，由分析者选定基函数来施加处理变化，实现对处理修改的清晰控制，并有效阻断时变混杂反馈；3）证明所提估计量具有渐近正态性和速率双重稳健性。

Result: 理论上证明了估计量的渐近正态性和速率双重稳健性。在NHANES数据的实证应用中，分析了三小时时段内增加体力活动对死亡率的因果效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机政策框架和单一基函数修改方法能够有效估计函数型处理的因果效应，为基于可穿戴设备的体力活动干预政策提供了可靠的因果推断工具。

Abstract: Wearable devices can accurately measure human behavior, providing a unique opportunity to understand how behavior impacts health. Recent studies leveraging functional regression methods have found a strong relationship between accelerometer-collected physical activity and mortality. However, to determine if physical activity patterns impact mortality it is necessary to understand the causal effects of policies for physical activity, i.e., a function-valued treatment. Functional treatments present several challenges for causal effect estimation: 1) defining a scientifically meaningful estimand that reflects real-world policies and satisfies positivity is nontrivial; and 2) the potential for temporal confounding over continuous time. To address these, we propose stochastic policies for functional treatments that allow estimation of causal effects of changing the treatment distribution without requiring a positivity assumption. We develop a novel method for such that modifies the treatment through a single basis function chosen by the analyst, allowing for clear control over treatment modification and temporal confounding feedback. We show asymptotic normality of our estimators and that they exhibit rate double robustness. We apply our methods to the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey to determine the causal effect of increasing physical activity over three-hour periods on mortality.

</details>


### [133] [Structural Change Detection in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27614)
*Wei Zhang,Fang Yao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架，用于检测和定位由常微分方程（ODE）控制的动态系统中的结构变化，能处理非线性、稳定及发散轨迹，结合残差异常与参数对比构建检验统计量，并通过多尺度筛选与FDR控制实现高精度定位和错误发现控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实动态系统常因政策变化、气候强迫等外部干预产生结构变化，现有方法多假设均值或线性趋势变化，无法应对复杂非线性动力学（包括稳定和发散轨迹），亟需一种更通用的结构变化检测与定位方法。

Method: 提出统一框架：(1) 构造融合残差差异与归一化参数对比的新型检验统计量；(2) 采用多尺度“seeded-narrowest-over-threshold”算法结合数据驱动阈值进行候选变化点高效筛选；(3) 引入基于顺序保持样本分裂与对称对比校准的FDR控制流程，以精炼选择并控制错误发现。

Result: 理论上建立了检测一致性、近极小极大定位精度以及弱依赖下有效FDR控制的保证；大量模拟显示在精度和FDR控制上均优于现有方法；在COVID-19动态和全球温度趋势等真实数据上验证了方法的实用性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效处理ODE控制下的非线性动态系统中的结构变化检测与定位问题，兼具理论保证与实证优异表现，具有广泛的现实应用前景。

Abstract: Structural changes often arise in real-world dynamic systems due to external interventions or environmental shifts, such as policy changes in epidemiology or climate forcing in environmental science. In this paper, we propose a unified framework for detecting and localizing structural changes in dynamic systems governed by ordinary differential equations. Unlike existing methods that assume mean or linear trend changes, our approach accommodates complex, nonlinear dynamics with both stable and diverging trajectories. We develop a new test statistic that combines residual-based discrepancy and normalized parameter contrast, capturing evidence for structural changes from both model fit and parameter shifts. Candidate structural changes are efficiently screened using a multiscale seeded-narrowest-over-threshold algorithm with a data-driven thresholding strategy. To refine selections and control false discoveries, we introduce a false discovery rate control procedure that leverages order-preserved sample splitting and symmetric contrast calibration. Theoretical guarantees are established, including detection consistency, near-minimax localization accuracy, and valid FDR control under weak dependence. Extensive simulations demonstrate superior performance over existing methods in both accuracy and FDR control. Applications to real-world data sets, including COVID-19 dynamics and global temperature trends, highlight the practical relevance and broad applicability of our method.

</details>


### [134] [Fast Approximate MM-Estimation for Outlier Robust Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27638)
*Martin Huang,Samuel Muller,Garth Tarr*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出FAMM（快速近似MM估计），通过利用全数据MM估计的权重进行加权最小二乘拟合，大幅降低了分层稳健模型选择中反复拟合稳健估计量的计算成本，同时在模型选择性能和一致性上保持了原有效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层稳健模型选择虽能减少大残差和过代表异常值的影响，但在大量候选模型中反复拟合迭代求解的稳健估计量（如MM估计）时计算量极大。需要一种快速替代方案来降低计算成本。

Method: 提出FAMM（Fast Approximate MM-estimator），其核心是将全数据MM估计得到的权重直接应用于加权最小二乘拟合，从而避免逐模型迭代求解MM估计的高昂计算开销。

Result: 通过大量人工模拟和NBA实际数据应用验证：用FAMM替代MM估计量后，模型选择性能得以保持，同时实现了显著的计算加速；此外，FAMM满足模型选择一致性的必要条件。

Conclusion: FAMM是一种有效的MM估计近似方法，在保证模型选择准确性和理论一致性的同时，大幅提升了分层稳健模型选择的计算效率。

Abstract: Stratified robust model selection reduces the impact of large residuals and overrepresented outliers in bootstrap samples but is computationally intensive when fitting iteratively-solved robust estimators across many candidate models. We propose FAMM, a Fast Approximate MM-estimator, implemented as a weighted least squares fit with weights derived from a full-data MM-estimator, to reduce this computational cost. Using extensive artificial simulations and applications to National Basketball Association data, we show that substituting the MM-estimator with FAMM preserves model selection performance while achieving a substantial computational speedup. Furthermore, we demonstrate that FAMM satisfies the required conditions for model selection consistency.

</details>


### [135] [Modeling Educational Performance Using School Demographics and Teacher Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27654)
*Brianna Reed,Paramahansa Pramanik*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维教育数据中稀疏性、分组预测变量和局部相关协变量问题，本文提出了一种自适应加权组融合LASSO估计量，在统一惩罚回归框架下同时实现自适应变量选择、组正则化和系数融合，并通过ADMM算法求解，具备良好的渐近性质，在模拟和实际数据中均表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维教育数据集普遍存在稀疏性、预测变量自然分组以及局部相关协变量等问题，传统回归方法难以有效处理这些结构特征，因此需要一种能够同时进行变量选择、组结构利用和相邻系数融合的新型惩罚回归方法。

Method: 提出自适应加权组融合LASSO (Adaptive Weighted Group Fused LASSO) 估计量，将自适应变量选择、组正则化和系数融合整合进统一惩罚回归框架；开发了高效的ADMM（交替方向乘子法）算法进行求解；并建立了包括一致性、Oracle性质和去偏渐近正态性在内的渐近理论。

Result: 模拟研究表明，与现有惩罚方法相比，该方法在估计精度和预测性能上均具有显著优势。在阿拉巴马州公立学校数学 proficiency 数据应用中，模型展现出更好的可解释性、更高的预测准确性，并成功识别出最具影响力的制度性预测变量。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应加权组融合LASSO是一种有效的统一框架，能同时处理高维数据中的稀疏性、分组结构和局部相关性，兼具理论保证和实用价值，尤其适用于教育数据等结构化高维数据的建模与分析。

Abstract: High-dimensional educational datasets often exhibit sparsity, grouped predictors, and locally correlated covariates, limiting the effectiveness of conventional regression methods. We propose an Adaptive Weighted Group Fused LASSO estimator that jointly performs adaptive variable selection, group regularization, and coefficient fusion within a unified penalized regression framework. An efficient ADMM algorithm is developed, and asymptotic properties, including consistency, oracle property, and debiased asymptotic normality, are established. Simulation studies demonstrate superior estimation and prediction performance compared with existing penalized methods. An application to Alabama public school mathematics proficiency data illustrates improved model interpretability, predictive accuracy, and identification of the most influential institutional predictors.

</details>


### [136] [Design-Aware Variance Reduction for Switchback Experiments: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27662)
*Sergei Pankratev*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文通过层次化仿真框架，系统评估了CUPED、CUPAC和双重稳健估计等设计感知方差缩减方法在轮换实验中的表现，生成了面向实践者的"制度图谱"，指明各方法的最适用条件及局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮换实验等聚类随机设计在在线平台广泛使用，但其聚类和时序依赖特性使标准方差缩减方法的表现与常规A/B测试不同，缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 构建层次化仿真框架，系统变化聚类数、簇大小不平衡、簇内自相关、遗留效应和预测信号强度等关键参数，以聚类稳健标准误为基线，评估CUPED、CUPAC和DR三种方差缩减方法的效度（假阳性率、置信区间覆盖率）和效率（标准误缩减、检验功效、最小可检测效应），并进行跨簇溢出敏感性分析。

Result: 生成了实践者导向的制度图谱，明确各方法的优势区间；揭示了时间和聚类依赖以及有限簇效应会限制方差缩减收益的具体条件。

Conclusion: 轮换实验的方差缩减方法选择需根据实验制度参数进行设计感知决策，不存在普适最优方法；当存在强簇内自相关或跨簇溢出时，方法收益显著下降。

Abstract: Switchback experiments and other clustered randomized designs are widely used on online platforms, but the clustered, time-dependent nature of these designs can make standard variance reduction methods behave differently than in standard A/B tests. We evaluate design-aware variance reduction methods for switchbacks -- CUPED, CUPAC (ML-based covariate adjustment), and doubly robust (DR) estimators -- relative to a baseline switchback analysis with cluster-robust standard errors. Through a hierarchical simulation framework that varies key regime parameters -- number of clusters, cluster-size imbalance, within-cluster autocorrelation, carryover, and predictive signal strength -- we evaluate validity (false positive rate and confidence interval coverage) and efficiency (standard error reduction, power, and minimum detectable effect as a function of run length). We also include a sensitivity analysis for cross-cluster spillovers to quantify bias and inference degradation under mild interference. The primary outcome is a practitioner-oriented regime map: when CUPED, CUPAC, or DR are most beneficial, and when time and cluster dependence and finite-cluster effects limit improvements.

</details>


### [137] [Robust and Scalable Sure Screening of Fixed effects in Ultrahigh-dimensional Linear Mixed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27789)
*Abhik Ghosh,Magne Thoresen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最小密度幂散度（DPD）的稳健且计算高效的确定筛选过程（DPD-SISP），用于超高维线性混合模型中固定效应协变量的筛选，能在数据污染和模型误设下保持稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高维线性混合模型中，候选固定效应协变量数量随样本量指数增长，而随机效应诱导的组内相关和数据污染给现有变量筛选方法带来重大挑战，缺乏统一、稳健且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 1) 利用基于代理的变换解耦随机效应诱导的依赖性，将问题转化为变换后的回归模型中的边际分析；2) 以最小密度幂散度（DPD）构建边际效用度量，实现稳健性；3) 扩展框架支持条件筛选（融入先验信息）、迭代精炼（缓解相关诱导的掩蔽效应）以及筛选后固定效应的稳健估计。

Result: DPD-SISP在一般条件下（允许非高斯误差、维度非多项式增长）以指数级高概率保留所有相关协变量（确定筛选性质）；影响函数和崩溃点分析证实其强稳健性；模拟显示理想条件下性能有竞争力，数据污染下稳定性显著提升；ADNI2高维纵向数据应用验证其实用价值。

Conclusion: DPD-SISP为超高维线性混合模型提供了一套统一、稳健且可扩展的变量筛选框架，有效应对随机效应依赖、数据污染和超高维等多重挑战。

Abstract: In modern applications of linear mixed models, the number of candidate fixed-effects covariates can grow exponentially with the sample size, while dependence induced by random effects and possible data contamination pose substantial challenges for existing variable screening methods. We propose a robust and computationally efficient sure screening procedure for identifying relevant fixed-effects covariates in ultrahigh-dimensional linear mixed models with known random effects. The proposed method leverages a proxy-based transformation to decouple dependence induced by random effects, enabling screening via marginal analysis in a transformed regression model. Robustness is achieved by constructing marginal utilities based on minimum density power divergence, yielding stability under data contamination and model misspecification without sacrificing scalability. The resulting procedure, termed DPD-SISP, is shown to retain all relevant covariates (sure screening property) with exponentially high probability under general conditions, allowing for non-Gaussian errors and nonpolynomial growth of dimensionality. In addition, DPD-SISP exhibits strong robustness properties supported by influence function and breakdown point analyses. The framework is further extended to incorporate prior information through conditional screening, mitigate correlation-induced masking via iterative refinement, and enable robust post-screening estimation of fixed effects. Extensive simulation studies demonstrate competitive performance of DPD-SISP under ideal settings and substantial gains in stability under data contamination. Its practical utility is illustrated through an application to high-dimensional longitudinal data from the ADNI2 study. The proposed framework thus provides a unified, robust, and scalable approach for variable screening in ultrahigh-dimensional linear mixed models.

</details>


### [138] [Robust estimation of occupation probabilities for coarsened multistate processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27952)
*Niklas Nyboe Maltzahn,Gergely Dániel Lukáts,Kjetil Røysland*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文推导了针对多状态模型占用概率的增强逆概率加权估计量，适用于右删失和基线暴露两种粗化机制，在随机粗化假设下允许时变混杂且不依赖马尔可夫性质，兼具稳健性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多状态模型研究中常面临右删失和基线暴露导致的粗化数据问题，现有方法多依赖马尔可夫假设且难以处理时变混杂，需要一种既有稳健性又高效、且假设更灵活的估计方法。

Method: 利用因果推断和缺失数据文献中的已有技术，推导增强逆概率加权（AIPW）估计量，在随机粗化（CAR）的识别假设下构建估计方程，不要求马尔可夫性质，同时允许时变混杂因子存在。

Result: 所推导的估计量具有理想的双重稳健性和效率特性：理论推导证明了这些性质，模拟研究进一步验证了估计量在有限样本下的优良表现。

Conclusion: 增强逆概率加权估计量在多状态模型的粗化数据场景中提供了稳健且高效的推断方案，放宽了传统方法对马尔可夫假设的依赖，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We derive augmented inverse probability weighted estimators for occupation probabilities of multistate models under two levels of coarsening; right-censoring and baseline exposure. The key exchangeability assumption for identification is coarsening at random, while allowing for time-varying confounders, but not requiring Markov properties. Using existing techniques from causal inference and missing data literature, the derived estimators have highly desirable robustness and efficiency properties. These properties are demonstrated through both theoretical results, and a simulation study.

</details>


### [139] [A beta-binomial model respecting randomization and its comparison to the standard beta-binomial model that ignores randomization for the meta-analysis of rare events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27971)
*Tim Mathes,Maxi Schulz,Oliver Kuss*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种尊重随机化的beta-二项模型（BBM），通过共同beta参数并在估计时以四格表总计数为条件来保留随机化信息。模拟研究表明，在平衡随机化且研究样本量差异不大时，忽略随机化的标准BBM通常无偏，但样本量差异悬殊时可能产生生态学偏倚，因此尊重随机化的BBM应作为一般首选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对罕见事件meta分析，标准beta-二项模型虽有处理零单元格无需连续性校正的优势，但其关键缺陷在于忽略随机化。作者旨在提出一种能够保留随机化信息的改进BBM，并系统评估忽略随机化在何种实际情境下会产生问题。

Method: 核心方法采用共同-beta BBM，通过在模型参数估计时以每项研究四格表的总计数为条件来保留随机化。研究设计了反映真实Cochrane和非Cochrane综述特征的模拟研究，对比尊重随机化与忽略随机化两种BBM在不同异质性水平和样本量差异情境下的表现。

Result: 尊重随机化的BBM在模拟研究中表现良好。忽略随机化在纳入研究样本量差异很大时会出现问题，但在异质性较高时反而表现更优。当随机化均衡且研究间样本量差异不大时，忽略随机化的标准BBM通常不会产生偏倚。

Conclusion: 尽管在均衡随机化和样本量相近时标准BBM表现可接受，但由于忽略随机化可能导致的生态学偏倚是模型的固有缺陷，而尊重随机化的BBM在模拟中表现相当，因此通常应优先选用尊重随机化的beta-二项模型。

Abstract: Background: One of the suggested models for meta-analysis with rare events is the beta-binomial model (BBM). The main advantage of this model compared to inverse-variance models, is that it uses information from zero cells without needing a continuity correction. A disadvantage of the standard BBM is that it ignores randomization. Here we introduce a BBM that respects randomization. Methods: The main idea to preserve randomization is using a common-beta BBM. We illustrate that randomization is preserved by conditioning on the total sum of counts in a studys four-fold table when estimating the model parameters. We perform a simulation study reflecting real-world meta-analyses to compare the models. In addition, we explore in which situations ignoring randomization could be problematic. Results: The BBM that respects randomization performs well in the simulation study that mirrors real meta-analyses in Cochrane and non-Cochrane reviews, respectively. Ignoring randomization appears to be problematic in situations with very different sample sizes of the studies included in the meta-analysis. However, the BBM ignoring randomization tended to perform better when heterogeneity was high. Conclusion: The results show that using the standard BBM, which ignores randomization is usually not biased when the randomization is balanced and the size of studies included in the meta-analysis is not very different. However, as possible ecological bias due to ignoring randomization is an inherent disadvantage of the model and the BBM that respects randomization shows very similar results in the simulation study, it may be generally preferred. Key words Beta-binomial model, generalized linear mixed models, meta-analyses, simulation study, rare events, zero events

</details>


### [140] [Bayesian Simultaneous Credible Bands for Polynomial Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28015)
*Fei Yang,Yang Han,Wei Liu,Ian Hall*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一元多项式回归模型下贝叶斯同时置信带(BSCB)的统一构造框架，开发了基于模拟的临界常数确定方法，证明了BSCB与频率学派同时置信带(FSCB)的渐近等价性，并通过模拟研究和剂量-反应应用验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剂量-反应研究中需要量化整个剂量范围内的疗效不确定性。虽然频率学派同时置信带(FSCB)广泛使用，但无法融入先验知识；贝叶斯同时置信带(BSCB)是自然的替代方案，但文献中缺乏实用的构造方法。

Method: 提出统一框架，在有界协变量区间上为一元多项式回归曲线构造BSCB；开发高效的基于模拟的流程来确定BSCB的临界常数；该框架支持不同先验信息水平下的推断，可通过解析方法或后验抽样实现。

Result: 证明在温和正则条件下，BSCB与FSCB渐近等价，从而对广泛的先验类达到名义频率覆盖；模拟研究证实BSCB在多种场景下均能达到精确的后验同时覆盖概率。

Conclusion: 所提BSCB框架有效融合先验信息且保持频率学派效度，在Phase II临床试验中识别最小有效剂量方面具有重要应用价值，并已开发为R软件包供实用。

Abstract: Quantifying efficacy uncertainty across the entire dose range is crucial in dose-response studies. Although the frequentist simultaneous confidence band (FSCB) is widely used for this purpose, it does not readily incorporate prior knowledge. The Bayesian simultaneous credible band (BSCB) offers a natural alternative, yet practical methods for constructing BSCBs remain scarce in the literature. In this paper, we propose a unified framework for constructing a BSCB for the regression curve in a univariate polynomial model over a finite covariate interval. An efficient simulation-based procedure is developed to determine the critical constant of a BSCB. The framework accommodates inference under different levels of prior information and can be implemented either analytically or via posterior sampling methods. Notably, we prove that under mild regularity conditions, the BSCB is asymptotically equivalent to the FSCB, thereby attaining the nominal frequentist coverage for a broad class of priors. Simulation studies confirm that the BSCB attains the exact posterior simultaneous coverage probability across various scenarios. An application to a dose-response study illustrates its importance in identifying the minimum effective dose in Phase II clinical trials. Software implementation of the proposed methods is available in an accompanying R package.

</details>


### [141] [A joint meta-analysis framework for the accuracy of two diagnostic tests accounting for varying study designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28033)
*Vera Hudak,Nicky J. Welton,Efthymia Derezea,Hayley E. Jones*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯层次模型，通过研究特定的对数优势比对两种二分类诊断试验进行联合荟萃分析，能够处理条件依赖性，且能适应包含无金标准、部分验证等多种研究设计，无需假设参考标准无误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统诊断试验荟萃分析通常假设两试验在给定真实疾病状态下条件独立，这一假设常不成立，违反时会导致联合使用时的准确性估计产生偏倚。现有考虑条件依赖的模型要么要求所有研究均报告联合分类数据，要么存在计算不稳定问题。

Method: 提出贝叶斯层次模型，通过研究特定的对数优势比（log-odds ratios）来建模两试验间的条件依赖性。模型可容纳未报告联合分类数据的研究，并能扩展至无金标准研究和部分验证研究等多样化研究设计，且不要求参考标准无误差。

Result: 当条件依赖性较强时，忽略条件依赖会导致联合准确性估计产生偏倚。所提出的参数化方法保持计算稳定性，能适应多样化研究设计，无需初始数据填补步骤，也不假设所有研究的参考标准无误差。通过两个实例荟萃分析验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该建模框架保留了标准诊断试验准确性荟萃分析方法的关键特征，同时允许条件依赖的存在，为存在实质性条件依赖时的联合准确性估计提供了更可靠且灵活的工具。

Abstract: Meta-analyses of the accuracy of two diagnostic tests typically assume tests are independent conditional on true disease status. This assumption is often unrealistic and violation leads to biased estimates of the accuracy of tests used in combination. Existing models accounting for conditional dependence require `joint classification' data (results for both tests and the `gold standard' on all participants) from all studies and/or suffer from computational instability.
  We propose a Bayesian hierarchical model for joint meta-analysis of the accuracy of two binary tests, modelling conditional dependence through study-specific log-odds ratios. The model accommodates studies that do not report joint classification data. We show how the model extends to accommodate data from varied study designs, including studies without a gold standard and studies with partial verification, without assuming imperfect reference standards are error-free. We demonstrate the framework with two example meta-analyses.
  Our modelling framework retains key features of standard diagnostic test accuracy meta-analysis methods, while allowing for conditional dependence. Ignoring conditional dependence yields biased joint accuracy estimates when conditional dependence is substantial. Our parametrisation maintains computational stability and accommodates data from varied study designs, without requiring an initial data imputation step or assuming error-free reference standards in all studies.

</details>


### [142] [Experimental Design When N Equals One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28200)
*Wenxuan Guo,Tengyuan Liang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个马尔可夫框架来设计N-of-1试验的治疗分配过程，以最小化普通最小二乘估计的误差。作者分析了随机切换和循环切换两类设计，建立了大T渐近理论，证明了i.i.d.伯努利设计的稳健性，并量化了最优设计如何依赖于目标估计量（如累积效应和滞后效应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-of-1试验广泛应用于临床研究和在线平台，但估计多个处理效应时，尚无理论上最优的实验设计方案。现有方法缺乏对治疗分配中时间依赖性的原则性控制手段。

Method: 作者提出一个马尔可夫框架：治疗分配过程由可能时变的转移矩阵控制。在有限阶脉冲响应模型下，以最小化OLS估计误差为目标构建设计优化问题。重点分析两类结构化设计——随机切换设计和循环切换设计，并建立大T渐近理论来刻画最优时间结构。

Result: （1）证明了i.i.d.伯努利设计在N-of-1试验中的稳健性；（2）量化了最优设计如何依赖于目标估计量，包括累积处理效应和滞后特异性处理效应；（3）仿真验证了所提设计在多种场景下的有效性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 所提出的马尔可夫设计框架为N-of-1试验提供了原则性的时间依赖性控制方法。随机切换和循环切换两类设计的渐近最优性质被完整建立，为指导实践中的N-of-1试验设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: N-of-1 trials, or time-series experiments, are widely used in clinical research and online platforms. Yet the theoretically optimal design for estimating many treatment effects remains unclear. We propose a simple Markovian framework for experimental design in which the treatment assignment process is governed by possibly time-varying transition matrices. This formulation encompasses many existing N-of-1 designs and provides a principled way to control temporal dependence in treatment assignment through Markov transition probabilities. Under a finite-order impulse-response model, we formulate the design objective as minimizing the estimation error of ordinary least squares estimators for target treatment effects, and propose practical design optimization procedures. To characterize the optimal temporal structure, we focus on two structured design classes, random-switch and cycle-switch designs, and establish a complete large-$T$ asymptotic theory for the optimal designs in both classes. Our results justify the robustness of i.i.d. Bernoulli designs in N-of-1 trials and quantify how the optimal design depends on the target estimand, including cumulative and lag-specific treatment effects. Simulations demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed designs across multiple scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [OverFlowLight: Real-Time Gridlock Prevention and Traffic Signal Optimization for Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27381)
*Mingyuan Li,Boyang Huang,Tianqi Jiang,Chenpu Li,Chunyu Liu,Yang Li,Ruimin Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OverFlowLight 是一种实时交通信号控制框架，通过多模态传感（摄像头和雷达）实时检测队列溢出，并动态插入专用“溢出相位”来消除阻塞队列。该框架采用混合控制设计，结合快速规则干预与强化学习后端，在三个城市43个路口的实际部署中，将溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制算法以吞吐量优化为主，高峰时段无法有效应对队列溢出问题，导致上游交通阻塞和连锁拥堵，甚至引发安全风险。缺乏一种可实时检测并主动预防溢出的实用框架。

Method: 提出 OverFlowLight 框架，包含三大核心设计：(1) 利用摄像头与雷达多模态传感实现实时溢出检测；(2) 检测到溢出后，动态生成并插入专用溢出相位，清除阻塞队列；(3) 采用混合控制架构，将基于规则的快速溢出干预与强化学习等后端控制器相结合，兼顾即时响应与长期效率优化。

Result: 在三个主要城市43个路口的实际部署中，OverFlowLight 与现有 RL 交通信号控制智能体无缝集成。相比部署基线，溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%，并显著减少了对人工干预（专家调参信号方案）的依赖。

Conclusion: OverFlowLight 是首个实用、可扩展、数据驱动的主动预防交通拥堵瘫痪框架，为构建韧性高效的城市交通系统提供了关键组件。其模块化设计使其能够与现有 RL 信号控制系统无缝集成。

Abstract: Queue overflow, a severe consequence of urban traffic congestion, occurs when vehicle queues exceed intersection capacity, obstructing upstream traffic and triggering cascading gridlocks. Prevailing traffic signal control (TSC) algorithms, primarily optimized for throughput, often fail to address overflow during peak hours, exacerbating congestion and creating safety hazards. We propose OverFlowLight, a real-time framework designed to preemptively resolve overflow and enhance overall TSC performance. It first introduces a mechanism to accurately detect overflow in real-time by leveraging multi-modal sensing from cameras and radars. Upon detection, it dynamically generates and inserts dedicated overflow phases into the signal cycle to clear the blocking queues. This is orchestrated by a hybrid control design that combines rapid rule-based overflow intervention with controller back ends such as reinforcement learning (RL) for longer-horizon efficiency. We conducted extensive real-world deployments of OverFlowLight across 43 intersections in three major cities. The framework demonstrates seamless integration with existing RL-based TSC agents, highlighting its modularity and practical applicability. Empirical results show that OverFlowLight reduces overflow incidents by 60.4% and increases network throughput by 18.2% compared to deployed baselines. Furthermore, it substantially diminishes the need for manual intervention common with expert-tuned signal plans. This work presents the first practical, scalable, and data-driven framework for actively preventing traffic gridlock, offering a crucial component for building resilient and efficient urban transportation systems. Our demonstration videos, codes and datasets are available at the anonymous URL, https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OverFlowLight-FBF9.

</details>


### [144] [RANSAC Scoring Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27385)
*James Pritts,Felix Seegräber,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型RANSAC评分函数，通过共轭逆Gamma先验对内点尺度进行解析边缘化，彻底消除了传统方法中对内点尺度阈值的人工设定依赖，实现了从数据稀缺到数据丰富场景的自适应，并在大规模基准测试中显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RANSAC变体（如MSAC、MAGSAC）的评分函数均依赖于用户指定的内点尺度相关阈值参数，而该尺度本身需从被污染的数据中估计，导致算法对阈值标定敏感、在数据稀缺时性能退化。作者旨在从根本上消除这一依赖，使评分公式中不再出现内点尺度参数。

Method: 作者逆转传统推理顺序：不再先估计尺度再评分，而是在固定内点划分下，假设内点残差服从零均值高斯分布、尺度参数服从共轭逆Gamma先验，对尺度进行解析边缘化，得到闭式评分表达式。该表达式统一涵盖无信息Jeffreys先验与有信息经验贝叶斯先验，无需修改算法即可自适应不同数据量。评分计算可通过排序扫描（sort-and-sweep）在O(N log N)时间内完成。

Result: 在涵盖近70,000对图像的双视图估计基准测试（包含工程化与学习特征管线）上，所提方法超越RANSAC、MSAC、GaU、MAGSAC等现有最优方法：在阈值误标定下性能几乎持平而基线方法显著退化；仅需2对验证数据即可达到近最优精度，而基线方法需约100倍以上的数据；在验证数据稀缺时自动收紧先验正则化。

Conclusion: 所提RANSAC评分函数首次实现了内点尺度参数从公式中的真正消失，兼具理论优雅性与计算实用性（O(N log N)），在广泛的实验条件下一致优于现有方法，尤其在不同数据量下的自适应能力表现突出，为鲁棒模型拟合提供了新的基准方案。

Abstract: The most widely used RANSAC variants score candidate models by counting inliers or summing per-point scores that saturate beyond a residual threshold. Every such score requires a user-supplied parameter that is a function of the inlier scale, which must itself be estimated from contaminated data. We remove this dependence by reversing the usual order of inference: rather than estimating the scale and then scoring against it, we marginalize the inlier scale analytically in closed form under a conjugate Inverse-Gamma prior for a fixed inlier partition, then optimize over partitions. A single closed-form expression spans the non-informative Jeffreys limit and informative empirical-Bayes priors, so the same score adapts across data-rich and data-scarce regimes without any change to the algorithm. The proposed RANSAC score is the first in which the inlier scale is genuinely absent from the formula. The score admits O(N log N ) computation via sort-and-sweep. On a benchmark of nearly 70 000 image pairs spanning different two-view estimation problems and both engineered and learned feature pipelines, the proposed score exceeds the state of the art (RANSAC, MSAC, GaU, MAGSAC): it stays nearly flat under threshold miscalibration where baselines degrade, reaches near-optimal accuracy from as few as two validation pairs where baselines need ont he order of 100 times more,. and tightens its prior regularization as validation data grows scarce.

</details>


### [145] [Unified Zero-Shot Time Series Forecasting: A Darts Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27438)
*Zhihao Dai,Dennis Bader,Alain Gysi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Darts 引入统一的 FoundationModel 类集合，将 Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5 等基础模型整合到统一框架中，提供标准化全周期预测接口，实现零样本预测、微调、不确定性估计和回测的无缝集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来基础模型在零样本预测上表现出色，有望从定制模型训练转向通用预训练预测器。但这些基础模型通常以孤立包形式发布，接口碎片化、与传统工具的互操作性差，导致联合评估和完整流程集成困难。

Method: 在 Darts 库中开发统一的 FoundationModel 类集合（涵盖 Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM），提供标准化的全周期预测接口，最小化外部依赖，使基础模型能以即插即用的方式融入现有 Darts 生态。

Result: 用户仅需更改模型名称即可在现有 Darts 流水线中使用基础模型；新流水线可支持零样本或微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，并与数据处理和评估工具链无缝衔接。

Conclusion: Darts 通过统一接口有效解决了基础模型碎片化问题，显著降低了在完整时序分析流水线中集成和评估基础模型的门槛，推动了通用预训练预测器在实际应用中的落地。

Abstract: Since its initial release in 2020, Darts has become a widely used open-source Python library for time series analysis. A series of foundation models have recently claimed accuracy improvements in zero-shot forecasting, promising a paradigm shift from training custom models to harnessing pre-trained general-purpose forecasters. Foundation models, however, are often released as isolated packages with fragmented interfaces and limited interoperability with common tooling, making joint evaluation and integration within complete pipelines difficult. In Darts, we developed a unified $\texttt{FoundationModel}$ class collection (Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, TiRex, PatchTST-FM) that provides standardized, full-cycle forecasting interfaces with minimal external dependencies for integrating foundation models into the ecosystem. Existing Darts pipelines can now use foundation models with only a name change; new pipelines can use them for zero-shot or fine-tuned forecasting, uncertainty estimation, and backtesting, combined with data processing and evaluation tooling, all within a unified framework.

</details>


### [146] [PairSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability from Pair Representations in Protein Co-Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27440)
*Giosue Migliorini,Aristofanis Rontogiannis,Grigori Guitchounts,Nicholas Franklin,Axel Elaldi,Olivia Viessmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairSAE 引入 N 模 SVD 将配对张量压缩为 token 级交互角色，再通过稀疏自编码器学习共享的 token 级特征，从而在序列和对表示上均能解码，解决了标准 SAE 在 pairformer 架构上的维度爆炸和概念混淆问题。在 Boltz-2 的 PLINDER 蛋白-配体复合物上，所学特征可与 UniProt 注释对齐并预测亲和力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构生物学基础模型在预测和设计上表现优异，但内部特征可解释性差；标准稀疏自编码器在 transformer 序列嵌入上有效，却无法适配 pairformer 架构——直接处理对表示会导致特征二次膨胀，且难以捕捉跨序列和对表示的联合概念。

Method: PairSAE：先对 pairwise 张量进行 N 模 SVD，将其概括为 token 级交互角色向量；再训练稀疏自编码器，以共享的 token 级潜特征同时重建序列表示和对表示。

Result: 在 Boltz-2 模型处理 PLINDER 蛋白-配体复合物的激活上，PairSAE 学到的可解释特征与 UniProt 功能注释对齐，并能预测 Boltz-2 的亲和力值。

Conclusion: PairSAE 成功将结构生物学基础模型的潜在空间与可解释的结构概念桥接起来，揭示了模型"知道"什么，同时规避了传统 SAE 在 pairformer 架构上的缺陷。

Abstract: Foundation models for structural biology have achieved remarkable performance in predicting biomolecular structure and show promise for the design of proteins and small molecules. Yet understanding which internal features drive their outputs remains challenging. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs), effective on transformer-style sequence embeddings, do not transfer cleanly to pairformer-like architectures: naively operating on pairwise representations yields a quadratic blow-up of features and obscures concepts distributed jointly across sequence and pair representations.
  We introduce PairSAE, which summarizes pairwise tensors via an N-mode SVD into token-wise interaction roles, then uses a sparse autoencoder to learn a shared set of token-level features that decode into both sequence and pair representations. Evaluated on Boltz-2 activations for PLINDER protein-ligand complexes, PairSAE yields interpretable features that align with UniProt annotations and predict Boltz-2 affinity values. These results indicate that PairSAE links the latent space of foundation models for structural biology to interpretable structural concepts, clarifying what the model "knows" while avoiding pairformer-induced pitfalls that limit conventional SAEs.

</details>


### [147] [Learning in Markovian bandits with non-observable states and constrained decision epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27448)
*Thomas Hira,Victor Boone,Urtzi Ayesta,Ina Maria Verloop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究状态不可观测且决策时段可能受限的马尔可夫老虎机中的遗憾最小化问题，提出自退化马尔可夫老虎机模型，证明无先验知识时遗憾下界为ω(log T)，并设计UCB-NOM算法实现近对数遗憾；若给定偏置函数上界先验知识，则可达到O(log T)遗憾和O(√(T log T))最坏情况界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马尔可夫老虎机通常假设状态可观测，但现实中许多场景状态是隐藏的。作者希望探索在状态不可观测的马尔可夫老虎机中，以"纯策略"（从头到尾不切换动作的最优固定臂策略）为基准的遗憾最小化是否可行，以及状态不可观测性究竟会造成多大困难。

Method: 作者首先引入"自退化马尔可夫老虎机"模型（rested Markovian bandits的推广），保证纯策略渐近最优。然后证明无先验知识时任何少切换算法必然有ω(log T)遗憾下界。接着提出UCB-NOM算法（基于UCB的乐观算法），并证明在给定臂偏置函数上界的先验知识下，其遗憾可达O(log T)，最坏情况界为O(√(T log T))，且所有界均与状态数无关。

Result: (1) 无先验知识下，少切换算法遗憾下界为ω(log T)；(2) UCB-NOM算法实现近对数遗憾；(3) 给定偏置函数界先验时，UCB-NOM达O(log T)遗憾和O(√(T log T))最坏情况界；(4) 所有遗憾界均不依赖马尔可夫链状态数。

Conclusion: 在自退化马尔可夫老虎机中，状态不可观测性仅是一个"轻微的不便"（mild inconvenience）。虽然无先验知识时无法达到经典的对数遗憾，但借助偏置函数的先验知识仍可恢复对数遗憾，且所有界与状态空间大小无关。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of regret minimization in Markovian bandits with \emph{non-observable states} and possibly \emph{constrained} decision epochs. The focus is restricted to a ``pure'' regret benchmark, that compares the performance of the learning algorithm to the best \emph{pure policy} which -- akin to optimal policies of stochastic bandits -- picks the optimal arm from start to finish without ever switching. We introduce a generalization of rested Markovian bandits, \emph{self-degrading Markovian bandits}, for which pure policies are always asymptotically optimal.We show that without prior knowledge on the underlying bandit, the regret of algorithms that switch arms rarely necessarily scales super-logarithmically for every bandit, i.e., as $ω(\log(T))$, where $T$ is the learning horizon. Despite the unreachability of the logarithmic regime, we design UCB-NOM, an optimistic algorithm inspired by UCB, of which the regret is nearly logarithmic. Lastly, we show that given prior knowledge on the Markovian bandit in the form of a bound on the bias functions of its arm, a proper instantiation of UCB-NOM achieves $O(\log(T))$ regret. We further show that this prior knowledge allows for a $O(\sqrt{T \log(T)})$ worst-case regret bound for UCB-NOM. Notably, our regret bounds do not depend on the number of states of the underlying Markov chains. Our findings suggest that the non-observability of states is a mild inconvenience in self-degrading Markovian bandits.

</details>


### [148] [Benchmarking on Tasks That Matter: Dataset Selection for Preserving Model Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27997)
*Rostislav Gusev,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统框架，用于从大规模机器学习基准中选取代表性数据子集，并评估不同选择策略在保持模型全局排名方面的鲁棒性。结合Bootstrap聚合提供置信区间，对比了聚类、实验设计准则、随机基线和贪心最远优先（FAFI）等策略，并在时间序列分类、NLP和推荐系统三个领域进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习基准测试通常包含大量数据集，全量评估成本高昂。现有子集选择多依赖启发式方法,缺乏对所选子集能否保持模型排名的系统性分析与鲁棒性评估。需要一个有理论支撑的框架来比较不同选择策略在排名保持上的表现。

Method: 提出包含三个核心部分的框架：(1) 将数据集子集选择形式化为排名保持任务；(2) 采用Bootstrap聚合方法为排名相关性指标（如Spearman相关系数）构造有效置信区间，实现策略间的原则性比较；(3) 系统对比四种策略——聚类、实验设计准则（A/D-最优性）、贪心最远优先（FAFI）及随机基线，并对FAFI推导了排名误差关于所选数据集数量的上界。在TSC（112数据集）、MTEB衍生NLP基准（57任务）和推荐系统（30数据集）三个领域验证。

Result: 多个选择策略相较随机子集能改善排名保持效果，其中简单的FAFI表现突出。在TSC领域，最佳策略仅用5个数据集即达到与完整基准排名0.95的Spearman相关性。然而在推荐系统领域，策略相对随机选择的改进较小且多数不具统计显著性。实验还表明，选择方法的有效性依赖于数据集表示的质量和基准测试的规模。

Conclusion: 数据集子集选择可以有效降低评估成本同时保持模型排名，但效果高度依赖领域特性与数据集表示质量。FAFI作为一种简单策略在多个场景下表现优异，Bootstrap聚合为策略比较提供了严格的统计基础。选择策略的收益在数据表示质量高、基准规模大的场景下更为显著。

Abstract: Benchmarks of machine learning models often include many datasets, making evaluation expensive. For efficiency, it is preferable to perform evaluations on small, representative datasets instead. The selection of such subsets typically relies on heuristics and is rarely analyzed for the robustness of the resulting model rankings.
  We introduce a framework to perform the task of selecting datasets subsets with an evaluation of how different selection strategies preserve the global model rankings. Our framework includes bootstrap aggregation, which provides valid confidence intervals, allowing a principled comparison of selection strategies. We consider clustering, design criteria (A/D-optimality), random baselines, and greedy farthest-first (FAFI). For the latter, we derive upper bounds on selection quality in terms of ranking errors as a function of the number of selected datasets.
  Empirically, in time series classification (TSC, 112 datasets) and in a supplementary natural language processing benchmark derived from MTEB (57 tasks), several selection strategies improve rank preservation compared with random subsets, including simple FAFI. In contrast, in recommender systems (30 datasets), the improvement of strategies over random selection is small and typically statistically insignificant. For TSC, our best-performing strategy achieves a Spearman correlation of 0.95 with the full benchmark model rankings using only five selected datasets. Additional experiments indicate that the effectiveness of selection approaches depends on both the quality of dataset representations and the scale of the benchmarking regime.

</details>


### [149] [Prism Transformer: Progressive Head Schedules for Hierarchical Attention Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27449)
*Shubham Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 传统的多头注意力在所有层中均匀分配每个头的隐藏维度，本文指出这种均匀分配构成了结构瓶颈——早期层的头因维度受限无法捕捉复杂的高维上下文模式。为此，作者提出 Prism Transformer，通过跨层单调递增头数，构建从局部到全局的表示层级：早期层用少量宽头捕捉复杂的局部组合模式，深层用大量窄头将其分解为专门的语言特征。该方案参数中立、计算中立，在多个模型规模上一致优于均匀基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 在所有层中将隐藏维度均匀分配给各注意力头（dh = dmodel/h），这迫使早期层和深层使用相同的子空间维度。早期层的头由于维度空间受限，无法忠实捕捉复杂的高维上下文模式，这种均匀分配构成了根本性的结构瓶颈，限制了模型容量的有效利用。

Method: 提出 Prism Transformer，用渐进式头调度（progressive head schedule）替代静态均匀的头配置。具体而言，头数随层深单调递增：早期层使用少量但异常宽的头（大 dh），以捕获复杂的局部组合模式；深层使用大量窄头（小 dh），将这些模式分解为专门的语言特征。该方案保持权重矩阵和 FLOPs 预算与标准 Transformer 完全相同，实现参数中立和计算中立，不引入任何训练或推理开销。

Result: 在三个模型规模（124M、354M、757M）上，Prism Transformer 一致优于均匀基线：验证损失持续降低，在下游零样本基准测试（包括 PIQA、HellaSwag、ARC-Easy 和 WinoGrande）上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 非均匀子空间分配能在标准 Transformer 的预算内释放潜在容量，更有效地利用模型能力。Prism Transformer 通过简单的结构重排（头数渐进调度）实现了零开销的性能提升，验证了重新思考注意力头分配策略的价值。

Abstract: Multi-head attention conventionally partitions the hidden dimension equally across all heads at every layer, enforcing an identical representational subspace dimension (dh = dmodel/h) throughout the models depth. In this work, we identify this uniform allocation as a fundamental structural bottleneck: due to their restricted dimensional space, early-layer heads are unable to faithfully capture complex, high-dimensional contextual patterns. To resolve this, we introduce the Prism Transformer, a novel architectural paradigm that replaces the static, uniform head configuration with a progressive head schedule. By monotonically increasing the head count across layers, the Prism Transformer naturally establishes a local-to-global representational hierarchy: early layers leverage fewer, exceptionally wide heads to capture complex, local compositional patterns, while deep layers deploy many, narrow heads to decompose these patterns into specialized linguistic features. Crucially, this structural shift is parameter-neutral, compute-neutral, and introduces zero training or inference overhead, preserving identical weight matrices and FLOP budgets as the standard Transformer. Across three model scales (124M, 354M, and 757M), the Prism Transformer consistently outperforms uniform baselines, achieving consistent reductions in validation loss alongside consistent gains on downstream zero-shot benchmarks (including PIQA, HellaSwag, ARC-Easy, and WinoGrande). Our findings demonstrate that non-uniform subspace allocation unlocks latent capacity within the standard Transformer budget, enabling more effective use of model capacity.

</details>


### [150] [Dangerous Liaisons of Convex Learning and Non-Affine Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28123)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一阶凸学习中梯度聚合保持单调性的充要条件是聚合规则为正仿射变换。非仿射聚合（如自适应、差分隐私、鲁棒或公平性约束中的聚合）必然破坏单调性，导致收敛不稳定和算法稳定性下降。作者量化了这些损失，并提出了恢复单调性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习管线中广泛采用非仿射梯度聚合（如自适应优化器、隐私保护、鲁棒学习、公平性约束等），但这些实践常出现收敛失败。已有理论依赖更新算子的单调性来保证收敛和泛化，而线性平均能保持单调性，但非仿射聚合是否也能保持单调性一直是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 作者从算子单调性的公理化定义出发，严格证明了梯度聚合保持单调性当且仅当聚合规则为正向仿射变换（即形如 A(g)=αg+β，α>0）。进一步，作者量化了非仿射聚合违反单调性的程度，并推导出由此导致的收敛速度和算法稳定性的退化界。最后给出在某些结构化条件下可恢复单调性的充分条件。

Result: 核心结果为单调性保持的充要条件定理：聚合规则保持单调性⇔该规则是正仿射变换。由此推论，任何非仿射聚合（包括 clipping、sign compression、nonlinear normalization 等）必然破坏单调性，进而导致稳态收敛受阻和稳定性退化。作者进一步量化了这些退化效应，并给出恢复单调性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解现代学习系统中各种失败模式提供了统一的理论框架。非仿射聚合必然以牺牲单调性为代价，这解释了自适应方法、隐私机制、鲁棒聚合等在不同场景下的收敛问题。恢复单调性的充分条件为设计更好的聚合规则指明了方向。

Abstract: Last-iterate convergence and generalization guarantees in first-order convex learning hinge on the monotonicity of the update operator. While linear averaging preserves the monotonicity of gradient updates, this property is often violated when gradients are aggregated non-affinely, as in modern pipelines enforcing constraints like adaptivity, privacy, robustness or fairness. Whether it is possible to design non-affine aggregation rules that maintain monotonicity has remained an open question. We answer this question negatively: we prove that the monotonicity of aggregated gradients is preserved if and only if the aggregation rule is positively affine. Consequently, non-affine aggregation prevents steady convergence and substantially degrade algorithmic stability. We quantify these drawbacks and propose a path forward by identifying sufficient conditions under which monotonicity can be restored. Our results provide a unified theoretical framework explaining the disparate failure modes observed in modern learning systems.

</details>


### [151] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维平坦环面上带不同纵横比的立方散焦非线性薛定谔方程，提出了一种几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO），以纵横比参数 ω² 作为额外输入，在随机相位初值生成的数据上进行训练和评估。结果表明，该算子能捕捉两种几何（有理与无理环面）上不同的 Sobolev 范数增长行为，且显式几何条件化能提升长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维平坦环面上立方 NLS 方程的行为高度依赖于纵横比：有理环面与无理环面的傅里叶共振结构不同，导致高频能量级联行为差异显著。传统数值方法需逐几何重新计算，而现有神经算子缺乏对几何参数的显式建模能力。因此需要一种几何感知的神经算子来高效学习不同几何下的谱转移现象。

Method: 提出几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO），输入为解的实部、虚部以及纵横比参数 ω²，训练目标是逼近一步时间演化算子。训练数据由傅里叶拟谱法从随机相位初值生成，涵盖有理和无理纵横比。通过消融实验系统考察了保留傅里叶模数、激活函数、傅里叶层深度以及显式几何条件化对模型性能的影响。

Result: 所学习的算子能复现两种几何下本质不同的 Sobolev 范数演化行为：有理环面上 H² 范数增长更强，无理环面上则更为受限，与 Hrabski 等人（2021）的结论一致。消融实验表明，纳入 ω² 参数显著提升长期预测精度（尤其对有理几何），验证了几何感知神经算子在捕获非线性色散 PDE 谱转移现象方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 几何条件化的 FNO 能够成功学习并区分不同纵横比下立方散焦 NLS 方程的动力学差异，显式几何条件化对提升有理几何下的长期预测精度尤为重要。该工作支持在非线性色散偏微分方程的谱转移问题中采用几何感知的神经算子架构。

Abstract: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>


### [152] [Disentangling Continuous-Time Latent Dynamics: Identifiability of Latent SDEs via Diffusion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28228)
*Yuanyuan Wang,Wenjie Wang,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间隐变量随机微分方程（SDE）模型的可识别性问题，利用环境驱动的扩散协方差变化，证明在对角扩散且坐标方差比两两不同的条件下，隐变量坐标可识别至排列和缩放，无需对漂移项施加稀疏性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列因果表示学习在离散时间隐变量因果模型中已建立了强有力的可识别性结果，但在连续时间隐变量SDE模型中的可识别性仍基本未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究加性噪声隐变量SDE，观测通过未知非线性微分同胚映射得到，漂移项共享而扩散协方差随环境变化。假设两个对角扩散环境，其坐标方差比两两不同，先对线性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck系统证明可识别性，再推广至一般加性噪声隐变量SDE。提出两阶段估计器用于隐变量解耦和可选的因果图恢复。

Result: 在对角扩散且坐标方差比两两不同的条件下，隐变量坐标可识别至排列和缩放；瞬时漂移-雅可比因果图可识别至相同排列。合成实验验证了可识别性边界，Hardanger大桥监测数据的应用展示了该方法在真实传感器轨迹上的效果。

Conclusion: 通过环境驱动的扩散协方差变化，可在无需漂移稀疏性假设的条件下实现对连续时间隐变量SDE模型的坐标级可识别性，为连续时间隐变量因果表示学习提供了新的理论保证和实用方法。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time series has developed strong identifiability results in discrete-time latent causal models, but identifiability in continuous-time latent stochastic differential equation (SDE) models remains largely open. We address this gap using environment-induced shifts in diffusion covariance. We study additive-noise latent SDEs observed through an unknown nonlinear diffeomorphism, with shared drift but environment-specific diffusion covariance. We show that two diagonal diffusion regimes with pairwise distinct coordinate-wise variance ratios identify the latent coordinates up to permutation and scaling, without any sparsity assumption on the drift. We first prove this result for linear Ornstein--Uhlenbeck systems and then extend it to general additive-noise latent SDEs. Under mild smoothness, the instantaneous drift-Jacobian causal graph is identifiable up to the same permutation. We propose a two-stage estimator for latent disentanglement and optional graph recovery; experiments on synthetic systems confirm the predicted identifiability boundary, and an application to Hardanger Bridge monitoring data illustrates the approach on real sensor trajectories.

</details>


### [153] [The Curse of Multiple Mediators: Hidden Interaction Effects in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27510)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David Arbour,Aaron Mueller,Scott Niekum,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新推导了激活修补（activation patching）的因果估计量，指出自然间接效应（NIE）不仅捕捉目标组件的因果效应，还包含交互效应（INT）——即该组件因果效应本身依赖于模型中其他组件状态的程度。作者在GPT-2 IOI电路中展示了INT导致的失败模式：条件性重要的组件要么被遗漏要么被夸大，并证明INT可用作诊断工具，揭示因果结论何时依赖于prompt，以及何时需要组合搜索才能发现机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补是机制可解释性的核心工具，但其估计量NIE的构成未被充分审视。作者发现NIE混杂了交互效应INT，导致组件因果重要性评估不可靠（如faithfulness scores的不稳定性）。现有补救方案（调整估计器或分析单元）各有可预测的失败模式，因此需要理解INT的本质，并将其从"需要消除的干扰"重新定位为"可解释性研究的诊断信号"。

Method: 1. 从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补的估计量，形式化分解NIE为目标组件的直接效应与INT；2. 理论证明INT随clean与patched激活间距离缩放，在模型局部仿射时可忽略，并可组合分解为成对及高阶组交互；3. 在GPT-2的Indirect Object Identification（IOI）电路上实证展示INT导致的失败模式；4. 分析INT的方差如何解释faithfulness scores的不稳定性。

Result: 1. NIE估计量包含INT，INT衡量组件因果效应依赖于其他组件状态的程度；2. 在GPT-2 IOI电路中，条件性因果重要的组件（依赖其他组件状态）在NIE排名中要么不可见，要么被人工膨胀；3. INT方差解释了先前文献中faithfulness scores的不稳定性；4. 调整估计器（如zero-out patching vs. mean patching）或分析单元（如更粗粒度的模块）均无法消除INT，各有可预测的失败模式。

Conclusion: INT是激活修补中不可避免的，但不应当作需要消除的干扰。其大小和符号（个体及群体层面）可作为诊断信号：当因果结论依赖于prompt时给出警示，当贪婪式NIE组件排名会错失需要组合搜索才能发现的机制时提供线索。这要求机制可解释性研究在NIE之外同时报告和分析INT。

Abstract: Activation patching is the primary tool in mechanistic interpretability. It attributes causal responsibility for a model behavior to each of its individual components by estimating its natural indirect effect (NIE). Re-deriving the activation patching estimand from causal mediation analysis, we find that the NIE does not solely capture the causal effect through the specific component. It also contains interaction effects (INT) that measure how much the component's causal effect itself depends on the state of other components in the model. A natural response may be to try to eliminate INT by adjusting the estimator or unit of analysis, but each of these potential remedies has predictable failure modes. We demonstrate these failure modes in the GPT-2 IOI circuit; components whose causal importance is conditional on the state of other components are either invisible or artificially inflated, and INT variance explains the previously documented instability of faithfulness scores. We prove that INT scales with the distance between clean and patched component activations, is negligible when the model is locally affine, and decomposes combinatorially into pairwise and higher-order group interactions. Despite its inevitability, INT is not a nuisance to be eliminated, but rather a diagnostic for interpretability studies. Its individual and group-level magnitude and sign signal when causal conclusions are prompt-dependent, and when greedy NIE-based component ranking will miss mechanisms only discoverable through combinatorial search.

</details>


### [154] [How Width and Data Shape Generalization Scaling Laws in Quadratic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28242)
*Julius Girardin,Emanuele Troiani,Yizhou Xu,Vittorio Erba,Florent Krzakala,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在特征学习框架下研究了二次两层网络的泛化标度律，揭示了泛化误差随参数量和样本数变化的相图，其中标度指数由目标函数的谱结构决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标度律理论通常局限于固定特征或无限宽度机制下的在线SGD分析，缺乏对特征学习模型中可训练参数量与样本数联合标度行为的理解。

Method: 作者在一个有限样本设定下，分析带有结构化数据的二次两层网络中的ℓ₂正则化经验测试误差最小化问题，通过显式刻画泛化误差作为样本数、模型宽度和正则化系数的函数，推导出不同参数区域的标度行为。

Result: 研究揭示了一个相图，其中泛化误差在不同的参数量区域呈现出不同的标度机制，遵循由目标谱结构控制的数据依赖型幂律；同时刻画了包括插值起始点在内的机制转变及其对泛化的影响。

Conclusion: 泛化标度律并非普遍恒定，而是依赖于模型参数量、样本数和数据谱结构的相互作用；在特征学习模型中，理解这种相图结构对于预测和控制泛化行为至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding how performance scales jointly with model size and data is a central problem in modern machine learning. Existing theoretical works on scaling laws typically describe generalization as a function of data or compute, often in fixed-feature or infinite-width regimes and for online SGD. Here, we instead study how generalization scales with the number of trainable parameters and the number of samples in a feature-learning model. We analyze $\ell_2$-regularized empirical test error minimization in a quadratic two-layer network in a finite-sample setting with structured data. This setting allows for an explicit characterization of the generalization error as a function of the number of samples, model width, and regularization. Our results reveal a phase diagram with distinct scaling regimes as the number of parameters varies. In particular, the generalization error follows data-dependent power laws controlled by the spectral structure of the target. We further characterize the transitions between regimes, including the onset of interpolation, and their impact on generalization.

</details>


### [155] [Boundary condition fidelity for bottom-hole pressure and CO2 plume prediction in geological carbon storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27515)
*Romal Ramadhan,Seyyed A. Hosseini,Larry W. Lake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了碳封存模拟中截断域边界条件对井底压力（BHP）和CO₂羽流预测精度的影响，测试了十种边界处理方案，发现保留角点孔隙体积是最关键要求，渐进修正结合传导率校正能在均质和非均质储层中取得最优一致性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际碳封存模拟常因计算成本而采用截断域，但人工边界会扭曲压力扩散和CO₂饱和度分布，目前缺乏对不同边界条件处理方法在BHP和羽流预测精度方面的系统对比研究。

Method: 作者在均质和非均质储层模型中，将十种截断域边界处理方法与全域参考模拟进行对比，包括均匀孔隙体积乘子、传导率修正、角点校正孔隙体积、分层校正和渐进修正等方法，采用BHP的RMSE、NRMSE、峰值压力偏差和羽流IoU作为评价指标。

Result: 忽略角点存储的均匀处理方法在均质模型中BHP RMSE达362-382 psi，非均质模型中达250-304 psi，羽流IoU仅0.80-0.84；角点校正方案显著降低压力误差并将IoU提升至0.94以上；传导率校正在非均质储层中可能适得其反；渐进修正结合传导率校正表现最优，BHP NRMSE低于3.7%，羽流IoU超过0.97。

Conclusion: 保留角点孔隙体积是截断域建模的最重要条件；传导率校正并非普遍有益，在非均质储层中可能因过度限制变渗透率边界的流动而增大误差；渐进修正结合传导率校正在两类储层中均表现最佳，是实现高保真截断域模拟的推荐方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bottom-hole pressure (BHP) and CO2 plume migration is essential for safe geological carbon storage, yet practical simulations often rely on truncated domains where artificial boundaries distort pressure diffusion and CO2 saturation footprints. In this study, we evaluate how boundary-condition fidelity affects BHP and CO2 plume prediction by comparing ten reduced-domain boundary treatments against full-domain reference simulations in homogeneous and heterogeneous reservoirs. We test uniform pore-volume multipliers, transmissibility modifiers, corner-adjusted pore-volume corrections, layered corrections, and gradual modifiers using BHP RMSE, NRMSE, peak pressure deviation, and plume Intersection over Union (IoU) as performance metrics. Our results show that conserving corner pore volume is the most important requirement for truncated-domain modeling. We find that uniform treatments which neglect corner storage generate large pressure errors, with BHP RMSE of 362 to 382 psi in the homogeneous model and 250 to 304 psi in the heterogeneous model, and yield plume IoU values near 0.80 to 0.84, indicating roughly 16 to 20% of the combined plume area is misrepresented. Corner-adjusted scenarios substantially reduce pressure errors and raise plume IoU above 0.94, but we observe that transmissibility correction is not universally beneficial. In homogeneous reservoirs, uniform transmissibility adjustment improves pressure fidelity; in heterogeneous reservoirs, it can over-restrict flow across variable-permeability boundary faces, increasing BHP error and contracting the predicted plume. We find the gradual modifier with transmissibility correction provides the most consistent performance, achieving BHP NRMSE below 3.7% and plume IoU above 0.97 in both reservoir types.

</details>


### [156] [VGB for Masked Diffusion Model: Efficient Test-time Scaling for Reward Satisfaction and Sample Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28301)
*Kijung Jeon,Thuy-Duong Vuong,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MDM-VGB，一种基于掩码扩散模型的离散扩散采样器，通过理论上有原则的奖励引导重掩码来增强去掩码生成过程。该方法将回溯随机游走从固定前缀树扩展到掩码状态图，在Sudoku和QM9等基准上取得了优异的性能，且理论复杂度为二次方，优于best-of-N的指数复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时缩放是提升生成模型的有效范式，尤其在输出需满足结构约束或优化下游奖励时。现有test-time启发式方法（如best-of-N）存在误差累积导致的指数复杂度问题，需要一种更高效且理论上有保障的奖励引导采样方法。

Method: 将经典Jerrum-Sinclair回溯马尔可夫链从固定前缀树扩展到掩码状态图，允许token在任意位置进行去掩码和重掩码。采样器偏向能产生更高价值部分配置的去掩码与重掩码移动，既能高效生成高奖励样本，又能修复低奖励样本。证明了该方法对过程验证器噪声具有鲁棒性，且达到二次方复杂度。

Result: 理论证明MDM-VGB对过程验证器噪声鲁棒，复杂度为二次方；而best-of-N可因误差累积导致指数复杂度。在Sudoku（约束满足）和QM9（科学发现）等基准上取得了强大的实证表现，验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: MDM-VGB将回溯马尔可夫链与掩码扩散模型成功结合，为推理时缩放提供了一种理论上有保障、实践中高效的解决方案，在约束满足和科学基准上表现优异。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling is a promising paradigm to improve generative models, especially when outputs must satisfy structural constraints or optimize downstream rewards. We consider Masked Diffusion Model (MDM) and introduce MDM-VGB, a discrete diffusion sampler that augments unmasking generation with theoretically principled reward-guided remasking. Inspired by the recent success of the classical Jerrum-Sinclair backtracking Markov chain in reward-tilted generation, MDM-VGB extends the backtracking random walk from a fixed prefix tree to a masked-state graph, allowing tokens to be unmasked and remasked at arbitrary positions. The resulting sampler favors unmasking and remasking moves that lead to higher-value partial configurations, enabling both effective high-reward generation and efficient repair of low-reward samples. We prove that MDM-VGB is robust to process-verifier noise and achieves quadratic complexity, while popular test-time heuristics such as best-of-$N$ can incur exponential complexity due to error accumulation. Our theoretical findings are corroborated by strong empirical performance, particularly on popular constraint-satisfaction and scientific benchmarks such as Sudoku and QM9.

</details>


### [157] [Productionized Fairness Measurement Under Privacy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27558)
*Osonde A. Osoba,Yuzi He,Saikrishna Badrinarayanan,Varun Mithal,Sakshi Jain,Natesh S. Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种隐私保护的概率性种族/族裔估计方法（PPRE），结合安全两方计算、差分隐私和加法同态加密技术，利用BISG估计器和稀疏的自报调查数据，在保护隐私的前提下实现对LinkedIn美国会员的种族/族裔公平性测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性测量通常需要按人口统计特征进行细分评估，但种族和族裔信号在法律上受限且文化上敏感，难以获取和使用。因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能有效进行种族/族裔公平性测量的方法。

Method: PPRE方法整合了三项隐私技术：安全两方计算（2PC）、差分隐私（DP）和加法同态加密（HE），并将其应用于两个种族/族裔人口统计信号源：贝叶斯改进的姓氏地理编码估计器（BISG）以及稀疏的自报人口统计黄金调查集。

Result: 该方法已成功应用于LinkedIn平台上候选人端和查看者端的公平性测量，文中详细阐述了其隐私保障机制并展示了实际应用效果。

Conclusion: PPRE为机构在不直接访问敏感种族/族裔数据的情况下进行公平性评估提供了一种可行的隐私保护方案，并给出了一个可迁移的框架，供其他机构构建类似的隐私保护测量基础设施。

Abstract: Fairness measurements in the form of disaggregated evaluations often rely on demographic signals that are legally constrained or culturally sensitive. Race and ethnicity signals are among the more difficult signals to curate and use for this task. This paper presents Privacy-Preserving Probabilistic Race/Ethnicity Estimation (PPRE) as a method for enabling fairness measurements with respect to race/ethnicity for U.S.\ LinkedIn members in a privacy-preserving manner. PPRE applies privacy technologies (specifically: secure two-party computation, differential privacy, and additive homomorphic encryption) on top of two race/ethnicity demographic signal sources (the Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding estimator and a sparse golden survey set of self-reported demographics) to power a fairness measurement solution with respect to US-based race/ethnicity demographics. We detail its privacy guarantees and demonstrate its application on candidate- and viewer-side fairness measurements. We close with a transferable framework for institutions seeking to implement similar privacy-preserving measurement infrastructure.

</details>


### [158] [Quantum Generative Diffusion Model for Real-World Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27561)
*Jack Waller,Filippo Caruso,Dimitrios Makris,Rajagopal Nilavalan,Xing Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QDiffusion-TS，首个在IQM量子处理器上验证的量子生成扩散模型，用于时间序列合成。该混合架构将去噪Transformer中的前馈组件替换为量子神经网络，参数量降低近三个数量级，同时在金融时间序列上生成质量（Wasserstein距离降低约44%）和下游预测任务（RMSE改善最高71%）上均优于经典对应模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型随着规模增大面临计算成本与效率瓶颈，而量子机器学习有望以紧凑且高表达力的模型表示复杂数据分布。研究者旨在探索量子增强架构能否在显著减少参数量的同时，保持或超越经典模型的生成性能。

Method: 提出QDiffusion-TS框架：将经典扩散架构中降噪Transformer的前馈组件替换为量子神经网络（QNN），形成混合量子Transformer。该模型在IQM量子处理器上实际验证，并在Apple和Amazon金融时间序列数据上进行评估，同时通过下游预测任务检验生成数据的增广效果。

Result: 混合量子模型的可训练参数量比经典对应组件减少近三个数量级。在两个数据集上，生成数据与实际分布的Wasserstein距离均降低约44%。在下游预测任务中，用生成数据增广后，RMSE相比仅用真实数据训练的基线最高改善71%。

Conclusion: 量子增强架构能够以大幅减少的参数量持续匹敌甚至超越经典模型性能，为更高效、可扩展的数据驱动生成建模提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable success in data synthesis, though recent advances driven by increasing model scale have introduced challenges in computational cost and efficiency. Quantum machine learning offers a promising alternative, representing complex data distributions using compact, highly expressive models. Here, we propose QDiffusion-TS, the first quantum generative diffusion model for time series synthesis, and validate it on the IQM quantum processor. The framework extends a classical diffusion architecture by replacing feed-forward components within the denoising transformer with quantum neural networks, yielding a hybrid quantum transformer that reduces the number of trainable parameters in each replaced component by nearly three orders of magnitude. Evaluated on financial time series from Apple and Amazon, the model generates synthetic data that more accurately reproduces the real distributions, reducing Wasserstein distance by approximately 44% relative to its classical counterpart across both datasets. In a downstream forecasting task, augmentation with the generated data improves predictive performance by up to 71% in RMSE over a baseline trained solely on real data. These results show that quantum enhanced architectures can consistently match and frequently surpass classical performance with substantially fewer parameters, establishing a practical framework towards more efficient and scalable data-driven generative modelling.

</details>


### [159] [hia-gat: A Heterogeneous Interaction-Aware Graph Attention Network For Frame-Level Traffic Conflict Risk Prediction On Freeways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27577)
*Mahshid Malazizi,Seyedmehdi Khaleghian,Mina Sartipi,Toru Hirano,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HIA-GAT，一种双流异构图注意力网络，将高速公路逐帧风险评估建模为多智能体场景图二分类问题，通过专用注意力通路分别处理纵向（同车道）和横向（相邻车道）交互，并利用冲突类型感知的门控机制进行融合，在NGSIM数据集上取得最优AUC表现，尤其在有PET阈值的场景下优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高速公路风险评估方法通常缺乏对车辆间关系结构的显式建模，尤其在仅依赖PET（后侵占时间）的横向/换道冲突场景中表现不足。作者认为图结构对建模横向冲突风险至关重要，而纵向风险可通过非关系聚合捕捉，因此需要一个能同时处理两种交互类型并具备可解释性的框架。

Method: 首先将每帧构建为关系感知图，节点为车辆，边分为同车道（纵向）和相邻车道（横向）两类，并附加物理启发的边特征以对齐追尾和换道冲突机制。在此基础上提出HIA-GAT——双流异构图注意力网络：两条独立的注意力通路分别处理纵向和横向交互，通过冲突类型感知的门控机制融合两路信息，门控受SSM冲突归因的事件级监督。构建了包含非图模型和图基线的结构化评测套件，在NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集上使用九种TTC和PET阈值配置进行实验。

Result: HIA-GAT在I-80上取得平均AUC 0.835，在US-101上取得0.867，均为最优。在仅使用PET（换道场景）的设定中增益最大，验证了关系结构对横向冲突建模的关键作用。学习到的门控机制可提供逐车辆的主导冲突类型归因，支持可解释的实时安全监测。纵向风险可通过非关系聚合有效捕捉，而图结构对横向冲突风险建模不可或缺。

Conclusion: HIA-GAT通过双流异构图注意力与冲突类型感知门控融合，有效统一了高速公路纵向与横向冲突风险评估，在精度和可解释性上均优于现有基线。图结构对横向冲突建模至关重要，而纵向风险可被非图方法较好捕获，这为未来实时安全监测系统的设计提供了明确指引。

Abstract: This paper formulates frame-level freeway risk assessment as a multi-agent scene graph-level binary classification problem, where each video or trajectory frame is labeled risky if any TTC- or PET-based conflict violates a specified severity threshold. We construct a relation-aware graph per frame with vehicles as nodes and two interaction types as edges: same-lane (longitudinal) and adjacent-lane (lateral), augmented with physics-informed edge features aligned to rear-end and lane-change conflict mechanisms. Building on a structured benchmarking suite of non-graph models and graph baselines, we propose HIA-GAT, a dual-stream heterogeneous graph attention network that processes longitudinal and lateral interactions through dedicated attention pathways and fuses them via a conflict-type-aware gating mechanism with event-level gate supervision derived from SSM conflict attribution. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 freeway datasets across nine TTC and PET threshold configurations show that HIA-GAT achieves the best average risk-ranking performance (AUC 0.835 on I-80 and 0.867 on US-101), with the largest gains on PET-only (lane-change) settings where relational structure is essential. Beyond accuracy, the learned gate provides interpretable per-vehicle attribution of dominant conflict type, supporting actionable, real-time freeway safety monitoring. We show that graph structure is critical for modeling lateral conflict risk, while longitudinal risk can often be captured by non-relational aggregation.

</details>


### [160] [PEBS: Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage for RLHF Reward-Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27578)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEBS 是一种事后、闭式的逐标注者经验贝叶斯收缩估计器，通过为每个标注者拟合仿射校准器并施加 Morris-James-Stein 收缩，在不重新训练奖励模型的前提下，显著降低了用户内留出 RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 RLHF 奖励模型将所有标注者的偏好汇总拟合单一全局仿射校准器，忽略了标注者间系统性的评分尺度（斜率）和偏移（截距）差异，导致全局平均拟合无法匹配任何个体标注者的真实分布。

Method: PEBS（Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage）在每位标注者的留出评分上分别拟合仿射校准器，然后利用 Morris-James-Stein 经验贝叶斯方法将这些逐标注者参数向总体均值收缩，整个过程为闭式解，无需重新训练奖励模型，仅调整推理时的映射层。

Result: 在 PRISM 数据集上，PEBS 相比总体斜率基线将用户内留出 RMSE 降低 8.58%；在 PluriHarms 危害评分（Qwen-2.5 base, in-family）上复现了 9.66% 的 RMSE 降幅。

Conclusion: PEBS 提供了一种轻量、闭式、事后校正的方案，有效建模标注者个体差异，在不改动奖励基模型的前提下显著提升校准精度，具有较好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Reward models for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pool preferences across thousands of annotators and fit one global affine calibrator, collapsing raters with systematically different rating-scale offsets and slopes into a single average-rater fit that does not match any individual annotator. PEBS is a per-rater empirical-Bayes shrinkage estimator: it fits per-rater affine calibrators on a held-out slice of each annotator's ratings and applies Morris-James-Stein empirical-Bayes shrinkage toward the population mean, in closed form and without retraining the reward model. On PRISM, PEBS reduces within-user held-out RMSE by 8.58% over the pooled population-slope baseline. The procedure replicates on PluriHarms harm ratings (Qwen-2.5 base, in-family) with a +9.66% RMSE reduction over the same population-slope baseline. PEBS is a closed-form post-hoc estimator for annotator-specific affine calibration in RLHF reward modeling; it leaves the reward base model unchanged and estimates only the rater-level map used at inference time for new ratings.

</details>


### [161] [Retroactive Advantage Correction: Closed-Form V-Trace Bias Correction for Delay-Aware RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27580)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生产环境中RLHF延迟奖励问题，提出回顾性优势修正(RAC)方法，通过核函数对延迟奖励进行加权回溯注入，理论上在满足条件下可达到无偏估计，实验显示可将策略偏差降低47.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RLHF常面临异步奖励信号——代码执行验证器、慢速判别器集成、人工审核队列可能在实际rollout之后多个梯度步才返回奖励，这破坏了标准PPO的同步奖励假设。现有方法缺乏对这类延迟奖励的有效处理机制。

Method: 提出回顾性优势修正(RAC)方法：将每个未完成的慢速奖励加入队列，通过非负延迟核函数对其进行"老化"加权，在后续优化步中以带裁剪的残差形式重新注入优势估计。理论证明：在无偏裁剪重要性比条件下，当有效延迟核函数完全重新注入其全部质量时，累积RAC修正精确无偏；否则偏差与未重新注入比例成线性关系；在零延迟恒等核时退化为V-trace。实现上仅需两行代码的奖励管理器补丁即可集成PPO和GRPO。

Result: 在表格MDP概念验证中，双慢速通道配置下RAC将闭式策略偏差降低高达47.9倍，优于wait-for-slow方案且墙钟时间成本更低。

Conclusion: RAC为异步RLHF场景提供了一种理论完备、实现轻量的解决方案，能够有效利用延迟奖励信号来修正优势估计，显著降低策略偏差。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in production does not always have a synchronous reward signal. Code-execution verifiers, slow judge ensembles, and queued human review can return several gradient steps after the rollout that produced them, breaking the synchronous-reward assumption underlying standard PPO. We address this gap with Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC): each pending slow completion is queued, aged through a non-negative kernel, and reinjected as a clipped residual into the next optimiser step's advantage. We prove that under an unbiased clipped importance ratio, the cumulative RAC correction is exactly unbiased when the effective delay kernel reinjects all of its mass, and carries a bias linear in the unreinjected fraction otherwise; at the no-delay identity kernel it reduces to V-trace. On a tabular Markov decision process (MDP) proof-of-concept, RAC reduces the closed-form policy bias by up to 47.9x at the two-slow-channel configuration, beating wait-for-slow at lower wall-clock cost. RAC integrates with PPO and GRPO through a two-line reward-manager patch.

</details>


### [162] [Global Explanations for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Models via $K$-Order Markov Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27599)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KARMA 提出一种基于马尔可夫代理模型的时间序列预测解释方法，通过识别最小充分历史长度 K、估计最优 K 阶马尔可夫转移核，并构建五级全局解释层次，在真实天气数据和合成数据上验证了其恢复因果结构的能力优于 TimeSHAP 等归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 XAI 方法多假设时间戳特征相互独立，忽视了时间序列数据的时间依赖性这一根本属性，导致解释结果违反数据的序列结构与因果结构。

Method: KARMA 围绕三个核心：1) 识别模型预测所需的最小历史长度 K；2) 在离散化历史空间上估计最佳拟合的 K 阶马尔可夫转移核；3) 从转移核中提取五级全局解释层次（包括稳态分布、转移概率、吸收态等）。

Result: 在真实北京 PM 2.5 天气数据上展示了五级解释层次的实际应用；在已知因果边的合成数据控制实验中，KARMA 成功恢复了模型学到的数据因果结构，并且在时间依赖关系识别上优于 TimeSHAP。

Conclusion: KARMA 通过马尔可夫代理模型有效捕捉时间序列预测器中的时间依赖关系，提供结构化、多层次的全局解释，并在因果结构恢复和时间依赖识别任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: While many explainable AI (XAI) methods have been proposed, most are not designed for time-series forecasting models and often rely on the implicit assumption that timestamp features are independent. This assumption ignores the fundamental property of temporal dependence and can lead to explanations that violate the sequential and causal structure of the data. We introduce \textsc{KARMA}, a method for explaining time-series predictors by constructing a Markov surrogate model that captures the temporal dependencies learned by the predictor. Our approach revolves around three main aspects: identifying the minimal history length $K$ that is predictively sufficient for the model, estimating the best-fitting $K$-order Markov transition kernel from the discretized history space, and a five-level global explanation hierarchy that can be derived from the Markov transition kernel, which we illustrate using real-world weather data (Beijing PM 2.5). We also certify using complex synthetic data with known true causal edges that KARMA (i) recovers the data causal structure as learned by the model via a controlled experiment and (ii) identifies temporal dependencies better than established attribution methods such as TimeSHAP.

</details>


### [163] [Training Observable Control Policies to Expose Agent State Through Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27609)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,John J. Bird*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在通信受限条件下，如何利用智能体的动作作为隐式信息源来估计其状态，并通过强化学习训练使策略本身具备更强的可观测性，从而在不显著影响任务性能的前提下改善状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体常因物理或操作约束而面临通信限制，这使得多智能体协调与监控变得困难。但在缺乏显式通信时，智能体执行的动作仍可被观察，这些动作蕴含了其内部状态的信息。因此，探索如何利用动作来推断智能体状态具有重要的实际意义。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，通过在训练奖励中引入策略可观测性（policy observability）的激励项，引导智能体学习出易于被外部观察者通过其动作序列来估计状态的行为策略。以飞机跟踪问题为仿真场景进行验证。

Result: 在飞机跟踪任务中，成功训练出具有增强可观测性的策略，该策略在名义任务性能上仅产生极小的影响，表明可观测性与任务性能之间可以实现较好的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习可以设计出在无显式通信条件下仍具备良好可观测性的智能体策略，动作为状态估计提供了有效的信息渠道，且对任务性能的牺牲很小，验证了该方法的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Physical or operational constraints often impose communications limitations on autonomous agents. Such limitations complicate monitoring or multiagent coordination. Even when strong communications are absent, some information may still be available. The remainder of the relevant agent state may be reconstructed via estimation. The actions taken by an agent are a potential source of information -- as the agent interacts with the environment, these actions may be observed even in the absence of explicit communication. We investigate using actions to estimate the state of an agent, using reinforcement learning to develop policies which make the estimation problem more tractable. Policy observability is encouraged through the training reward and is analyzed using simulation of the trained agent. In an aircraft tracking problem a policy with enhanced observability is found that has minimal impact on nominal task performance.

</details>


### [164] [COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27611)
*Chuanhao Li,Xiaoan Xu,Dirk Bergemann,Ethan X. Fang,Yehua Wei,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COOPA 是一个模块化的 LLM-agent 架构，通过迭代置信度建模、元素级溯源解释和多求解器路由三大组件，实现了可解释、可扩展的运筹学决策支持，在多个基准和骨干模型上取得最优准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的运筹学建模自动化系统存在三个关键局限：(1) 复杂问题上准确率低；(2) 输出不透明、缺乏可解释性；(3) 求解器支持范围狭窄。亟需一种兼具高精度、可解释性和广泛求解器兼容性的 OR 决策支持系统。

Method: 提出 COOPA 模块化架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 迭代置信度建模——生成多个候选建模方案，在多个建模维度上自我评估，采用 max-min 置信度准则择优；(2) 元素级溯源与置信度解释——将变量、参数、约束和目标函数链接到引用原文，提供可审计的决策轨迹；(3) 多求解器路由——根据问题类别将任务分派给专门的优化器 agent。

Result: 在 3 个 OR 基准、8 个 LLM 骨干模型、4 个基线方法的统一条件下，COOPA 在 6/8 的骨干模型上取得最优宏观平均准确率，相较最强基线最高提升 6.7 个百分点。消融实验验证了迭代置信度建模的独立贡献，附加分析和案例研究证明了溯源可追溯性和多求解器调度的价值。

Conclusion: COOPA 通过协同组合迭代置信度建模、可解释溯源和多求解器路由，有效克服了现有 LLM-OR 系统的核心瓶颈，为构建可解释、可扩展的运筹学智能决策支持提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) provides a rigorous framework for high-stakes decision-making, but effective OR modeling requires substantial domain knowledge, mathematical abstraction, and solver expertise.
