Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet remain limited by low accuracy on complex problems, opaque outputs, and narrow solver support. We propose COOPA (COoperative OPerations Agent), a modular LLM-agent architecture for interpretable and scalable OR decision support. It combines three components: iterative confidence-based modeling, which generates multiple candidate formulations, self-evaluates them across modeling dimensions, and selects one using a max-min confidence criterion; element-level provenance and confidence explanations, which link variables, parameters, constraints, and objectives to quoted source text and provide an audit trail for human verification; and multi-solver routing to specialized optimizer agents for different OR problem classes. Across three OR benchmarks, eight LLM backbones, and four baselines under identical conditions, COOPA achieves the best macro-average accuracy on six of eight backbones and improves over the strongest baseline by up to 6.7 percentage points. A within-system ablation isolates the contribution of iterative confidence-based modeling, while additional analyses and case studies illustrate the value of source traceability and multi-solver dispatch.

</details>


### [165] [FoggyTrust: Robust Federated Learning with Hierarchical Trust Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27622)
*Emmanuel Rassou,Tomas Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOGGYTRUST 是对 FLTrust 的层次化扩展，将信任计算下放到雾节点，通过两层架构同时解决全局异构数据下的信任估计分布不匹配和客户端漂移问题，在异构攻击场景下相较 FLTrust 性能提升超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒的联邦学习方法 FLTrust 依赖单一的服务器端根数据集进行信任评估，在处理全局数据异构（non-IID）场景时面临信任估计分布不匹配和客户端漂移的双重挑战，亟需一种能够适配异构数据分布的鲁棒聚合框架。

Method: 提出 FOGGYTRUST，一种两层层次化架构：(1) 将信任计算本地化至雾节点，利用本地同质客户端群组内更准确的信任评分进行鲁棒聚合；(2) 在全局层面结合异构感知优化器（如 FedAdam、SCAFFOLD）来缓解跨组客户端漂移，实现分层信任聚合与全局优化器的协同。

Result: 在基准数据集上，FOGGYTRUST 在异构设置下表现最优，尤其在 CIFAR-10 数据集上面对 Krum 和 Trim 攻击时，相比 FLTrust 性能提升超过 50%；在真实野生动物监测的 safari 数据集上也验证了层次化信任网络在安全关键场景中的潜力。

Conclusion: FOGGYTRUST 验证了层次化信任架构在拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习中的有效性，通过本地化信任计算与全局异构感知优化的结合，显著提升了异构数据分布下的鲁棒性，为分布式安全关键应用（如野生动物监测）提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust federated learning seeks to protect distributed model training from malicious or corrupted clients without requiring access to their private data. FLTrust addresses this challenge by introducing a trusted server-side root dataset that assigns trust scores to client updates for more robust aggregation. In this work, we propose FOGGYTRUST, a hierarchical extension of FLTrust that localizes trust computation to fog nodes, allowing the framework to better handle globally heterogeneous data while preserving robustness within locally homogeneous client groups. We further show that this two-level architecture can simultaneously address distribution mismatch in trust estimation and client drift across groups by combining local trust-based aggregation with heterogeneity-aware global optimizers such as FedAdam and SCAFFOLD. Across benchmark datasets, FOGGYTRUST achieves its strongest gains on more challenging heterogeneous settings, particularly on CIFAR-10 under Krum and Trim attacks, where it achieves an over 50% improvement over FLTrust. We also test FOGGYTRUST in a real-world safari dataset to show the promise of hierarchical trust networks for robust federated learning in socially impactful, safety-critical settings such as distributed wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [166] [HybridCodec: Modeling Discrete and Continuous Representations for Efficient Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27627)
*Artem Ploujnikov,Francesco Verdini,Samir Sadok,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合离散-连续音频表示方法，结合时间压缩的离散token和降维连续残差，构建了混合编解码器和混合Transformer架构。该方法在离散域进行自回归推理，同时进行非自回归预测和连续残差上采样，在保留说话人特征方面显著优于纯离散方法，并减少了自回归步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散音频表示在离散化过程中存在信息损失，导致下游任务性能下降。亟需一种既能利用离散token便于LLM集成、又能保留精细音频信息的方法。

Method: 提出混合离散-连续聚焦调制编解码器（hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec）和混合Transformer。核心设计：(1) 将音频压缩为时间压缩的离散token；(2) 同时提取降维的连续残差以补偿离散化损失；(3) 离散域自回归推理与非自回归连续残差预测、上采样相结合。

Result: 在说话人特征保留方面，所提方法相比纯离散方法有显著提升，同时有效减少了所需的自回归推理步数。

Conclusion: 混合离散-连续表示是一种有效的折中方案，兼顾了离散表示的LLM兼容性与连续表示的保真度，为构建高质量多模态文本-音频系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Discrete audio representations have become increasingly popular for building multimodal text-audio systems and integrating audio capabilities into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, numerous studies report performance degradation on various downstream tasks due to information loss during discretization. To address this, we propose a novel approach combining temporally compressed discrete tokens with dimensionality-reduced continuous residuals. Our framework consists of a hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec and a hybrid Transformer. This architecture performs autoregressive inference in the discrete domain, coupled with non-autoregressive prediction and continuous residual upsampling. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the retention of speaker characteristics compared to discrete-only methods, while simultaneously reducing the number of required autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [167] [Continual Learning for Sequential Personalization of Small Language Models: A Stability Monitoring Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27634)
*Thomas S. Paula,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘设备上进行序列化 LoRA 个性化时的稳定性监测方法。通过在每个适配阶段保存模型检查点，并在当前任务、历史任务和固定参考集上进行评估，利用轻量级参考集分布诊断揭示模型特异性不稳定模式，从而发现隐藏的有害适配现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLMs 在边缘设备上的个性化部署需要持续适配不断变化的用户或任务特定数据，这引入了灾难性遗忘的风险。现有基准（如 TRACE）已表明，持续微调会显著降低对齐大语言模型的通用能力。然而，针对 SLMs 序列化 LoRA 个性化过程中的稳定性监测研究尚不充分，任务级指标可能掩盖有害适配。

Method: 采用基于检查点级别（checkpoint-level）的评估协议：在每次 LoRA 适配阶段后保存模型检查点，随后分别在当前任务、先前见过的任务以及一个固定的参考集上进行评估。通过轻量级的参考集分布诊断（distributional diagnostics）来追踪任务性能、遗忘程度以及参考集漂移随时间的变化，从而揭示模型特异性的不稳定模式。

Result: 研究表明，轻量级参考集分布诊断能够有效揭示 SLMs 在序列化 LoRA 个性化过程中的模型特异性不稳定模式，包括任务级指标无法察觉的有害适配情形。该方法可以监测任务性能、遗忘程度和参考集漂移的时序变化。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了 SLMs 在持续学习场景下进行 LoRA 个性化时的潜在不稳定性，并展示了通过分布诊断进行监测的可行性，为 SLMs 稳定性监测开辟了新的研究方向。研究者希望这项工作能推动该领域的进一步探索。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly being considered for deployment on edge devices such as laptops, enabling private, low-latency, and locally personalized applications. However, personalization requires models to adapt over time to evolving user- or task-specific data, placing them in a continual learning setting. This creates the risk of catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously learned tasks or broader model capabilities. Recent benchmarks such as TRACE have shown that continual fine-tuning can significantly degrade the general abilities of aligned large language models. In this work, we present a study for sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs. We save model checkpoints after each adaptation stage and evaluate them on current tasks, previously seen tasks, and a fixed reference set. This checkpoint-level protocol enables us to monitor task performance, forgetting, and reference set drift over time. We show that lightweight reference set distributional diagnostics can reveal model-specific instability patterns during sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs, including cases where task-level metrics alone hide harmful adaptation. We hope this can highlight new research avenues for monitoring stability of SLMs in a continual learning setting.

</details>


### [168] [TeRoR: Decoupled Temporal Rotation with Relational Circular Region for Temporal Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27651)
*Peijia Xie,Yike Liu,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeRoR 是一种新的时序知识图谱嵌入方法，通过将实体嵌入的时序演化分解为头尾实体在复向量空间中的独立旋转变换，并引入半径约束来捕获关系的多种映射属性，解决了 TeRo 模型在此方面的不足，在四个 TKG 数据集上取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TKG 嵌入方法 TeRo 虽然简单高效，但存在两大缺陷：(1) 对关系的一对多、多对一、多对多等映射属性建模能力不足；(2) 时序信息表达能力有限。需提出新方法解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 TeRoR 方法：(1) 将实体嵌入的时序演化进行分解，在复向量空间中对头实体和尾实体分别进行独立的旋转变换，增强时序信息建模能力；(2) 训练一个半径参数，将经过旋转和平移后的头实体约束在以尾实体为中心的圆形区域内，从而有效捕获关系的多种映射属性。

Result: 在四个不同的 TKG 数据集上，TeRoR 取得了与当前最优模型相比具有竞争力的实验结果。

Conclusion: TeRoR 通过独立旋转和半径约束机制，有效弥补了 TeRo 在关系映射属性建模和时序表达方面的不足，是一种有效的时序知识图谱嵌入方法。

Abstract: In recent years, with the emergence of Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs), research on learning entity and relation representations in TKGs has attracted increasing attention, giving rise to a large number of TKG embedding methods. TeRo is a simple and efficient temporal knowledge graph embedding approach. However, TeRo does not do well in modeling the mapping properties of various relations, such as one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many. Meanwhile, it also has limitations in the expression of temporal information. To address these issues, we propose a novel TKG embedding method named TeRoR. This method divides the temporal evolution of entity embeddings, and conducts independent rotation transformations on head and tail entities in the complex vector space to strengthen temporal information modeling capacity. In terms of relational characteristics, we train a radius to constrain the rotated and translated head entities within a circular region centered on the tail entity, which effectively captures the diverse mapping properties of relations. Experimental results demonstrate that TeRoR achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models on four distinct TKG datasets.

</details>


### [169] [Are Time-Series Foundation Models Ready for E-Nose Data? An Empirical Assessment of Their Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27672)
*Taeyeong Choi,Mohammed Kamruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在电子鼻气体传感数据上的有效性，发现微调是达到满意性能的必要条件，且将TSFM嵌入与专用模型表示相融合可进一步提升气体识别与浓度预测的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域展现出强大的跨任务、跨领域泛化能力，但其在气体传感数据（尤其是电子鼻数据）上的实用性尚未被探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 选取代表性TSFMs（Chronos-2 和 MOMENT），在电子鼻数据集上系统评估其嵌入表示对气体识别和浓度预测任务的有效性；对比直接使用、微调以及将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型学习到的表示进行融合三种策略的性能。

Result: 直接使用TSFMs效果不佳；微调是实现满意性能的必要步骤；将TSFM嵌入与专用模型表示相融合可进一步超越单独微调的性能。

Conclusion: 当前TSFMs在气体传感应用中既有潜力也存在局限性：微调不可或缺，且嵌入融合策略能有效提升性能，但距离即插即用的通用泛化仍有差距。

Abstract: Inspired by advances in natural language processing and computer vision, "time-series foundation models" (TSFMs) have recently been introduced with the promise of strong generalization across diverse time-series tasks, including forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection, as well as across domains such as healthcare, climate science, and manufacturing. However, their utility for gas-sensing data remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper systematically evaluates recent TSFMs on electronic nose (E-Nose) data. In particular, we investigate whether embeddings produced by representative TSFMs, including Chronos-2 and MOMENT, provide effective representations for gas identification and concentration prediction. Specifically, we show that fine-tuning is necessary to achieve satisfactory performance on E-Nose data, and fusing TSFM embeddings with representations learned by specialized predictive models can further improve the performance, suggesting both the potential and limitations of current TSFMs for gas-sensing applications.

</details>


### [170] [Textual Belief States for World Models: Identifiable Representation Learning Under Strict Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27681)
*Xiang Gao,Kaiwen Dong,Yuguang Yao,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Kamalika Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM架构中"历史绕过"导致潜在状态不可识别的问题，提出文本潜在状态与因子化GRPO（fGRPO）方法，通过严格的潜在状态中介训练，在保持预测精度的同时显著提升表示质量和 rollout 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分观测环境中，基于LLM的世界模型存在"历史绕过"（history bypass）问题：预测性能虽好，但潜在表示质量差且不可识别。经典解法是严格潜在状态中介（strict latent state mediation），但在文本域中难以实施，因为文本潜在状态是离散、不可微的，且LLM解码器极易绕过瓶颈。本文旨在解决文本域中严格中介的实现难题。

Method: 首先，形式化论证严格中介的必要性：严格中介使表示质量可被经验检验，而历史泄露架构破坏了这一联系。然后，引入了**文本潜在状态**（textual latent states）——离散、可解释、可变长度的表示；并提出**因子化GRPO（fGRPO）**——一种树结构强化学习方法，在训练过程中强制执行严格中介，使预测仅依赖于潜在状态和动作。

Result: 在TextWorld和ScienceWorld基准上，fGRPO在保持单步预测准确率的同时，表示质量提升高达57%，rollout性能提升高达98%，且这些增益随任务复杂度和时间跨度增加而进一步增大。

Conclusion: 严格中介原则在文本域中同样可行且有效。通过fGRPO训练的文本潜在状态既保持了预测能力，又具备了可识别性和高质量，为构建可解释、可控的文本世界模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: World models in partially observed environments rely on latent representations that summarize interaction history, but in many modern LLM-based architectures predictive performance fails to reflect representation quality due to history bypass, rendering the latent state unidentifiable. Strict latent state mediation, requiring predictions to depend only on the latent state and action, is a classical principle that resolves this, but enforcing it in text-based settings is an open challenge: textual latent states are discrete and non-differentiable, precluding variational training, and expressive LLM decoders readily ignore the bottleneck. We show how to make strict mediation work in the text domain. We formalize why it is necessary, showing that strict mediation makes representation quality empirically testable while history-leaky architectures break this connection. We then introduce textual latent states, which are discrete, interpretable, and variable-length, and factorized GRPO (fGRPO), a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that enforces strict mediation during training. Experiments on TextWorld and ScienceWorld show preserved one-step prediction accuracy alongside up to 57\% gains in representation quality and 98\% improvements in rollout performance, increasing with task complexity and horizon.

</details>


### [171] [CBD: API-Only LLM Black-Box Unlearning through Controlled Behavioral Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27683)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Yijing Lin,Zhipeng Gao,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CBD是一种仅需API黑盒访问的机器遗忘框架，通过两个辅助模型在保留输入和遗忘目标输入之间构建受控行为分歧，并利用基于梯度统计的判别基来提升高相似性场景下的遗忘精度，在遗忘效果与保留效用之间取得了优于11种白盒/灰盒基线的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备通过API调用LLM，产生的数据可能引入敏感、版权、有害或过时信息。现有遗忘方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 仅能通过API黑盒访问，无法获取模型参数和内部logits；(2) 当遗忘目标数据与保留数据共享高度相似的提示结构或语义模式时，难以在遗忘的同时保持模型效用。

Method: 提出CBD（Controlled Behavioral Divergence）框架：使用两个辅助模型在保留输入和遗忘目标输入之间产生受控行为分歧，将其转化为遗忘相关性分数，并将遗忘相关提示路由离开目标LLM。为提升高相似性场景下的判别精度，通过估计经验Fisher矩阵并求解正则化广义特征值问题，构建基于梯度统计的判别基，将遗忘信号引导至目标特定信息而非共享提示结构。

Result: 在ToFU forget10数据集上，CBD在遗忘集上接近重训练参考模型，同时模型效用提升至74.90，比第二佳基线高出约15%。在WMDP数据集上，将危险知识准确率降至25.68（接近随机猜测），同时保留MMLU准确率52.67。

Conclusion: CBD作为一种API-only黑盒遗忘方法，有效解决了黑盒访问限制和高相似数据下的遗忘-效用权衡问题，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，为边缘智能场景中的LLM遗忘提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge devices increasingly invoke large language models (LLMs) through API services for context aware edge intelligence, while edge generated data may be collected to improve LLMs and may introduce sensitive, copyrighted, harmful, or outdated information into model behavior. Machine unlearning offers a practical way to remove the influence of undesired data without retraining LLMs. However, existing methods still face two gaps. The first is API only black box access, where target model parameters and internal logits are unavailable. The second is how to preserve retained utility when unlearning target data and retained data share highly similar prompt structures or semantic patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Controlled Behavioral Divergence (CBD), an API only black box unlearning framework. CBD uses two auxiliary models to create controlled behavioral divergence between retained inputs and unlearning target inputs, converts this divergence into an unlearning relevance score, and routes unlearning related prompts away from the target LLM. To improve discrimination accuracy under high similarity between target and retained data, CBD constructs a gradient statistics based discriminative basis by estimating empirical Fisher matrices and solving a regularized generalized eigenvalue problem, guiding the unlearning signal toward target specific information rather than shared prompt structures. Compared with eleven white box and gray box unlearning baselines, CBD achieves a better unlearning utility trade off and its performance varies little across settings. On ToFU forget10, CBD approaches the retrained reference on the forget set while raising model utility to 74.90, about 15% above the second best baseline. On WMDP, it lowers hazardous knowledge accuracy to 25.68, near random guessing, while preserving MMLU accuracy of 52.67. Code is at https://github.com/DGL-codes/CBD.

</details>


### [172] [Deployment-Side Adaptiveness in Multi-Horizon Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27688)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 同一训练好的多输出波动率预测模型，在推理阶段采用不同的滚动规则会产生不同精度与成本的预测结果。研究发现，非默认的部署规则通常优于标准MIMO，但最优规则因模型架构和预测期限而异；基于验证集选择的部署策略能以低成本获得接近集成方法的效果，且策略排序对损失函数敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注金融预测中"训练什么模型"，而忽视了"训练好的模型如何部署"这一同样影响预测性能的关键环节。作者旨在系统研究多期限波动率预测中推理阶段部署规则对预测精度和计算成本的影响。

Method: 以多输入多输出（MIMO）预测器为起点，通过改变推理时的滚动规则（rollout rule），从同一训练模型中诱导出一族预测结果。在20个股票波动率序列、三个预测期限及多种架构（线性模型至PatchTST）上，评估不同固定规则、基于验证集的单例选择策略及规则子集集成策略在MSE和QLIKE损失下的表现。

Result: （1）非默认滚动规则常优于标准MIMO部署，但最优固定规则随架构和期限变化显著，无统一的最优静态替代方案；（2）基于验证集选择的单例规则以低成本实现优于默认MIMO的改进；（3）小型规则子集能以显著降低的推理成本恢复大型集成的大部分收益；（4）MSE下选择的策略无法一致迁移至QLIKE，策略排序具有度量敏感性。

Conclusion: 推理阶段部署是金融预测中一个重要的自适应来源，应被纳入模型评估体系。训练好的波动率预测器不应仅按架构评估，还需考虑其部署策略。这为金融预测实践提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: In financial forecasting, predictive performance depends not only on which model is trained, but also on how the trained model is deployed. We study this issue in multi-horizon volatility forecasting. Our starting point is that a trained multi-output (MIMO) forecaster does not define a single deployable predictor: by changing the inference-time rollout rule, the same trained model induces a family of forecasts with different accuracy and cost profiles. Across 20 stock-volatility series, three forecast horizons, and architectures ranging from linear models to PatchTST, we find that non-default rollout rules often improve over standard MIMO deployment. However, the best fixed rule varies substantially across architectures and horizons, making any single static replacement unreliable. We therefore evaluate validation-based deployment policies over the induced rule family. Under the primary MSE objective, validation-selected singletons provide a low-cost improvement over default MIMO, while small rule subsets recover much of the benefit of larger ensembles at substantially lower inference cost. We also find that policy rankings are metric-sensitive: MSE-selected policies do not transfer uniformly to QLIKE, a finance-standard volatility loss. These results show that inference-time deployment is a meaningful source of adaptiveness in financial forecasting, and that trained volatility forecasters should be evaluated not only by their architecture, but also by their deployment policy.

</details>


### [173] [Halt Fast! Early Stopping for Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27694)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于元学习的随时有效认证鲁棒性框架，通过轻量级元学习器预测图像特定的先验，结合序贯E过程实现自适应计算资源分配，在保持严格统计保证的同时将样本复杂度降低20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑（RS）虽能为神经网络提供严格的鲁棒性保证，但其计算成本极高——每次输入需数万次模型评估，且需预先承诺固定的采样数量。这严重限制了RS在实际部署中的应用，尤其是在实时、安全关键场景中。

Method: 提出一种元学习框架，用于随时有效（anytime-valid）的认证鲁棒性。核心思路是：训练一个轻量级元学习器来预测图像特定的先验，该先验被馈入序贯E过程（sequential E-process）。基于这些先验，框架能够自适应地部署计算资源——在认证过程中动态决定是否需要继续采样，而非事先固定采样数量。

Result: 相比传统随机平滑方法，样本复杂度降低约20倍，同时保持了严格的统计保证。此外，随时有效性使得可根据应用特定的风险阈值自适应分配计算资源（称为"资源分诊"），这在经典认证框架下无法实现。认证性能与现有方法相当。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持严格统计保证的前提下大幅提升了计算效率，为实时、安全关键的认证部署提供了一条可行路径，同时引入的资源分诊能力进一步拓展了随机平滑的实用性。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) provides rigorous robustness guarantees for neural networks without architectural constraints, yet its adoption is limited by extreme computational costs. Standard RS requires tens of thousands of model evaluations per input and forces practitioners to commit to fixed sample sizes a priori. In this work, we present a novel meta-learning framework for anytime-valid certified robustness that adaptively deploys computational resources. By using a lightweight meta-learner to predict image-specific priors for a sequential E-process, we achieve a 20-fold reduction in sample complexity compared to traditional methods while maintaining rigorous statistical guarantees. Beyond raw efficiency, we demonstrate how anytime-validity enables adaptively allocating compute based upon application-specific risk thresholds, a form of resource triage impossible under classic certification frameworks. That this is achievable while also providing similar certification performance demonstrates that our approach provides a pathway for real-time, safety-critical certification deployments.

</details>


### [174] [Class-frequency Guided Noise Schedule for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27696)
*Jiequan Cui,Beier Zhu,Qingshan Xu,Xiaojuan Qi,Bei Yu,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了扩散模型中类别频率与多尺度噪声调度之间的相关性，发现低频类因低密度区域更大而导致得分估计更不准确，且高频类主导得分空间。为此提出类别频率引导（CFRG）噪声调度，为低频类分配更大尺度噪声，在CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT等不平衡数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中，低密度区域会导致得分估计不准确，多尺度噪声调度虽能缓解该问题，但低频类仍面临更大的低密度区域挑战；同时高频类主导得分空间，使得生成样本偏向高频类，导致低频类生成质量差、多样性不足。因此需要一种考虑类别频率的噪声调度策略。

Method: 提出类别频率引导（Class-frequency Guided, CFRG）噪声调度方法，核心思想是根据类别频率为低频类分配更大尺度的噪声，以补偿其更大的低密度区域，从而改善得分估计精度。在CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT等不平衡数据集上，将CFRG应用于图像生成、图像分类和文本到图像生成任务中进行验证。

Result: 采用CFRG噪声调度后，在CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT数据集上的图像生成、图像分类及文本到图像生成等任务中，相比基线方法均取得了显著提升，证明了频率统计在噪声调度设计中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 类别频率与噪声调度之间存在紧密关联，将频率统计信息纳入噪声调度设计可有效提升扩散模型在类别不平衡场景下的生成质量与多样性。CFRG噪声调度为低频类分配更大噪声的策略被证明是有效且通用的。

Abstract: In this paper, we are the first to examine the correlations between class frequency and the multi-scale noise schedule within diffusion models. For score-based generative models, low-density regions often lead to inaccurately estimated scores, thereby compromising the generation quality. Although the multi-scale noise schedule can alleviate this issue during the diffusion process, low-frequency classes still face the challenge of large low-density regions, resulting in more inaccurate estimated scores than high-frequency classes. Furthermore, high-frequency classes tend to dominate the score space, causing a convergence of most data points towards generating samples from these classes. Consequently, samples generated within low-frequency classes exhibit suboptimal quality and limited diversity. To address this challenge, we propose the \textit{Class-frequency Guided (CFRG)} noise schedule, leveraging the insight that low-frequency classes should be endowed with larger-scale noises. To illustrate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct experiments on various tasks, including image generation, image classification, and text-to-image generation, using imbalanced datasets, \textit{i.e.}, CIFAR-100-LT, and ImageNet-LT. By employing the CFRG noise schedule, we achieve substantial improvements over baselines, manifesting the crucial role of frequency statistics in noise schedule design.

</details>


### [175] [What Was That Again? Certified Robustness for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27698)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil Marchant,Jiani Xie,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双门诊断管道机制，包含双边原子审计和基于排名的锦标赛选择，用于自动语音识别系统。该方法无需真实转录即可检测对抗性和良性扰动，在四种架构上实现了高达55%的相对词错误率降低，同时提供词语级和句子级认证以增强声学安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别系统对对抗性和良性扰动都非常敏感，但现有方法依赖参考数据集验证。在真实部署环境中，由于缺少真实转录的标注（oracle knowledge），检测此类行为极为困难。因此需要一种无需真实标签即可诊断和缓解ASR系统错误的方法。

Method: 提出双门诊断管道（dual-gate diagnostic pipeline）：(1) 双边原子审计（Two-Sided Atomic Audit）：通过累积统计财富（statistical wealth）来认证令牌的存在性（token existence）和对抗性排除（adversarial exclusion）；(2) 基于排名的锦标赛（Rank-Based Tournament）：用于选择最优序列输出。整体方法借鉴了认证机制（certification-inspired）的思想。

Result: 在四种不同架构上评估，实现了高达55%的相对词错误率（WER）降低；同时提升了召回率（recall），降低了置信度与WER之间的Spearman相关性，并提供了词语级和句子级的细粒度认证。

Conclusion: 该认证启发的双门诊断管道能够在无需真实转录的情况下有效检测并缓解ASR系统中的扰动影响，显著降低WER并提升声学安全性，为实际部署场景中的ASR鲁棒性提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition systems are notoriously both sensitive to adversarial and benign perturbations. While this has been repeatedly demonstrated using reference datasets, detecting such behaviors in deployed systems is incredibly challenging, due to the absence of oracle knowledge of the true transcription. We demonstrate that employing a certification-inspired mechanism can significantly decrease WER, increase recall, and decrease the Spearman correlation between confidence and WER. We achieve this through a dual-gate diagnostic pipeline: a Two-Sided Atomic Audit that accumulates statistical wealth to certify both token existence and adversarial exclusion, and a Rank-Based Tournament that selects the winning sequence. Our evaluations across four diverse architectures demonstrate up to a 55% relative reduction in Word Error Rate, while also providing granular word- and sentence-level certifications to enhance acoustic security.

</details>


### [176] [The Simulacrum: Decision-Theoretic Pretraining for Near-Optimal Time-Series Forecasting and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27711)
*Pablo Montero-Manso,Marcel Scharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的"决策论预训练"框架，通过在分层模拟数据上训练，直接逼近最优决策规则，从而实现时间序列的零样本估计（预测、参数估计、预测区间、模型选择），在有限样本下可控制风险、偏差、极小化极大性能与校准性，并在真实基准上达到竞争性或最优预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列估计方法（如MLE、AICc模型选择）在有限样本下常面临偏差、校准不良等分析难题，且许多复杂结构方程或最优性准则缺乏解析解或计算代价过高。分析师需要一种能直接针对其特定决策目标、在有限样本下实现近乎最优性能的通用估计框架。

Method: 提出"决策论预训练"框架：分析师指定一个生成世界（数据生成过程的分布）和目标决策目标；在分层模拟数据上训练神经网络，直接近似该生成世界下的最优决策规则；训练后的网络可作为零样本估计器，处理未见时间序列的预测、参数估计、预测区间或模型选择。框架支持对过程层面的有限样本性质（风险、偏差、极小化极大、均匀校准）进行显式控制。

Result: 实验表明，对于相同结构模型类，神经估计器在有限样本下优于MLE和AICc等传统基线；即便仅在结构模型模拟上训练，在真实世界基准数据集上的预测精度也达到竞争性或最先进水平，可与统计模型、神经网络或大型预训练模型媲美。在AR(p)模型的有限样本偏差/校准问题以及预测组合谜题两个长期挑战上验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式控制决策论权衡，为分析师提供了针对特定分析需求定制的高效估计工具，尤其擅长逼近那些解析不可解或计算不可行的复杂时间序列问题的最优解。

Abstract: We introduce a neural network-based framework for learning time series estimators through a process we term decision-theoretic pretraining. Analysts specify a generative world, a distribution over data-generating processes, and a target decision objective. A neural network trained on stratified simulations from this world approximates the corresponding optimal decision rule, yielding a neural estimator that provides forecasts, parameter estimates, predictive intervals, or model-selection for zero-shot inference on previously unseen time series.
  The joint specification of the generative world and objective enables the estimators to directly approximate process-level, finite-sample properties: near-optimal risk, bias control, minimax performance, and uniform calibration. Our experiments demonstrate that these neural estimators can outperform traditional baselines such as maximum likelihood estimation and model selection via AICc, for the same model structural model classes. Furthermore, even when trained purely on simulations of structural models, they achieve competitive or state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy on major real-world benchmarks, compared with statistical, neural or large pre-trained models.
  We illustrate the framework by addressing two longstanding challenges: finite-sample bias and miscalibration in AR(p) models, and the forecast combination puzzle. These applications highlight the approach's main advantage: its ability to approximate solutions to analytically intractable or computationally prohibitive time series problems, including complex structural equations or optimality criteria. Ultimately, by enabling explicit control over decision-theoretic trade-offs, the framework equips analysts with highly efficient estimation tools tailored to their specific analytical needs.

</details>


### [177] [Aurora: A Leverage-Aware Spectral Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27715)
*Alec Dewulf,Dhruv Pai,Li Yang,Ashley Zhang,Ben Keigwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aurora优化器通过强制矩阵参数更新的行均匀性，同时保持Muon的极因子几何结构，解决了Muon在高矩阵参数中行范数不均匀导致的神经元失效问题，在预训练实验中超越Muon并达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在处理高矩阵参数（如MLP投影矩阵）时，更新量的行范数可能出现任意程度的不均匀，导致自我强化的反馈循环：某些神经元持续获得微小更新，最终丧失对网络输出的有效贡献。现有行归一化方法会扭曲Muon更新的极因子几何结构，因此需要一个既能强制行均匀性又不破坏该几何结构的解决方案。

Method: 提出Aurora优化器，在Muon优化器基础上引入行均匀性约束，同时严格保持动量矩阵的极因子（polar factor）几何结构，避免现有行归一化方法对更新几何的破坏。

Result: Aurora在预训练实验中优于Muon；结合现有方法后，在modded-nanoGPT speedrun的优化器赛道上达到谱优化器中的最先进性能；Aurora相对Muon的经验增益随MLP扩展因子扩大而增加，表明其可能支持极宽MLP层的有效训练。

Conclusion: Aurora成功解决了Muon在高矩阵参数中的行范数不均匀问题，保持了极因子几何结构的优良特性，为训练具有极宽MLP层的网络提供了新的优化器选择。

Abstract: We show that for tall matrix parameters, like projection matrices in the MLP layers, the Muon update can have row norms that are arbitrarily non-uniform. This can lead to a self-reinforcing feedback loop whereby neurons receive persistently small updates and eventually do not contribute meaningfully to network outputs. This problem is effectively mitigated by an additional row normalization step, but current methods do this in a way that moves the Muon update geometry away from the polar factor of the momentum matrix, which we find is undesirable. We propose Aurora, an optimizer that enforces row-uniformity of matrix parameter updates while respecting Muon's polar factor geometry. Aurora outperforms Muon in our pre-training experiments and, when combined with existing methods, achieves state-of-the-art performance among spectral optimizers on the optimizer track of the modded-nanoGPT speedrun. Additionally, we find that Aurora's empirical gains over Muon scale with the MLP expansion factor, suggesting that Aurora may allow for effective training of very wide MLP layers.

</details>


### [178] [Learning to Reason with Curriculum II: Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27721)
*Nived Rajaraman,Audrey Huang,Miroslav Dudik,Robert Schapire,Dylan Foster,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合泛化的理论基础，通过学习模拟半自动机（序列计算预测）这一典型问题，揭示了基于自课程（autocurriculum）的递归分解方法在统计复杂度上远超直接方法。具体表现为：在监督微调场景下，课程学习只需次多项式量级的监督token即可完成学习；在强化学习可验证奖励场景下，课程方法将参考模型的覆盖要求从全序列长度指数级地降低到更短的块长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合泛化（将复杂问题分解为简单子问题并组合求解）是自然与人工智能的核心能力，也是链式推理（chain-of-thought）的关键机制，但其理论基础仍然薄弱——何时以及为何分解问题比直接求解更高效？本文旨在从统计学习角度为这一问题提供理论解答。

Method: 以学习模拟半自动机（semiautomata）为典型问题框架（涵盖状态跟踪、正则语言识别、模运算），采用基于该系列第一部分的自课程方法：递归地将长序列分解为较短的子问题，学习解决子问题，再将解组合起来。分析覆盖两种设定：(i) 监督微调（SFT）——学习者在中间计算状态获得交互式反馈；(ii) 强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）——学习者利用结果验证器来改进预训练参考模型。

Result: (i) SFT场景：课程方法仅需 2^{Õ(√log T)} 个监督token即可完成学习，这是序列长度T的次多项式量级，突破了直接模拟所需的 Ω(T) token下界。(ii) RLVR场景：课程方法将参考模型所需的覆盖条件从全序列长度T指数级地放宽到更短的块长度 B ≪ T。

Conclusion: 递归分解与组合的策略在统计效率上具有显著优势：课程学习能够以远少于直接方法的监督信号实现组合泛化，且能大幅降低对预训练参考模型的质量要求。这为理解链式推理和组合泛化的理论基础提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Compositional generalization, the ability to solve complex problems by combining solutions to simpler sub-problems, is a fundamental capability of both natural and artificial intelligence, and a key mechanism underlying chain-of-thought reasoning. However, the theoretical underpinnings of compositional generalization remain poorly understood: when and why does decomposing a problem into parts yield more efficient learning than solving it directly? We study this question through the canonical problem of learning to simulate semiautomata (predicting the outcome of $T$ steps of sequential computation), a model that captures state tracking, regular language recognition, and modular arithmetic. We show that an autocurriculum-based approach building on Part I of this series, recursively decomposing longer sequences into shorter sub-problems, learning to solve them, and composing the solutions, achieves dramatically better statistical complexity than direct methods. (i) For a setting inspired by supervised fine-tuning (SFT) where the learner receives interactive feedback on intermediate states of the computation, curriculum facilitates learning from only $2^{\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log T})}$ tokens of supervision; i.e., subpolynomial in the sequence length $T$, overcoming the $Ω(T)$ token barrier required by direct simulation. (ii) For a setting inspired by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where the learner improves a pre-trained reference model using an outcome verifier, we show that curriculum reduces the requirement on the reference model from coverage at the full sequence length $T$ to coverage at a shorter block length $B \ll T$, an exponentially weaker condition.

</details>


### [179] [Reduction of Probabilistic Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27737)
*Mauricio Montes,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将因子图缩减技术迁移到化学实现的方法，可在保留信念传播不动点性质的前提下，显著缩小 Napp–Adams 编译方法生成的化学反映网络规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生化系统中实现概率计算是细胞级编程的长期目标。化学反映网络（CRN）虽已被证明能实现隐马尔可夫模型和因子图等概率模型，并复现贝叶斯推断与信念传播的动力学，但现有编译方法（如 Napp–Adams 编译）产生的 CRN 规模过大，且经典 CRN 缩减技术无法直接应用。

Method: 首先恢复 Napp–Adams 编译 CRN 中编码的因子图结构，然后将近期在因子图缩减方面的理论结果迁移到对应的化学实现中，通过消去冗余变量来缩减 CRN 规模。

Result: 在保持存活变量上信念传播不动点性质不变的前提下，获得了规模显著减小的化学反映网络。

Conclusion: 通过连接因子图缩减与 CRN 编译两个领域，可以克服 CRN 规模过大的障碍，为在细胞级高效实现概率推理算法提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Programming adaptive behaviors at the cellular level is a long-standing goal that raises the question of how probabilistic computation can be implemented in biochemical systems. Chemical reaction networks (CRNs) provide such a substrate and have been shown to realize probabilistic models, including hidden Markov models and factor graphs, with dynamics reproducing Bayesian inference and belief propagation. However, encoding these algorithms typically requires prohibitively large reaction networks, and classical CRN reduction techniques do not directly apply. By recovering the factor graph structure encoded in Napp--Adams-compiled CRNs, we transport recent factor-graph reduction results to their chemical implementations, obtaining significantly smaller CRNs while preserving the belief-propagation fixed points on surviving variables.

</details>


### [180] [The Weakest Link Tells It All: Outcome-Supervised Process Reward Modeling via Learnable Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27739)
*Tianyu Jia,Yue Fang,Hongxin Ding,Rihong Qiu,Zhibang Yang,Zhijing Wu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LCA框架，将结果监督PRM建模为多示例学习问题，通过可学习的信用分配（Weakest Link Assignment原则）和Softmax-Weighted-Sum池化联合优化信用分配与奖励建模，解决步骤级反馈中的信用归属难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果监督PRM采用均匀分配或因果分配策略，未能将信用锚定在步骤正确性上，导致过程错误识别能力不足。需要一种能自动学习步骤级信用分配的方法。

Method: 1) 将结果监督PRM形式化为多示例学习（MIL）问题；2) 提出Weakest Link Assignment原则（推理链强度取决于最弱步骤）；3) 设计Softmax-Weighted-Sum（SWS）池化，适配推理状态间的强依赖与冗余性；4) 联合学习信用分配与奖励建模；5) 证明算法在温和假设下的贝叶斯一致性。

Result: 在多个任务和多种backbone上，LCA一致优于现有最先进的结果监督PRM方法。

Conclusion: LCA通过可学习信用分配有效解决了结果监督PRM中的核心挑战，为无需昂贵步骤标注的高质量过程奖励建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) enhance the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by providing fine-grained feedback, yet training PRMs typically requires expensive stepwise annotations. Outcome-supervised PRMs offer a scalable alternative by learning from final-answer correctness alone, but this introduces a fundamental *credit assignment* challenge, i.e., attributing outcomes to responsible reasoning steps. Existing approaches rely on either uniform or causal assignment, both of which fail to anchor credit in step correctness and thus hinder process error identification.
  In this work, we propose Outcome-Supervised Process Reward Modeling via **L**earnable **C**redit **A**ssignment (**LCA**), an outcome-supervised PRM framework that jointly learns credit assignment and reward modeling under the principle of *Weakest Link Assignment: a reasoning chain is as strong as its weakest link*. To address mutual dependence between credit assignment and reward modeling, we formalize outcome-supervised PRM as a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) problem and introduce Softmax-Weighted-Sum (SWS) pooling, an MIL pooling technique tailored for strong dependence and redundancy among reasoning states. We prove Bayes consistency of our algorithm under mild assumptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **LCA** consistently outperforms state-of-the-art outcome-supervised PRMs across multiple tasks and backbones. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LCA.

</details>


### [181] [Flexformer: Flexible Linear Transformer with Learnable Attention Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27748)
*Haoran Zhang,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flexformer 提出一种完全数据驱动的线性 Transformer，将随机傅里叶特征中的频谱频率作为可训练参数来学习注意力核，包含平稳与非平稳两种变体，在长序列任务上高效且性能有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 的注意力机制复杂度为二次方，难以扩展到长序列。现有基于核的线性注意力虽降低复杂度，但多依赖固定或弱可学习的核函数，限制了模型的表达能力与性能。

Method: 基于随机傅里叶特征线性注意力框架，将频谱频率参数化为可训练变量，实现完全数据驱动的核学习；提出平稳（stationary）与非平稳（nonstationary）两种变体，后者具有更强的表达能力；并探索从预训练 Transformer 蒸馏以恢复 softmax 注意力，以及跨领域核迁移。

Result: 在语言建模和序列分类任务上一致优于基线方法；可有效从预训练 Transformer 蒸馏恢复 softmax 注意力；注意力核表现出较强的跨领域迁移能力，在长序列任务上兼具高效率与竞争力性能。

Conclusion: Flexformer 通过可学习的频谱频率实现了灵活且表达力强的线性注意力核，在保持线性复杂度的同时，显著缩小了与 softmax 注意力的性能差距，为长序列建模提供了高效实用的方案。

Abstract: Transformer models rely on attention mechanism to capture long-range dependencies but suffer from quadratic complexity, limiting their scalability to long sequences. Kernel-based linear attention reduces this complexity but typically relies on fixed or weakly learnable kernels, restricting expressiveness and performance. In this work, we propose Flexformer, a flexible linear Transformer that learns attention kernels in a fully data-driven manner. Flexformer builds on random Fourier feature-based linear attention and treats spectral frequencies as trainable parameters, enabling the model to learn a broad family of attention kernels.
  We develop both stationary and nonstationary variants, with the latter offering strictly greater expressiveness.
  Extensive experiments on language modeling and sequence classification demonstrate that Flexformer consistently outperforms baselines. Moreover, Flexformer can be effectively distilled from pretrained Transformers to recover softmax attention and exhibits strong kernel transferability across domains, achieving both high efficiency and competitive performance on long-sequence tasks.

</details>


### [182] [PerturbCellRL: Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27752)
*Dongxia Wu,Mingyu Li,Yuhui Zhang,Anurendra Kumar,Emma Lundberg,Serena Yeung-Levy,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerturbCellRL 提出用强化学习对预训练的单细胞转录组生成模型进行后训练，通过四个细胞级别的验证器（Pearson top-k、RMSE top-k、DE Spearman、Pathway activity）作为奖励信号，使生成的单个细胞在生物学上更加一致，在保持群体水平指标竞争力的同时提升细胞级别的生物学保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞扰动生成模型虽然在群体水平预测上表现良好，但从未对个体生成细胞进行生物学一致性检查，导致生成的单个细胞可能不符合已知的扰动生物学规律，限制了模型在可信赖的单细胞预测中的应用。

Method: 提出 PerturbCellRL 框架：以预训练的流匹配（flow-matching）单细胞转录组生成器为基础，设计四个细胞级验证器（Pearson top-k 相似度、RMSE top-k 邻近度、差异表达 Spearman 相关性、通路活性一致性）作为强化学习的奖励信号，通过 RL 后训练对生成器进行对齐优化，使生成细胞在通路响应等维度上匹配已知的扰动生物学。

Result: 在多个遗传和化学扰动基准上，PerturbCellRL 在奖励对齐的评估指标和一个留出的评估指标上均优于预训练的流匹配生成器，同时在群体水平指标上与当前最优方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作将可信赖的单细胞预测重新定义为验证器引导的生成对齐问题，从单纯匹配表达分布转向对单细胞扰动效应进行显式的生物学一致性检验，为单细胞扰动建模提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation models can reduce costly wet-lab screening by predicting how cells respond transcriptionally to interventions. While recent generative models improve population-level prediction, individual generated cells are not explicitly checked for biological consistency. We introduce PerturbCellRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that post-trains a pretrained single-cell transcriptomic generator using a suite of cell-level verifiers as rewards. These verifiers define four rewards: Pearson top-k similarity, RMSE top-k proximity, DE Spearman, and Pathway activity. The Pathway activity verifier rewards cells whose pathway responses match known perturbation biology. We evaluate PerturbCellRL on multiple genetic and chemical perturbation benchmarks. Across these benchmarks, PerturbCellRL improves over the pretrained flow-matching generator on reward-aligned evaluation metrics and a held-out evaluation metric. Moreover, PerturbCellRL remains competitive with state-of-the-art methods on population-level metrics. Together, these results frame trustworthy single-cell prediction as verifier-guided generative alignment, moving beyond matching expression distributions toward predictions whose single-cell perturbation effects are explicitly checked for biological consistency.

</details>


### [183] [Layerwise Progressive Freezing: A Training Scaffold for Depth-Scalable Binary Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27759)
*Evan Gibson Smith,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 StoMPP（随机掩码部分渐进二值化），一种无 STE 的 BNN 训练方法，通过从输入到输出逐层渐进地将裁剪后的权重和激活替换为硬二值，配合随机部分掩码和软刷新，有效缓解深度网络中的精度退化。核心发现是二值化顺序至关重要：前向逐层渐进可防止深度坍塌，反向则失败，根源在于激活二值化引起的梯度阻滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 BNN 训练依赖直通估计器（STE），其前向/反向不匹配随网络加深导致严重精度退化。作者希望探索一个正交方向：训练过程中何时、何处强制执行二值化，寻求完全无 STE 的训练方案。

Method: 提出 StoMPP：1) 从输入层到输出层逐层渐进地将裁剪权重/激活替换为硬二值；2) 使用随机部分掩码（仅部分元素二值化，其余保持实值）；3) 软刷新机制；4) 最终冻结所有二值化层后，可选择性对冻结部分施加 STE 作为组合方案。

Result: 纯 StoMPP（无 STE）：ResNet-50 BNN 在 CIFAR-10/100/ImageNet 上分别提升 +18.0/+13.5/+3.8；与 STE 组合后进一步提升至 +27.1/+19.8/+17.7。前向逐层渐进有效，反向渐进坍塌至接近随机水平；纯二值权重网络（无二值激活）对顺序不敏感。

Conclusion: 二值化顺序是决定性因素：前向逐层渐进可避免深度坍塌。根源在于已二值化的激活会切断上游梯度流，形成"梯度阻滞"，顺序控制这些阻滞的形成时机。所有消融实验在无 STE 条件下进行，确保结论反映的是渐进机制本身而非与 STE 的交互。

Abstract: Training binary neural networks (BNNs) from scratch is dominated by the straight-through estimator (STE), whose forward/backward mismatch produces severe accuracy degradation as networks deepen. We study an orthogonal axis: when and where binarization is enforced during training. We introduce StoMPP (Stochastic Masked Partial Progressive Binarization), which gradually replaces clipped weights and activations with their hard binary counterparts layer by layer from input to output, using stochastic partial masks with soft refresh. StoMPP delivers two complementary benefits. As a standalone training rule, it provides a fully STE-free procedure that improves over vanilla STE with gains that grow with depth (ResNet-50 BNN: +18.0/+13.5/+3.8 on CIFAR-10/100/ImageNet), and the pattern holds across ResNet-18/34/50, MobileNetV2, and BERT fine-tuning. Composed with surrogate gradients by applying STE only to frozen entries, it reaches +27.1/+19.8/+17.7 over vanilla STE on the same setting. Underlying both regimes is a single mechanistic finding: progression order is decisive. Forward layerwise progression prevents depth collapse, reverse progression collapses to near-chance, and binary-weight networks (without binary activations) are insensitive to order. We trace this asymmetry to activation-induced gradient blockades: a committed binary activation severs gradient flow upstream, and ordering controls when these blockades form. To isolate the progression's contribution from any benefit conferred by STE, we conduct all ablations in the STE-free regime; the resulting characterization (schedule, refresh, ordering, dynamics) thus reflects the progression itself rather than its interaction with surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [184] [RS-Diffuser: Risk-Sensitive Diffusion Planning with Distributional Value Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27766)
*Shiqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RS-Diffuser 提出了一种风险敏感的离线扩散规划框架，将扩散轨迹生成与分布值批评相结合，通过推理时调整风险参数，单一模型即可灵活实现风险厌恶、中性或寻求行为，在风险敏感基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散规划器通常为风险中性，忽视了真实部署中罕见但灾难性的安全后果。离线强化学习在安全关键应用中需兼顾性能与最坏情况鲁棒性，因此需要能够感知尾部风险的决策框架。

Method: RS-Diffuser 包含三个核心组件：(1) 对未来状态轨迹建模的扩散规划器；(2) 独立的逆动力学模型用于动作解码；(3) 基于分位数回归的蒙特卡洛分布批评家，估计候选计划的完整回报分布。在采样阶段，将风险敏感引导信号（如条件风险价值 CVaR 的梯度）注入去噪过程，实现尾部感知的轨迹生成。

Result: 在风险敏感 D4RL 和危险机器人导航基准上的实验表明，RS-Diffuser 在整体回报、最坏情况鲁棒性上均达到最优，同时显著减少了安全违规。

Conclusion: RS-Diffuser 通过推理时风险参数调节，在不重新训练的情况下灵活切换风险偏好，有效平衡了性能与安全性，为安全关键的离线 RL 应用提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables policy learning from fixed datasets without additional environment interaction, making it appealing for safety-critical applications where online exploration is costly or unsafe. Diffusion-based decision-making methods have recently achieved strong performance in offline RL by modeling rich, multimodal trajectory distributions. However, existing diffusion planners are typically risk-neutral and therefore may overlook rare but catastrophic outcomes that are crucial in real-world deployment. In this work, we propose RS-Diffuser, a risk-sensitive offline diffusion planning framework that combines diffusion-based trajectory generation with distributional value critics. RS-Diffuser learns a diffusion planner over future state trajectories, a separate inverse dynamics model for action decoding, and a Monte Carlo distributional critic that estimates the full return distribution of candidate plans through quantile regression. At sampling time, we incorporate a risk-sensitive guidance signal into the denoising process, using gradients computed from tail-aware objectives such as Conditional Value at Risk to steer generation toward desired risk profiles. As a result, a single trained model can flexibly produce risk-averse, risk-neutral, or risk-seeking behaviors by changing only the inference-time risk parameter. Extensive experiments on risk-sensitive D4RL and risky robot navigation benchmarks demonstrate that RS-Diffuser achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving both overall return and worst-case robustness while reducing safety violations.

</details>


### [185] [Difference of Convex Programming in the Wasserstein Space with Applications to MMD Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27767)
*Clément Bonet,Pierre-Cyril Aubin-Frankowski,Youssef Mroueh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将在Wasserstein空间上具有差分凸（DC）分解的目标函数与经典的凹凸过程（CCCP）相结合，提出了一种新的优化框架。针对最大均值差异（MMD）和能量距离（ED）泛函，作者给出了显式的Wasserstein DC分解，并在温和假设下证明了算法的局部收敛性，实验表明该方法比Wasserstein梯度下降更快且更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率测度空间上的泛函优化在机器学习中广泛存在，但许多实际感兴趣的目标泛函在Wasserstein测地线上是非凸的，这使得标准一阶方法的分析极具挑战性。因此需要为这类非凸问题开发新的优化理论和算法。

Method: 将经典凹凸过程（CCCP）从欧氏空间提升到Wasserstein空间：首先识别目标泛函的差分凸（DC）结构，即在Wasserstein空间中将目标表示为两个凸泛函之差；然后在光滑性和强凸性假设下，对凸分量应用CCCP迭代，每次迭代通过线性化凹的部分来构造凸子问题并求解。

Result: 理论层面：证明了算法迭代序列的近似平稳性。针对MMD和ED泛函，建立了显式的Wasserstein DC分解，并在温和假设下证明了局部收敛性。实验层面：合理选择的DC分解在MMD目标上相比Wasserstein梯度下降收敛更快且更稳定。

Conclusion: DC分解框架为Wasserstein空间中的非凸泛函优化提供了一种有效的算法范式。通过将CCCP推广到测度空间，成功处理了MMD和ED等实际重要的非凸目标，理论和实验均验证了该方法的优越性。

Abstract: Optimizing functionals over the space of probability measures is now ubiquitous in machine learning. A widely used approach is to perform the optimization directly over the Wasserstein space, but many objective functionals of practical interest are non-convex along Wasserstein geodesics, making the analysis of standard first-order methods challenging. In this work, we study a class of objectives over the Wasserstein space that admit a difference-of-convex (DC) decomposition and we lift the classical convex-concave procedure (CCCP) to this setting. Under smoothness and strong convexity assumptions on the convex components of the decomposition, we prove almost stationarity along the iterates of the resulting algorithm. Our main focus is on the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and the Energy Distance (ED) functionals, for which we develop explicit Wasserstein DC decompositions, and establish local convergence of the scheme under mild assumptions. Empirically, we show that well-chosen DC decompositions yield faster and more stable convergence than Wasserstein gradient descent on these MMD objectives.

</details>


### [186] [NormGuard: Reward-Preserving Norm Constraints in Flow-Matching Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27771)
*Tianlin Pan,Lianyu Pang,Cheng Da,Huan Yang,Changqian Yu,Kun Gai,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RL 微调流匹配生成器时，速度范数会膨胀 5–15%，导致感知质量退化。推理时重缩放无法修复该问题，因为范数膨胀已内化到权重中。本文提出一种铰链惩罚（仅在速度范数超过参考值时激活），在保持奖励的同时显著提升图像质量和真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练虽能提升流基生成器的奖励对齐，但常以感知质量退化为代价，且这种退化未被奖励代理捕捉。现有推理时重缩放策略（如 CFG 中的范数修正）无法迁移至 RL 场景，需要新的训练时干预方法。

Method: 首先，在 NFT、AWM、DPO 三种后训练方法中测量并确认速度范数膨胀现象（5–15%）。其次，通过伴随灵敏度分析证明速度幅度重缩放不携带一致的一阶奖励信号。最后，提出一种铰链惩罚项（hinge penalty），仅在 ||v_θ|| > ||v_ref|| 时激活，与任意速度局部基损失可加性组合。

Result: 在两个基模型、三种后训练方法、两个奖励代理上，该方法一致提升了 MLLM 判定的图像质量和取证真实感，同时保持奖励水平。增益在少步推理场景下更显著，且不能用早停解释。

Conclusion: 训练时范数正则化是解决 RL 微调中感知质量退化的有效策略，铰链惩罚提供了简单、通用且不损害奖励的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reward alignment of flow-based generators, but often degrades perceptual quality in ways that are not captured by the reward proxy. We identify a simple structural signature of this drift: across three post-training methods (NFT, AWM, DPO), RL fine-tuning inflates the per-step velocity norm $\|v_θ\|$ by $5\%$ to $15\%$ relative to the reference. A form of norm inflation has been studied in classifier-free guidance (CFG), where rescaling the velocity back to a reference norm at inference time can mitigate the resulting artifacts. However, this inference-time correction does not transfer cleanly to RL: rescaling $v_θ$ to match $\|v_{\text{ref}}\|$ at inference time neither improves reward nor fixes the quality degradation, because the inflation is co-adapted into the model weights. Furthermore, an adjoint sensitivity analysis shows that velocity magnitude rescaling carries no coherent first-order reward signal at the batch level, indicating that suppressing norm inflation is unlikely to remove a consistently reward-carrying component. Since inference-time renormalization fails while norm suppression carries no reward cost, training-time intervention is the appropriate strategy. Together, these findings motivate \methodname, a hinge penalty that activates only when $\|v_θ\|$ exceeds $\|v_{\text{ref}}\|$ and composes additively with any velocity-local base loss. Across two base models, three post-training methods, and two reward proxies, \methodname consistently improves MLLM-judged image quality and forensic realism while preserving reward, with gains that amplify under few-step inference and are not explained by early stopping.

</details>


### [187] [Accelerating Hierarchical Sparse Predictive Coding with Hybrid Amortized Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27802)
*Kazuhisa Fujita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了层次稀疏预测编码模型中四种推断方案的性能，发现浅层LISTA式摊销编码器加少量迭代精化步骤，能在保持重建质量和稀疏性的同时，大幅降低推理延迟，优于纯迭代和纯摊销方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次稀疏预测编码结合了预测编码的层级推断能力和稀疏编码的简约表示优势，其实用性受限于迭代潜变量推断的高计算成本——随着层级加深，每个输入需要大量循环精化步骤才能获得有效的稀疏表示。本文旨在在不改变能量函数的前提下，探究不同推断过程对该瓶颈的缓解效果。

Method: 固定层次稀疏能量函数不变，在静态图像基准上对比四种推断方案：(1) 基于ISTA的经典迭代推断；(2) 加速版MFISTA作为参考；(3) 结构感知的摊销推断，采用适配层次模型的LISTA式自底向上编码器；(4) 混合方法——先用快速摊销初始化，再进行少量基于能量的纠正性循环精化步骤。评估指标包括重建质量、稀疏度、延迟和稳定性。

Result: 浅层LISTA式初始化器配合短程纠正性循环精化，在重建质量和稀疏性上优于纯摊销推断，同时比长程迭代推断快得多，实现了质量与速度的有效权衡。

Conclusion: 混合推断策略——摊销初始化与少量能量精化步骤相结合——是层次稀疏预测编码模型中平衡推断速度与表示质量的最优方案，为实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hierarchical predictive coding provides an interpretable framework for perception as error-driven inference in multi-layer generative models, while sparse coding imposes parsimonious latent representations through explicit sparsity constraints. Their combination yields hierarchical sparse predictive coding models with appealing computational and neuroscientific properties, but practical use is often limited by the cost of iterative latent inference. In such models, each input may require many recurrent refinement steps before a useful sparse representation is obtained, and this burden becomes more severe as the hierarchy deepens. We study this bottleneck by holding the hierarchical sparse energy fixed and varying the inference procedure. The comparison includes four schemes: classical iterative inference based on ISTA, an accelerated MFISTA reference, structurally informed amortized inference using a LISTA-style bottom-up encoder adapted to the hierarchical model, and a hybrid method in which this fast amortized initialization is followed by a small number of corrective energy-based refinement steps. Under this shared objective, we measure reconstruction quality, sparsity, latency, and stability on static image benchmarks. The results show that a shallow LISTA-style initializer plus short corrective recurrence improves over pure amortization while remaining much faster than long iterative inference.

</details>


### [188] [Pepti-drift: Toxicity-Repulsive Drifting for Antigen-Conditioned Discrete Peptide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27824)
*Takashi Fujiwara,Hikaru Shindo,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pepti-drift 是一种基于潜在空间精炼的肽生成框架，通过单步抗原条件漂移，在促进抗原特异性结合的同时避开毒性相关区域，并采用预热策略平衡结合与毒性这两个竞争性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗性肽发现面临两大核心挑战：一是设计对抗原具有特异性结合能力的肽，二是避免毒性。由于在肽表示空间中，促进结合的理化特征常与毒性相关特征重叠，同时满足这两个目标极具难度。

Method: 提出 Pepti-drift，一种在肽嵌入空间中运行的毒性感知潜在精炼框架。它通过单步抗原条件漂移生成候选肽：将生成的肽潜在表示拉向抗原匹配的结合肽，同时推离毒性相关区域。引入预热（warm-up）策略来稳定这一竞争性优化过程——先学习结合导向的吸引力，再逐步增强毒性排斥力。

Result: 论文展示了 Pepti-drift 在平衡结合亲和力与毒性规避方面的有效性，预热策略成功稳定了竞争性目标的训练过程。

Conclusion: Pepti-drift 为治疗性肽的从头设计提供了一种有效的毒性感知生成方案，有望推动安全、高特异性治疗肽的发现。

Abstract: Peptides are a promising therapeutic modality that combine the chemical tunability of small molecules with the target specificity of macromolecular therapeutics. However, designing antigen-specific binding peptides while avoiding toxicity remains a major challenge for therapeutic peptide discovery. Here, we present Pepti-drift, a toxicity-aware latent refinement framework that generates peptide candidates through a single antigen-conditioned drift step. In a peptide embedding space, Pepti-drift learns to attract generated peptide latents toward antigen-matched binding peptides while repelling them from toxicity-associated regions. This is challenging because binding-promoting physicochemical features often overlap with toxicity-associated features in peptide representation space. To address this, we introduce a warm-up strategy to stabilize this competing objective by first learning binding-oriented attraction and then increasing toxicity repulsion.

</details>


### [189] [USAD: Uncertainty-aware Statistical Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27832)
*Zhijian Zhou,Xunye Tian,Jiacheng Zhang,Zesheng Ye,Yiyi Guo,Donghao Zhang,Liuhua Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的统计对抗检测方法(USAD)，通过方差差异(VD)和扰动协方差差异(PCD)两个新统计量，捕捉对抗样本的全局和局部不确定性特征，在控制误报率的同时提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最大均值差异(MMD)的统计对抗检测方法，无法有效捕捉对抗样本的两个关键不确定性模式：异常的特征散布（全局不确定性）和扰动下的不稳定性（局部不确定性），限制了检测能力。

Method: 提出USAD方法，包含两个新统计量：(1) 方差差异(VD)，衡量对抗样本与干净样本在特征散布上的差异，捕捉全局不确定性；(2) 基于扰动的协方差差异(PCD)，比较高斯扰动下的特征协方差，捕捉局部不确定性。通过聚合VD和PCD进行两样本检验，实现统计对抗检测。

Result: USAD在多种对抗攻击下均取得优于基线方法的检测性能，验证了捕捉对抗样本特征性行为对有效统计对抗检测的重要性。

Conclusion: 考虑对抗样本的不确定性特征（全局散布异常和局部扰动不稳定）能够显著提升统计对抗检测的效果，USAD为SAD领域提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Statistical adversarial detection (SAD) treats detection as a two-sample test. Given a reference set of clean examples (CEs) and a batch of queries, potentially containing an unknown mixture of CEs and adversarial examples (AEs), SAD decides whether the query distribution drifts away from the CE distribution while controlling the false-alarm rate. Existing SAD-based methods mainly use maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to measure the distributional discrepancy. However, MMD's distributional properties limit its ability to capture characteristic uncertainty patterns of AEs that are crucial for detection: AEs typically exhibit abnormal feature spread (i.e., global uncertainty) and instability under perturbations (i.e., local uncertainty). To close the gap, we propose Uncertainty-aware Statistical Adversarial Detection (USAD), which explicitly captures these uncertainty patterns with two new statistics: (1) Variance Discrepancy (VD), which measures the difference in feature spread between AEs and CEs to capture global uncertainty differences. (2) Perturbation-based Covariance Discrepancy (PCD), which compares feature covariance under Gaussian perturbations to capture local uncertainty differences. By aggregating VD and PCD, USAD achieves superior detection performances over baseline methods against various adversarial attacks, highlighting the importance of considering characteristic behaviors of AEs for effective SAD. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/USAD.

</details>


### [190] [WattLayer: Get Layers Right to Estimate Inference Energy of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27841)
*Adrien Sardi,Marie-Line Alberi Morel,Sara Alouf,Frédéric Giroire,Joanna Moulierac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种任务无关、逐层分解的AI推理能耗估算模型，在295种架构、3类任务、3种硬件平台上验证，中位误差19.6%，优于现有方法，且无需完全重训练即可泛化到新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI广泛应用引发能耗担忧，但缺乏标准化的方法来准确估算AI推理能耗，尤其是跨任务、跨架构场景下的能耗评估。

Method: 提出任务无关的逐层能耗估算模型，利用跨架构共享层实现逐层分解，在新任务上无需完全重训练即可泛化。

Result: 在超过10万层、295种神经网络架构、3种广泛使用的任务和3种不同硬件平台的大规模数据集上，模型实现19.6%的中位误差，优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 该逐层能耗估算方法具有任务无关性和良好的泛化能力，为利益相关者设计高能效AI系统提供了精确的工具、见解和方法论。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) has led to increasing concerns about energy consumption, yet there is a lack of standardized methodologies to accurately estimate AI inference energy consumption, particularly across various tasks and architectures. In this study, we propose a task independent, layer-wise energy estimation model for AI architectures. Our model is evaluated on a large dataset of more than 100,000 layers for 295 neural network architectures across 3 widely-used tasks and 3 distinct hardware platforms. Our approach achieves a median error of 19.6%, outperforming state-of-the-art methods. We further show that layer-wise decomposition generalize to new tasks without complete retraining, by leveraging shared layers across architectures. It offer tools, insights and a precise methodology to empower stakeholders in designing energy-efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [191] [Applicability of memorization indicators for early spotting of overfitting while recalibrating sEMG-decoders on low sample sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27855)
*Stephan J. Lehmler,Tobias Glasmachers,Ioannis Iossifidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在少样本sEMG（表面肌电图）校准场景下，利用ReLU激活统计量作为记忆化指标来检测过拟合。通过在预训练CNN上进行迁移学习实验，发现微调过程中测试精度下降与激活率特征变化相伴发生，表明基于激活的记忆化指标有望成为低样本校准中早期发现不良学习的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG深度学习模型需要用户特异性校准，但校准可收集的样本量极为有限，容易导致过拟合甚至性能反降。传统过拟合检测手段（验证集、早停法）在低样本场景中难以应用，因为额外预留数据在实际校准中几乎不可得。因此亟需一种仅依赖训练数据即可判断过拟合的方法。

Method: 作者采用迁移学习范式：先用多个被试数据预训练CNN，然后在个体用户上仅用少量repetition进行微调。在校准过程中，同时监测解码性能及最后隐藏层ReLU的激活行为，考察一类基于ReLU激活统计量的"记忆化指标"（memorization indicators）。该指标仅从训练数据计算，无需验证集。

Result: 实验结果表明，微调过程中测试精度下降时，伴随出现激活率的特征性变化，两者之间存在关联。这为激活记忆化指标在低样本sEMG校准中作为过拟合早期预警信号提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 基于ReLU激活统计的记忆化指标无需额外验证数据即可反映过拟合倾向，是低样本sEMG校准场景中早期发现不良学习的有前景的工具，未来可用于提升用户校准的鲁棒性和接受度。

Abstract: Deep learning models for surface electromyography (sEMG) can benefit substantially from subject-specific (re-)calibration, since no sufficiently large and diverse datasets are available to train fully generic decoders. However, for user acceptance, the number of repetitions that can realistically be collected during calibration is severely limited, which increases the risk of overfitting and, in extreme cases, can even degrade performance compared to the uncalibrated model. Classical overfitting indicators such as validation performance and regularization with early stopping are difficult to apply in this low-sample regime, as they require additional held-out data that is rarely available in practical calibration scenarios. In this work, we investigate a recently proposed class of memorization indicators based solely on the activation statistics of rectified linear units (ReLU) in deep neural networks, which can be computed directly from training data without any extra validation set. We conduct a transferlearning experiment on a benchmark sEMG dataset, where a convolutional neural network is first pre-trained on multiple subjects and subsequently fine-tuned on individual users using only a small number of repetitions. During calibration, we monitor both decoding performance and the activation behaviour of the last hidden layer. Our results provide first evidence that decreases in test accuracy during fine-tuning are ac companied by characteristic changes in activation rates, indicating that activation-based memorization indicators are a promising tool for early spotting of unsuccessful learning in low-sample sEMG calibration settings.

</details>


### [192] [GNBAN: Graph Neural Basis Attention Networks for Long-Horizon Forecasting over Large Entity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27863)
*Janak M. Patel,Anirudh Deodhar,Dagnachew Birru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GNBAN 是一种端到端的图神经网络架构，将异构图表示学习与可解释的基分解预测头相结合，通过逐基注意力机制让不同基函数专注于不同时间模式，在零售需求预测的大规模基准上实现了约4-5%的WRMSSE提升，同时天然暴露趋势、季节性和残差等需求驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售需求预测面临三大挑战：底层序列数量庞大（数万级）、高度相关、预测跨度长。传统统计方法需要逐序列建模，难以规模化；深度自回归模型在联合状态维度达万级以上时难以训练；图预测方法虽能捕捉跨实体依赖，但长跨度预测往往不透明。因此需要一种既能规模化处理相关序列、又能提供可解释预测的统一框架。

Method: GNBAN 包含两大核心设计：（1）异构图表示学习——将零售数据直接按关系模式建模为异构图，使单模型服务整个产品目录；（2）可解释的基分解预测头——将每个预测分解为趋势、季节性和通用基成分，而非直接预测整个时间跨度。关键创新在于逐基注意力机制：每个基函数维护各自可学习的查询向量，独立从实体的历史邻居中检索信息，使不同基能专门化到不同时间模式，同时保持可解释性。

Result: 在两个大规模基准数据集（M5 Walmart 和 Favorita Grocery Sales）上，采用匹配协议评估，GNBAN 在体积加权的 WRMSSE 指标上相比匹配的图基线方法提升约 4-5%。定性分析表明，学习到的分解无需事后解释方法即可清晰暴露趋势、季节性和残差需求驱动因素。

Conclusion: 可扩展的关系预测与可解释的预测分解可以在统一的图框架中同时实现，GNBAN 为大规模零售需求预测提供了一种兼顾性能和透明度的实用方案。

Abstract: Demand forecasting at the bottom of a retail hierarchy requires predicting tens of thousands of correlated long-horizon series across products, stores, and regions. Modern systems must scale across massive catalogs, capture shared demand dynamics, and remain interpretable enough to be trusted. Classical statistical methods need a separate model per series and are hard to manage at scale; deep autoregressive models struggle as the joint state grows to tens of thousands of dimensions; and recent graph-based forecasters, while capturing cross-entity dependencies, often produce opaque long-horizon forecasts. We propose GNBAN (Graph Neural Basis Attention Network), an end-to-end architecture combining heterogeneous graph representation learning with an interpretable basis-decomposition head. Retail data are represented directly as a heterogeneous graph derived from the relational schema, so a single model serves the entire catalog. Rather than predicting the horizon directly, GNBAN decomposes each forecast into trend, seasonal, and generic components. Its key innovation is a per-basis attention mechanism: each basis function keeps its own learnable query and retrieves information independently from the entity's historical neighborhood, letting different bases specialize to distinct temporal patterns while preserving interpretability. On two large-scale benchmarks, M5 Walmart and Favorita Grocery Sales, evaluated under matched protocols, GNBAN improves volume-weighted WRMSSE by roughly 4-5% over a matched graph baseline. Qualitative analysis shows the learned decomposition exposes trend, seasonal, and residual demand drivers without post-hoc explanation methods. These results demonstrate that scalable relational forecasting and interpretable forecast decomposition can be achieved together in a unified graph-based framework.

</details>


### [193] [FlexMoE: One-for-All Nested Intra-Expert Pruning for MoE Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27866)
*Fan Mo,Yuxuan Han,Geng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将预训练MoE大模型转化为可嵌套部署子网络族的方法，通过通道重要性排序与离散动作学习实现多预算下的渐进式剪枝，仅需一次训练即可导出从高到低预算的剪枝方案，并在Qwen2-57B-A14B上实现剪枝50%路由专家参数而保留~99.8%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE压缩方法大多为固定预算，每次仅优化一个目标预算下的压缩端点，无法适应不同设备、用户和工作负载下差异巨大的部署预算需求。作者希望将单个预训练MoE大模型转化为可在多个预算灵活切换的嵌套子网络族。

Method: 1. 对每个专家的FFN通道按重要性排序；2. 让每个专家学习离散动作（action）来决定剪枝哪些通道；3. 通过逐步增加成本压力（cost pressure），单次动作训练运行即可导出从高到低预算的一系列动作掩码，每个掩码标识一个嵌套在排序基模型中的可靠子网络；4. 在中等剪枝预算（40%）处进行一次恢复微调（recovery fine-tune），并将恢复后的模型迁移到其他未见预算。

Result: 在Qwen2-57B-A14B上，不进行微调的情况下剪枝50%路由专家参数仍能保留约99.8%的基础性能，超越现有MoE压缩基线方法。剪枝后的子网络实现了真实的内存减少和吞吐量提升，并支持内核级协同设计的实时在线预算切换。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将大规模预训练MoE模型转化为预算可弹性伸缩的嵌套子网络族，兼顾压缩效率与模型质量，并具备在线动态切换部署预算的实用能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models scale model ability with sparsely activated experts, making this architecture a standard recipe for modern large models. However, sparse activation does not remove the deployment burden of storing and serving all experts, and the available deployment budget can vary substantially across devices, users, and workloads. Existing MoE compression methods are still largely fixed-budget, typically optimizing one compressed endpoint at each chosen target budget. We study a different setting: converting a large pretrained MoE LLM into a nested family of deployable subnetworks across budgets. Our method first ranks expert FFN channels by their importance, then lets each expert learn a discrete action to prune its channels. By gradually increasing cost pressure, a single action-training run exports a series of action masks from high to low budgets, each of which identifies a reliable smaller subnetwork nested in the ranked base model. Moreover, we use a single recovery fine-tune at a mid pruning budget (40%) to recover degraded model quality and transfer the recovered model to other unseen budgets. Overall, our framework surpasses recent MoE compression baselines. Specifically, on Qwen2-57B-A14B, our method retains ~99.8% of base performance while pruning 50% of routed expert parameters even without fine-tuning. For deployment, our pruned subnetworks deliver real memory reduction and throughput gains, and further support realtime online budget switching with kernel-level co-design.

</details>


### [194] [A Comparison of Fusion Techniques for Multi-Modal Human Activity Recognition on the HARMES Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27886)
*Ahmed Mohamady,Robin Burchard,Kristof Van Laerhoven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多模态人类活动识别（HAR）中的七种传感器融合方法进行了系统性头对头比较，在HARMES数据集上实验表明，门控多模态融合（Gated Multi-modal Fusion）以0.82的宏观F1分数表现最优，比基线高出6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态深度学习在可穿戴传感器HAR中表现优异，且已有多种融合范式被提出，但目前缺乏在统一基准数据集上对这些融合方法的头对头系统性比较，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 在HARMES数据集（包含61小时的IMU、音频和环境湿度标注数据，涵盖15种家庭及个人卫生活动）上，采用留一参与者交叉验证，对七种最先进的传感器融合方法进行系统比较，所有方法均基于统一的多模态模型架构实现。

Result: 门控多模态融合方法取得了最高的宏观F1分数（0.82），相比基于拼接的后期融合HARMES基线（0.76）提升了6个百分点。

Conclusion: 在多模态HAR任务中，门控多模态融合策略显著优于传统的拼接式后期融合方法，填补了融合范式系统性比较的研究空白，所有实验代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors have shown that multi-modal deep learning models consistently outperform their uni-modal counterparts. Modalities can include IMUs, RGB cameras, audio signals, and others. One important aspect of multi-modal deep learning is the sensor fusion approach we apply. Over recent years, multiple fusion paradigms have been proposed for multi-modal HAR. However, to the best of our knowledge, no head-to-head comparison of these paradigms exists on a common multi-modal HAR benchmark dataset. To address this research gap, we systematically compare seven state-of-the-art sensor fusion methods on the recently released HARMES dataset, which comprises 61 hours of fully labeled IMU, audio, and ambient humidity data. The chosen dataset focuses on 15 household and personal hygiene activities of daily living (ADLs). By applying the seven different fusion techniques to a state-of-the-art multi-modal model architecture, we show that Gated Multi-modal Fusion achieves the highest macro F1-score (0.82), surpassing the concatenation-based late fusion HARMES paper baseline of 0.76 by +6pp under leave-one-participant-out evaluation. All code used in our experiments is made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [195] [TA-SparseMG: Trend-Aware Sparse Forecasting via Multi-Scale Gating for Long-Term Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27908)
*Wenchao Liu,Hongbing Wang,Youji Zhu,Xiaodong Liu,Xiangguang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TA-SparseMG 是一种轻量级长时序列预测模型，通过在 SparseTSF 稀疏跨周期框架上引入趋势感知归一化、尺度自适应门控去噪和多尺度门控注意力三个模块，有效应对非平稳性、高频扰动和跨周期耦合等挑战，在多个基准上取得稳定优越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序列预测面临统计非平稳性、局部高频扰动以及耦合的跨周期依赖等固有挑战，现有轻量级模型难以在参数效率与预测性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 基于 SparseTSF 的稀疏跨周期建模框架，提出 TA-SparseMG 模型，包含三个核心模块：(1) 趋势感知可逆实例归一化模块，捕获输入窗口统计量并校准预测窗口分布，缓解分布偏移；(2) 尺度自适应门控去噪模块，在周期重排前进行特征平滑与残差抑制，减少高频扰动干扰；(3) 多尺度门控注意力 MLP 预测模块，通过条件门控与特征调制增强预测头的自适应表征能力。

Result: 在多个 LTSF 基准上的广泛实验表明，TA-SparseMG 持续取得优越且稳定的性能；消融实验证实各模块分别独立改善了分布适配、输入鲁棒性和跨周期特征映射能力。

Conclusion: TA-SparseMG 通过三个模块的协同设计，有效平衡了轻量级模型的参数效率与预测性能，为解决长时序列预测中的非平稳性、高频噪声和跨周期依赖问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting finds extensive applications in domains such as power demand, traffic flow, meteorological observation, and renewable energy dispatch. Forecasting dynamically varying long-term time series poses inherent challenges, including statistical nonstationarity, local high-frequency disturbances, and coupled cross-period dependencies, which make it difficult for lightweight models to balance parameter efficiency and forecasting performance. To address this issue, this study presents TA-SparseMG, a lightweight cross-period forecasting model built on SparseTSF's sparse cross-period modeling framework. It incorporates three key modules: a trend-aware reversible instance normalization module, a scale-adaptive gated denoising module, and a multiscale gated-attention MLP forecasting module. The trend-aware normalization module captures input-window statistics and calibrates forecast-window distributions, effectively mitigating distribution shift. The scale-adaptive gated denoising module performs feature smoothing and residual suppression before period rearrangement, thereby reducing interference from high-frequency perturbations. The multiscale gated attention prediction module strengthens the prediction head's adaptive representational capacity via conditional gating and feature modulation. Extensive experiments across multiple LTSF benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed TA-SparseMG consistently achieves superior, stable performance. Ablation studies confirm that each module independently improves distribution adaptation, input robustness, and cross-period feature mapping capability.

</details>


### [196] [Towards Automating Scientific Review with Google's Paper Assistant Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28277)
*Rajesh Jayaram,Drew Tyler,David Woodruff,Corinna Cortes,Yossi Matias,Vahab Mirrokni,Vincent Cohen-Addad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四层级AI-人类协作评审分类法，并介绍了论文助手工具PAT——一个基于推理扩展技术的智能体AI框架。PAT能深度审校科学论文，在SPOT基准上数学错误检测的召回率比零样本调用提升34%，并在STOC和ICML会议上成功试点部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的科学发现正在加速，但传统人类同行评审无法与之同步扩展，形成了系统性的验证瓶颈。必须部署AI来加速科学验证和评审过程本身，以解决这一结构性矛盾。

Method: （1）提出四层级递进的AI-人类协作科学评审分类法；（2）开发PAT（Paper Assistant Tool），一个能够摄入完整论文手稿并进行深度评审的智能体AI框架，涵盖理论结果核查、实验验证、改进建议和缺陷识别；（3）采用推理扩展（inference scaling）技术，通过多步推理而非单次模型调用发现更深层问题。

Result: 在SPOT基准上，PAT的数学错误召回率相比零样本调用提高了34%。在STOC和ICML两个顶级计算机科学会议上作为作者投稿前工具的试点部署中，PAT成功识别出关键错误并提出实质性改进建议。

Conclusion: 通过将AI引入评审流程，PAT能够及早发现错误、减轻审稿人的认知负担，同时保持人类对评审结果的控制权，为AI辅助科学评审的规模化应用迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is driving a revolution in scientific discovery, accelerating everything from hypothesis generation to mathematical theorem proving. However, this rapid acceleration is creating a systemic challenge: traditional human peer review cannot scale to match the influx of AI-assisted science. Ultimately, to resolve this tension, we must also deploy AI to accelerate the verification and review process itself. To frame the discussion around this transition, we propose a taxonomy consisting of four progressive levels of AI-human collaboration in scientific evaluation, and discuss various trade-offs involved with each.
  As a step toward this future, we introduce the Paper Assistant Tool (PAT), an agentic AI framework built for deep scientific review and verification. PAT ingests full scientific manuscripts and produces a comprehensive evaluation, checking theoretical results, validating experiments, suggesting improvements, and identifying potential flaws. By utilizing inference scaling techniques, PAT is able to identify deeper issues than a single model call alone, achieving a 34% improvement over zero-shot recall on mathematical errors in the SPOT benchmark. Pilot deployments of PAT as a pre-submission tool for authors at two major Computer Science conferences -- STOC and ICML -- demonstrate its ability to identify critical errors and suggest substantive improvements to research papers. By catching errors early, PAT eases the cognitive burden placed on referees, while preserving their control over the outcomes of the review process.

</details>


### [197] [Graph Dimensionality Reduction for Contextual Bandits: Structure-Specific Regret Bounds under Approximate Smoothness and Noisy Eigenspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27917)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphDR-LinUCB，将臂特征投影到图的低频谱子空间，在k维空间运行线性UCB。证明首个基于谱投影的上下文赌博机$\wtO(k\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，将维度依赖从$d$降至$k$。核心理论发现高频奖励分量的实际代价取决于其在博弈路径上的实现影响而非总能量。在6个真实数据集中5个上超越竞争方法，累积遗憾降低15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准降维忽略图结构，导致探索代价膨胀$d/k$倍。在图结构的臂（如推荐、引文检索）上，相连臂共享奖励信号，但现有方法未利用该结构。

Method: 将臂特征投影到图拉普拉斯矩阵的低频（小特征值）谱子空间上，在所得k维空间中运行LinUCB。通过扰动分析扩展至含噪图，并引入谱比较度量Γₖ预测不同降维方法的优劣。

Result: 证明$\wtO(k\sqrt{T})$遗憾界（首个子空间投影上下文赌博机结果）；高频分量不产生线性于T的最坏情况惩罚；Γₖ在6个真实数据集中正确预测5个的胜负；在合成基准和6个真实数据集上，累积遗憾较全维LinUCB降低15倍，5/6数据集优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 基于图谱子空间投影的降维方法有效利用臂间图结构，大幅降低探索代价，且谱比较度量Γₖ可无需训练阈值即可预测降维方法在具体数据集上的表现。

Abstract: Contextual bandits with graph-structured arms arise in recommendation, citation retrieval, and social advertising, where arms connected on a graph tend to share reward signal. Standard dimensionality reduction ignores this structure, inflating exploration cost by a factor of $d/k$. We propose GraphDR-LinUCB, which projects arm features onto the graph's low-frequency spectral subspace and runs linear UCB in the resulting $k$-dimensional space. We prove the first $\wtO(k\sqrt{T})$ regret bound for spectral-projection-based contextual bandits, reducing dimension dependence from $d$ to $k$; a perturbation argument extends this to noisy graphs, with an explicit penalty for reward-smoothness mismatch and graph-estimation error. Our central theoretical finding is that the high-frequency reward component need not incur a worst-case linear-in-$T$ penalty: its actual cost depends on its realized impact along the played path, not on its total energy. A simple spectral comparison between subspaces ($Γ_k$) predicts which reducer wins on a given dataset, correctly calling five of six real-dataset outcomes without any fitted threshold. Across a synthetic benchmark and six real datasets (MovieLens, Amazon, LastFM, ogbn-arxiv, MIND), GraphDR-LinUCB reduces cumulative regret by $15\times$ over full-dimensional LinUCB and outperforms competing graph-aware methods on five of six; the single failure is precisely where the graph's spectral subspace is misaligned with the reward.

</details>


### [198] [Two-Stage Fine-Tuning for Protein Sequence Generation with Targeted Amino-Acid Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27939)
*Violeta Basten-Romero,Rubén Muñoz-Tafalla,Anna María Díaz-Rovira,Bertran Miquel-Oliver,Isaac Filella-Merce,Víctor Guallar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究受约束的蛋白质序列生成问题，提出先进行领域自适应微调（FT）、再进行基于奖励加权的强化学习（RL）的两阶段流程，以实现目标氨基酸组成轮廓的精确匹配，同时保持序列质量和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成饲料蛋白设计中，膳食蛋白质的氨基酸组成直接决定其营养价值。现有蛋白质语言模型虽能生成合理序列，但缺乏将生成引向显式分布设计目标（如特定AA组成）的能力。

Method: 提出两阶段流程：第一阶段在领域内蛋白质数据集上进行领域自适应微调（FT）；第二阶段采用迭代奖励加权微调，通过强化学习（RL）优化氨基酸组成奖励项，并以冻结的FT模型作为KL正则化参考锚点，防止序列质量退化。

Result: FT使平均氨基酸组成接近目标；后续RL进一步满足了FT无法单独实现的特定序列约束。还评估了组成奖励项的设计选择（对比两种基线和一种消融变体），分离了各训练阶段的贡献，并验证了AA组成对齐未损害序列质量。

Conclusion: 领域自适应FT结合RL奖励微调的两阶段策略可有效实现蛋白质序列的氨基酸组成控制，在达成精确组成约束的同时保留了序列的合理统计特性和多样性。

Abstract: Protein language models are standard priors for biological sequence generation, but steering them toward explicit distributional design targets remains largely unexplored. We study a constrained protein generation problem in which sequences must match a desired amino-acid (AA) composition profile while preserving plausible sequence statistics and diversity. The motivating application is synthetic feed protein design, where the AA composition of dietary proteins directly determines their nutritional value. We propose a two-stage pipeline in which domain-adaptive fine-tuning (FT) on an in-domain protein dataset is followed by iterative reward-weighted FT via reinforcement learning (RL) anchored against the FT model as a frozen reference. We evaluate the pipeline on two AA compositions and find that FT brings the average composition close to the target, while the subsequent RL enforces specific sequence constraints that FT alone cannot satisfy. We additionally evaluate the design choices of the proposed composition reward term against two baselines and an ablated variant, isolate the contribution of each training stage, and verify that AA composition alignment is achieved without degrading sequence quality.

</details>


### [199] [RECAST: Model Reconstruction via Counterfactual-Aware Wasserstein Geometry under Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27948)
*Xuan Zhao,Lena Krieger,Zhuo Cao,Arya Bangun,Hanno Scharr,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAST 提出一种基于Wasserstein重心原型的黑盒模型重构方法，利用反事实解释在有限数据和受限访问条件下构建高保真替代模型，并支持系统性的群体公平性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反事实解释的黑盒模型重构方法面临三个关键问题：决策边界偏移、过拟合，以及对目标平台在线查询访问的强假设。在数据稀缺和访问受限的现实场景中，这些问题尤为突出，亟需一种更鲁棒的替代建模方法。

Method: 提出RECAST框架，核心是反事实感知的Wasserstein优化。具体地，将反事实样本视为两类别的信息性（但代表性不足）样本，通过Wasserstein重心原型（barycentric prototypes）构建行为替代模型，无需在重构阶段进行在线查询即可维持高保真度。

Result: 在真实世界数据集和多种实验设置下，RECAST 有效实现了高替代保真度和查询效率，即便在访问受限和存在噪声的条件下也能保持稳定结果。

Conclusion: RECAST 成功解决了决策边界偏移问题，在低样本条件下保持高替代保真度，同时无需在线访问，并能支持系统性的群体公平性审计，为第三方审计不透明决策系统提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFs) help understand machine learning models by identifying minimal input changes that would lead to alternative model outcomes. Recent work demonstrates their utility for reconstructing black-box models, enabling third-party auditing of opaque decision systems for fairness and accountability. Still, CF-based reconstruction may suffer from decision boundary shifts, overfitting, and restrictive assumptions requiring online query access to target platforms. We propose REconstruction via Counterfactual-Aware waSserstein opTimization (RECAST) under limited data and restricted access, a behavioral surrogate model based on Wasserstein barycentric prototypes. Our approach addresses decision boundary shifts by incorporating CFs as informative, though less representative, samples for both classes, maintaining high surrogate fidelity in low-sample regimes without requiring online access during reconstruction. To enhance fairness auditing, our method enables systematic group fairness diagnostics. Experiments on real-world datasets and various setups show that RECAST effectively achieves high fidelity and query efficiency, as well as stable results even when the access is limited and noisy.

</details>


### [200] [Dual-Learning based Penalized Multi-Align Clustering for Multi-View Incomplete and Disorderly Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27984)
*Liang Zhao,Shubin Ma,Bo Xu,Qingchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对偶学习的惩罚式多对齐聚类模型(DLPMAC)，通过双学习机制和惩罚式多对齐模块，解决多模态数据融合中模态缺失、时间异步以及类别数据量差异导致的样本级对齐不准确问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
