Motivation: 现有多模态融合方法依赖聚类中心对齐，存在两大缺陷：(1)无法保证样本级数据对的精确对齐；(2)未处理不同类别间数据量差异显著的问题，影响后续融合性能。实际应用（如锅炉燃烧监测）中模态缺失和时间异步进一步加剧了这些挑战。

Method: 提出DLPMAC模型，包含两个核心组件：(1)对偶学习机制——从各模态中学习语义和结构先验知识，在局部和全局层面保持跨模态的语义一致性与结构相似性；(2)惩罚式多对齐模块——通过惩罚机制实现多对多数据对齐，允许一个样本与来自其他模态的不同样本形成数据对，提升对齐精度，同时防止过多样本链接到同一单个样本的数据聚集现象。

Result: 实验结果表明，DLPMAC在采样和聚类两个角度均能有效解决数据对齐与融合问题，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DLPMAC通过引入对偶学习和惩罚式多对齐机制，有效克服了现有多模态融合方法在样本级对齐和类别不平衡方面的局限性，为模态缺失与时间异步场景下的多模态数据融合提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal feature fusion can effectively capture complex patterns in real-world data by integrating complementary information from different modalities. However, in many applications, such as boiler combustion monitoring, equipment failure, inconsistent sensor sampling frequencies, and network delays often cause missing modalities and temporal asynchrony. These issues lead to incomplete and disorderly multimodal data. To address them, previous studies have proposed several data fusion methods that align cluster centers before fusion. However, these methods have two key limitations. First, they cannot guarantee accurate sample-level alignment of data pairs. Second, they do not address significant discrepancies in data sizes across different classes, which may affect subsequent fusion performance.
  To address these problems, we propose a dual-learning based penalized multi-align clustering model, named DLPMAC. The dual-learning mechanism enables the model to learn prior knowledge from each modality, including semantic and structural information. This helps preserve semantic consistency and structural similarity across modalities at both local and global levels. In addition, the penalized multi-align module performs multi-to-multi data alignment through a penalty mechanism. It allows one sample to form data pairs with different samples from other modalities, thereby improving data-pair alignment accuracy. The penalty mechanism also prevents data aggregation, avoiding the case where excessive samples are linked to a single sample. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of DLPMAC in addressing data alignment and fusion challenges from both sampling and clustering perspectives.

</details>


### [201] [OperatorSHAP: Fast and Accurate Shapley Value Estimation for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28065)
*Joshua Stiller,Santo M. A. R. Thies,Felix Czaja,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OperatorSHAP 提出了一种适用于神经算子的网格无关归因方法，将 Shapley 值扩展到函数空间，解决了物理应用中不规则网格数据的事后解释问题，且可在不同分辨率间迁移而无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理应用（如结构载荷评估、天气预报、临床诊断）中的模型预测解释至关重要，但当前摊销解释器（如 FastSHAP）仅适用于同质化输入，无法处理来自不规则网格和几何结构的数据。因此需要一种能对神经算子进行高效归因的方法。

Method: 提出 OperatorSHAP，一种网格无关的归因方法和训练流程。核心思路是将 Shapley 值扩展到函数空间，建立与 Aumann-Shapley 值的理论联系。通过训练类似 FastSHAP 的摊销解释器，使其能够直接对神经算子输出进行归因，而不依赖特定离散化网格。

Result: OperatorSHAP 的解释结果在不同分辨率下与最先进的离散 Shapley 值保持一致，并且无需重新训练即可在不同网格尺寸之间迁移。

Conclusion: OperatorSHAP 成功将摊销 Shapley 解释方法推广到函数空间，为物理应用中基于神经算子的安全关键预测提供了高效、灵活且理论完备的归因手段。

Abstract: Understanding model predictions is essential for physical applications, where outputs often inform safety-critical decisions, such as structural load assessment, weather warnings, and clinical diagnosis. Shapley values satisfy many desirable properties as an attribution method, but their computational cost during inference hinders their practical use. Current amortized explainers, such as FastSHAP, are limited to homogeneous inputs, which is problematic for physical applications where data often comes from irregular grids and geometries. We introduce OperatorSHAP, a grid-agnostic attribution method and training procedure that allows us to train FastSHAP-like explainers for neural operators. We establish a theoretical framework for attributions in function space, connecting to Aumann-Shapley values. We further show that OperatorSHAP's explanations are consistent with state-of-the-art discrete Shapley values across resolutions and transfer across grid sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [202] [Fair Classification with Efficient and Post-hoc Controllable Fairness-Accuracy Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28097)
*Maaya Sakata,Kazuto Fukuchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于梯度优化的公平分类算法，通过学习有效的特征表示来提升后处理公平分类器的权衡效率，在不需重新训练的情况下，达到甚至超越训练中（in-processing）方法的权衡表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后处理方法虽支持训练后的可控性（post-hoc controllability），但常伴随显著的精度损失；而训练中方法虽能高效权衡公平与精度，但每次调整权衡比例都需要昂贵的重新训练。因此需要一种兼具两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的公平分类算法，采用基于梯度的优化方法学习有效的特征表示（feature representations），从而提升后处理公平分类器的权衡效率。该方法在训练后无需重新训练即可灵活调整公平性与精度的权衡。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在权衡效率上可与训练中方法相媲美甚至更优，同时完全避免了重新训练的计算开销。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了后处理可控性与高效权衡的统一，为公平机器学习模型的灵活高效部署提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-hoc controllability of fair machine learning models, the ability to control the trade-off between fairness and accuracy after training, is valuable for practical deployment. Existing post-processing methods provide such post-hoc controllability but often suffer from significant accuracy degradation, whereas in-processing methods achieve efficient trade-offs but require computationally expensive retraining for each change in trade-off ratio. To achieve both post-hoc controllability and efficient trade-offs, we propose a novel fair classification algorithm that learns effective feature representations to improve the trade-off efficiency of post-processing fair classifiers, by a gradient-based optimization approach. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves trade-off efficiency comparable to, or even surpassing, in-processing methods, without requiring any retraining.

</details>


### [203] [When One Adapter Speaks for Many: Discovering Low-Rank Redundancy in Continual Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28117)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Giulia Lanzillotta,Enis Simsar,Louis Barinka,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了持续学习中每个任务需独立LoRA适配器的标准假设，发现任务间存在显著低秩冗余，并提出LiteLoRA——一种通过门控机制决定复用或新建适配器的方法，可减少20-70%的适配器数量且性能不减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准持续学习假设每个新任务都需要专属的低秩适配器，但作者通过实证观察发现，不同任务的LoRA适配器之间存在显著的子空间重叠，早期适配器常能忠实表征后续任务。这种结构冗余意味着可以大幅减少适配器数量，从而提升参数效率。

Method: 提出LiteLoRA，一种即插即用的门控机制。在训练时，该方法学习判断当前任务应激活一个新的低秩适配器，还是复用已有的低秩表示。该门控机制在训练过程中动态决策，从而在保持模型容量（plasticity）的同时，通过选择性学习实现参数稳定（stability），无需为每个任务分配独立适配器。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准上，LiteLoRA将活跃适配器数量减少了20-70%，同时性能匹配甚至超越了现有最先进方法，验证了结构冗余的普遍性以及选择性学习足以兼顾稳定性与可塑性。

Conclusion: 持续学习中LoRA适配器存在广泛的结构冗余，通过LiteLoRA的门控选择性学习机制，可以在大幅降低参数开销的同时保持高性能，为高效持续学习提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard tool for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large pretrained models. When applied sequentially across tasks in Continual Learning (CL), the standard assumption is that each new task requires a dedicated low-rank adapter. In this work, we challenge this assumption empirically and structurally. We show that task-specific LoRA adapters in CL exhibit significant low-rank redundancy: the subspaces spanned by adapters trained on different tasks substantially overlap, and in many cases earlier adapters can faithfully represent later tasks. Building on this observation, we propose LiteLoRA, a plug-and-play gating mechanism that learns at train time whether to recruit a new adapter or reuse existing low-rank representations. Our method reduces the number of active adapters by 20-70% while matching or exceeding state-of-the-art performance on standard CL benchmarks, revealing that structural redundancy is pervasive and that selective learning is sufficient to achieve stability without sacrificing plasticity.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Sparse Supervision: Diffusion-Guided Learning for Few-Shot Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28134)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Yiwei Ge,Xingle Li,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADC-GNN 是一个面向少样本图欺诈检测的统一框架，通过扩散引导的特征增强、对比表征学习和多跳谱注意力，联合解决了标签稀疏不均衡与表征稀释两大挑战，在公开基准和私有电信数据集上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实欺诈图面临两个耦合挑战：(1) 监督信号稀疏且极度不均衡——已确认的欺诈标签稀少且严重偏向正常账户；(2) 表征稀释——空间消息传递会过度平滑伪装异常节点，而谱滤波器可能抑制与欺诈相关的中高频不规则信号。现有方法难以同时应对这两个问题。

Method: 提出 ADC-GNN 统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 扩散组件——将扩散建模为特征空间去噪增强机制（非全拓扑生成式图扩散），在余弦调度下构造噪声扰动的节点特征视图，利用对比学习稳定跨扰动表征；(2) 对比表征学习——在扰动视图间保持节点表征一致性；(3) 多跳谱注意力模块——自适应强调与欺诈相关的跳级（hop-level）和关系级（relation-level）线索。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集及一个约6万条记录的私有真实电信交易数据集上进行评估。在1%训练设置下，ADC-GNN 在公开基准上相较于原始图欺诈基线及四个协议一致的近期图异常/欺诈基线均取得一致提升。此外，对划分稳定性、训练比例、过采样替代方案、模块消融、扩散调度、运行时间和内存消耗等进行了全面分析。

Conclusion: ADC-GNN 通过扩散-对比-谱注意力的统一设计，有效缓解了少样本图欺诈检测中的标签稀疏不均衡和表征稀释问题，验证了扩散作为特征空间增强（而非全图生成）这一设计选择的有效性，为图欺诈检测提供了一种可操作且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-based fraud detection is essential for safeguarding large-scale transaction systems, where undetected anomalies may lead to substantial financial losses and security risks. Real-world fraud graphs pose two coupled challenges: sparse and imbalanced supervision, where verified fraudulent labels are scarce and heavily skewed toward benign accounts, and representation dilution, where spatial message passing may oversmooth camouflaged anomalies while spectral filters may suppress fraud-relevant mid- and high-frequency irregularities. To address these challenges, we propose ADC-GNN, short for Attention-guided Diffusion-Contrastive Graph Neural Network, a unified framework that combines diffusion-guided feature augmentation, contrastive representation learning, and multi-hop spectral attention for few-shot graph fraud detection. The diffusion component is formulated as a feature-space denoising augmentation mechanism rather than a full topology-generative graph diffusion model: it constructs noise-perturbed node-feature views under a cosine schedule and uses contrastive learning to stabilize node representations across perturbations. The spectral attention module further adaptively emphasizes fraud-relevant hop-level and relation-level cues. We evaluate ADC-GNN primarily on three public benchmarks and additionally report a proprietary real-world telecom transaction dataset with approximately 60,000 records as a private case study. Under the 1% training setting, ADC-GNN achieves consistent improvements over original graph fraud baselines and four protocol-consistent recent graph anomaly/fraud baselines on the public benchmarks. Additional analyses on split stability, training ratios, oversampling alternatives, module-level ablations, diffusion schedules, and runtime and memory-consumption comparisons further characterize the effective operating regime of ADC-GNN.

</details>


### [205] [MixTTA: Low-Rank Cross-Channel Mixing for Reliable Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28142)
*Mansoo Jung,Youngwook Kim,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MixTTA 提出一种轻量级跨通道混合模块，通过低秩变换增强归一化层的表达能力，解决传统 TTA 方法中逐通道仿射参数无法纠正跨通道结构偏移的问题，并在多种 TTA 场景下取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTA 方法大多只更新归一化层的逐通道仿射参数（缩放和偏移），这些参数在几何上仅限于轴对齐变换，无法纠正因分布偏移引起的跨通道结构性变化，导致在复杂偏移下适应能力不足甚至失败。

Method: 提出 MixTTA 插件模块：(1) 为归一化层引入低秩跨通道变换，实现层间通道混合；(2) 解耦投影（Decoupling Projection），强制低秩分支与对角仿射路径严格分离，确保仅捕获跨通道交互；(3) 谱投影（Spectral Projection），在非平稳测试流中防止秩-1 坍缩。该模块可无缝集成到任何基于归一化的 TTA 方法中。

Result: 在标准 TTA 和 wild TTA 两种设定下进行实验，MixTTA 在多个强基线上均取得一致提升，并在挑战性条件下有效缓解了适应失败问题。

Conclusion: MixTTA 通过轻量的低秩跨通道变换有效弥补了传统逐通道仿射参数的表征局限性，为基于归一化的 TTA 方法提供了通用且即插即用的增强方案。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods commonly update the affine parameters of normalization layers to adapt deployed models under distribution shifts. However, per-channel affine parameters perform axis-aligned scaling and shifting, making them geometrically incapable of correcting cross-channel structural changes induced by distribution shift. To address this limitation, we propose MixTTA, a lightweight plug-in module that equips normalization layers with a low-rank cross-channel transformation, enabling inter-channel mixing at each layer. To ensure that the low-rank branch captures only cross-channel interactions, we also propose Decoupling Projection that enforces strict separation from the diagonal affine path, along with Spectral Projection that prevents rank-1 collapse under non-stationary test streams. MixTTA can be seamlessly integrated into any existing normalization-based TTA method. Experiments in both standard and wild TTA settings show consistent improvements over strong baselines while mitigating adaptation failure under challenging conditions. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/delta6189/MixTTA.

</details>


### [206] [Autoencoder Architectures for Athlete Performance Scoring from Wearable Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28145)
*Mateusz Kubita,Jan Zubalewicz,Krzysztof Siwek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对可穿戴设备跑步日志的高维数据，本文系统评估了五种降维模型（三种自编码器变体、PCA 及 VAE）在将九维传感器跑步者画像压缩为单一潜在表现评分时的性能，提出结合重建误差与潜在评分可解释性的复合选择标准，发现深度自编码器综合表现最优，并确认跑步配速、有氧解耦和平均心率为主要的潜在评分驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备生成的跑步训练数据量大且维度高，数据解读（而非数据收集）已成为瓶颈。需要一种方法将多维传感器数据压缩为直观的单一表现指标（潜在评分），但现有降维模型的选择缺乏统一标准，尤其在没有监督标签的情况下，仅靠重建误差评估可能导致选择的模型缺乏可解释性。

Method: 在完全无监督设置下，对五种降维模型（深度自编码器 Deep AE、两种变体、PCA、变分自编码器 VAE）进行评估。建立两条互补评估轴：重建误差（MSE）和潜在评分可解释性（通过 Spearman 与 Kendall 秩相关、互信息、排列重要性四种指标衡量）。提出复合选择标准，避免仅依赖重建精度选模型。使用改进的 Borda 计数法聚合四种指标的特征排名，并通过 Bootstrap 重采样验证稳定性。引入双特征线性基线作为参照。

Result: 深度自编码器（Deep AE）获得最低重建误差和最高复合得分。当 PCA 的隐藏层拓宽后，其深层变体在复合标准上与 Deep AE 接近，表明瓶颈在于隐藏层容量而非一维瓶颈本身。跑步配速、有氧解耦和平均心率在所有模型和重采样轮次中均成为潜在评分的主导驱动因素，与已知生理学规律一致。

Conclusion: 无监督降维模型的选择应兼顾重建精度与潜在空间可解释性，仅凭重建误差不足以筛选出有实际意义的模型。隐藏层容量比瓶颈维度大小对模型性能影响更大。跑步配速、有氧解耦和平均心率是衡量跑步表现最稳健的生理指标。

Abstract: Wearable devices produce large, high dimensional training logs for everyday runners, and interpretation rather than data collection is now the limiting step. This paper evaluates five dimensionality reduction models, three autoencoder variants, PCA, and a Variational Autoencoder, on their ability to compress nine sensor runner profiles into a single scalar performance indicator, the latent score. Because the setting is fully unsupervised, model quality is assessed along two complementary axes: reconstruction error (Mean Squared Error) and latent score interpretability, measured via Spearman and Kendall rank correlations, Mutual Information, and Permutation Importance. These are combined into a composite selection criterion that prevents selecting models on reconstruction accuracy alone. Feature rankings from the four metrics are aggregated via a modified Borda count, and their stability is confirmed by bootstrap validation. A two feature linear baseline is included to anchor the comparison. Deep autoencoder achieved the lowest reconstruction error and the highest composite score. Once the PCA hidden layers were widened, the deeper variants became closely competitive with Deep AE on the composite criterion, indicating that the limiting factor was hidden layer capacity rather than the one dimensional bottleneck. Running pace, aerobic decoupling, and average heart rate emerged as the dominant latent score drivers across all models and resampling runs, consistent with established physiology.

</details>


### [207] [Regularized Reward-Punishment Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28152)
*Jiexin Wang,Eiji Uchibe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KL-Coupled Policy Regularization (KCPR)框架，使奖励-惩罚强化学习中的双策略通过互为动态先验实现协同，推导出KL-Coupled Soft Optimality并实现为klDMP算法，在导航任务中展现出更好的安全性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励-惩罚强化学习(RPRL)方法将奖励寻求策略和惩罚规避策略几乎独立优化，缺乏策略间直接交互，限制了奖惩信息的联合利用，导致安全性与任务性能难以均衡。

Method: 提出KCPR框架，将奖励策略和惩罚策略互为对方的动态学习先验，通过KL散度耦合；推导KCSO最优性条件和KL正则化Bellman算子；设计伴生先验软化机制和分离式经验回放缓冲区以提升训练稳定性；基于上述理论实现深度算法klDMP。

Result: 在网格世界和Gazebo机器人导航任务中，klDMP相比DQN、SQL和softDMP，在保持竞争性任务性能的同时显著提升了安全性和学习稳定性。

Conclusion: 策略级协调是整合多重行为目标的有效机制，可作为具有交互动机过程的强化学习系统的设计原则。

Abstract: We propose KL-Coupled Policy Regularization (KCPR), a policy coordination framework for Reward-Punishment Reinforcement Learning (RPRL). Based on KCPR, we derive KL-Coupled Soft Optimality (KCSO) and develop its deep realization, klDMP. Unlike existing RPRL approaches that optimize reward-seeking and punishment-related policies largely independently, KCPR enables direct interactions between companion policies by treating each as a dynamically learned prior for the other. KCSO yields coupled soft-optimal policies and KL-regularized Bellman operators, allowing reward and punishment information to jointly influence value propagation. To improve learning stability, we introduce a companion-prior softening mechanism and evaluate separate replay-buffer designs for balancing reward- and punishment-related experience. Experiments in grid-world and Gazebo robotic navigation tasks demonstrate that klDMP improves safety and learning stability while maintaining competitive task performance compared with DQN, SQL and softDMP. These results suggest that policy-level coordination provides an effective mechanism for integrating multiple behavioral objectives and may serve as a useful design principle for reinforcement learning systems with interacting motivational processes.

</details>


### [208] [Recovering Sharp Conductivity Features in the Finite-Data Calderón Problem with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28158)
*Ali AlHadi Kalout,Pablo Tejerina-Pérez,Konstantin Karchev,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez,Leonid Sarieddine,Raul Jimenez,Max Engelstein,Guy David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息神经网络的Calderón反问题求解框架，利用随机小波函数生成多尺度边界激励，并引入傅里叶特征编码来捕捉电导率的尖锐变化，在多种电导率场（包含物、尖锐界面、光滑剖面、异质介质）上实现了约3%-12%的相对重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calderón反问题（即从有限边界测量数据重建内部电导率）具有严重的不适定性。现有PINN方法在处理尖锐电导率变化时表现不佳，且边界激励的设计对重建质量的影响尚未被系统研究。本文旨在通过引入多尺度边界激励和傅里叶特征编码来提升对尖锐特征的重建能力。

Method: 提出双网络架构：一个网络表示未知电导率场，另一个网络（以边界激励为条件）表示对应的电势族。通过物理信息残差强制满足椭圆型PDE，通过边界损失函数融入有限Dirichlet-to-Neumann数据。使用随机小波函数生成多尺度边界激励以丰富测量信息。对比研究了原始坐标网络与傅里叶特征编码（FFE）在表示电导率尖锐特征时的性能差异。

Result: 在包含物、尖锐界面、光滑剖面和异质介质四类电导率场上进行数值实验。整体相对重建误差约为3%-12%。FFE显著改善了局部尖锐特征（尤其是包含物和界面）的重建效果，但对光滑场，原始坐标网络表现相当。结果揭示了坐标表示方式和边界激励设计是神经Calderón反演中的关键因素。

Conclusion: 多尺度小波边界激励结合条件PINN框架能够有效从有限边界测量中重建电导率场。傅里叶特征编码对尖锐特征重建有益但并非普适最优，坐标网络的选择应与目标电导率场的平滑特性相匹配。坐标表示和边界激励设计是影响神经Calderón反演性能的两大关键因素。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have recently emerged as a promising framework for addressing the Calderón inverse problem from limited boundary data. In this work, we revisit neural Calderón inversion by introducing multiscale boundary excitations based on randomized wavelet functions and investigating the role of Fourier-feature encoding (FFE) for representing sharp conductivity variations. We propose a physics-informed reconstruction framework that represents the unknown conductivity and the associated family of electric potentials with separate neural networks conditioned on the applied boundary excitations. The governing elliptic PDE is enforced through physics-informed residuals, while finite Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) data are incorporated through boundary losses. Using synthetic data from a finite-difference forward solver, we evaluate the method on conductivity fields with inclusions, sharp interfaces, smooth profiles, and heterogeneous media. Results show that the framework recovers dominant conductivity structures from finite boundary measurements with relative errors between $3\%-12\%$ approximately. We show that FFE improves the reconstruction of localized sharp features, particularly for inclusions and interfaces, but are not universally optimal, with raw-coordinate networks performing competitively for smoother fields. These results highlight coordinate representations and boundary excitation design as key factors in neural Calderón inversion.

</details>


### [209] [CPAgents: Agentic Composite Phenotype Generation for Cardiac Disease Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28179)
*Zuoou Li,Wenlong Zhao,Kelly Yu,Weitong Zhang,Paul M. Matthews,Wenjia Bai,Bernhard Kainz,Mengyun Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CPAgents，一个基于多智能体协作的迭代式表型组合框架，用于心血管全表型组关联研究（PheWAS），能够从基础影像特征中自动构建和验证可解释的复合表型（如多项式、比值、交互形式），在人群规模心脏影像队列上显著提升了疾病判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 PheWAS 研究依赖预定义的单变量表型或专家手工构建的特征，难以捕捉临床上有意义的非线性效应和跨表型交互作用，限制了关联发现的广度和深度。因此需要一种能够自动发现更强表型-疾病关联的可扩展方法。

Method: 提出 CPAgents 框架，由三个智能体协同工作：(i) Analyst：识别统计病理并提名候选变换；(ii) Proposer：在医学和统计动机约束下、遵循数值安全规则生成复合表型表达式；(iii) Verifier：使用多阶段标准评估候选表型，并为接受的表型生成透明的证据链。整个过程迭代进行。

Result: 在人群规模心脏影像队列上，发现的复合表型显著提升了疾病判别效果：在 72 种分类器-疾病-指标组合中，CPAgents 变体在 56 个组合中排名第一，基线方法仅 18 个，且在所有九类临床疾病类别中均有提升。生成的表型公式紧凑且临床可解释。

Conclusion: CPAgents 能够超越专家驱动的特征选择，实现可扩展、透明、可解释的复合表型发现，显著增强表型-疾病关联的识别能力，为大规模心血管研究提供有力工具。

Abstract: Identifying robust associations between cardiac imaging phenotypes and clinical diseases is fundamental to population-scale cardiovascular research and reliable risk stratification. However, current phenome-wide association studies rely on pre-defined, single-variable phenotypes or expert-crafted features, which limits their ability to capture clinically meaningful non-linear effects and cross-phenotype interactions. To address this, we propose CPAgents, an iterative phenotype-Composition framework for cardiovascular Phenome-wide association study (PheWAS) that automatically constructs and validates interpretable composite phenotypes (e.g., polynomial, ratio, and interaction forms) from base imaging features. Specifically, our system coordinates three agents: (i) an Analyst that identifies statistical pathologies and nominates candidate transformations; (ii) a Proposer that generates constrained, medically and statistically motivated expressions under numerical safety rules; and (iii) a Verifier that evaluates candidates using multi-stage criteria and produces transparent evidence trails for accepted phenotypes. Evaluated on a population-scale cardiac imaging cohort, the discovered composite phenotypes markedly improve disease discrimination: across 72 classifier-disease-metric combinations, our variants achieve the top rank in 56 cases versus 18 for baselines, with gains observed across all nine clinical disease categories. Our framework yields compact, clinically interpretable phenotype formulas with transparent evidence trails, enabling scalable discovery of stronger phenotype-disease associations beyond expert-driven feature selection.

</details>


### [210] [LLawCo: Learning Laws of Cooperation for Modeling Embodied Multi-Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28182)
*Qinhong Zhou,Chuang Gan,Anoop Cherian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLawCo 框架让具身智能体从过去的失败中反思并提取高层次的行为法则（如"必要时才说话""等待伙伴"），通过监督微调将这些法则融入推理链，从而显著提升多智能体协作的效率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的具身智能体在去中心化、部分可观测环境中，常表现出与伙伴行为不一致或与环境状态不协调的问题，导致协作效率低下、任务成功率不高。亟需一种方法使智能体能够自主地与伙伴及任务目标对齐。

Method: 提出 LLawCo（Learning Laws of Cooperation）框架：(1) 让智能体反思过去失败经验，提取出"misaligned behavioral patterns"；(2) 从这些模式中归纳出高层次的行为法则（如"Talk when necessary""Wait for partner"）；(3) 通过监督微调（SFT），将这些法则显式地融入智能体的思维链（Chain of Thought）中，使推理过程与任务需求和伙伴行为对齐。此外，构建了基于 PARTNR 环境的大规模多智能体沟通协作规划基准 PARTNR-Dialog。

Result: 在四个骨干 LLM 上，LLawCo 相比当前最优的开源沟通智能体框架，在 PARTNR-Dialog 基准上平均成功率提升 4.5%，在 TDW-MAT 基准上平均提升 6.8%。

Conclusion: LLawCo 通过从失败中学习协作法则并融入思维链，有效解决了具身多智能体协作中的行为不对齐问题，显著提升了协作效率和任务成功率，展现出良好的跨任务和跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in decentralized and partially observable environments have attracted growing attention in recent years. However, existing large language model (LLM)-based agents often exhibit behaviors that are misaligned with their partners or inconsistent with the environment state, leading to inefficient cooperation and poor task success. To address this challenge, we propose a novel framework, Learning Laws of Cooperation (LLawCo), that enables embodied agents to autonomously align with both their partners and task objectives. Our framework allows agents to reflect on past failures to extract misaligned behavioral patterns, which are used to derive high-level behavioral laws, such as "Talk when necessary" and "Wait for partner." These laws are explicitly incorporated into the agents' chains of thought via supervised fine-tuning, aligning their reasoning with task requirements and the behavior of other agents. To evaluate our approach, we introduce PARTNR-Dialog, a large-scale multi-agent communicative and cooperative planning benchmark built on the PARTNR environment. Experiments on existing tasks and our new benchmark demonstrate significant improvements in cooperative efficiency and task success rates. Across four backbone LLMs, our method achieves average success rate improvements of 4.5% on the PARTNR-Dialog benchmark and 6.8% on the TDW-MAT benchmark over state-of-the-art open-source communicative agent frameworks. See the LLawCo project page for details: https://www.merl.com/research/highlights/LLawCo

</details>


### [211] [The Remittance Blueprint: Data-driven Intelligence for Sri Lanka](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28190)
*Dhinanjaya Fernando,Dinura Ginige,Kalana Lakshan,Chanupa Gurusinghe,Lasana Pahanga,Subavarshana Arumugam,Sandeepa Weerasekara,Sandareka Wickramanayake,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了斯里兰卡32年（1994-2025）的移民与汇款数据，发现汇款流入主要由外部宏观经济变量（汇率、全球油价）驱动，机器学习模型预测精度远超传统时间序列方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡作为高度依赖汇款的发展中经济体，亟需深入理解汇款流入的驱动因素，以制定有效的经济政策。现有研究在方法论上存在局限，缺乏将传统计量经济学与机器学习相结合的系统性长期分析框架。

Method: 构建384个月调和数据集，综合运用：(1) 探索性数据分析；(2) 平稳性检验（ADF）与协整分析（Johansen）；(3) VAR/VECM时间序列建模与脉冲响应分析；(4) 监督学习模型（Ridge回归等），并以SARIMA为基准进行预测精度对比（年化RMSE）。

Result: （1）汇款流入主要由外部宏观变量驱动——汇率动态和全球油价，而非国内指标；（2）脉冲响应分析证实货币贬值和油价冲击存在非对称效应；（3）Ridge回归预测精度较SARIMA提升73.8%，年化RMSE仅4.948亿美元；（4）稳定条件下，2026年汇款预测值为90.01亿美元。

Conclusion: 斯里兰卡汇款对全球经济存在结构性依赖，政策制定者应着力于稳健的汇率政策、技能型移民引导和正规金融渠道建设，以增强长期经济韧性。

Abstract: This study analyzes Sri Lankan migration and remittances over 32 years (1994-2025). Using a 384-month harmonized dataset, we apply exploratory data analysis, stationarity corrected time-series modeling (ADF, Johansen, VAR/VECM), and supervised learning. Results reveal remittance inflows are primarily driven by external macroeconomic variables, specifically exchange rate dynamics and global oil prices, rather than domestic indicators. Impulse response analysis confirms the asymmetric impact of currency depreciation and oil price shocks. Predictively, multivariate machine learning models outperform traditional univariate approaches; Ridge Regression achieves a 73.8% accuracy improvement over SARIMA (Annualized RMSE: USD 494.8 Mn). The optimized framework projects 2026 remittances at USD 9,001 million under stable conditions. These findings highlight the structural dependence of remittances on global economies, emphasizing the need for robust exchange rate policies, skilled migration, and formal financial channels to enhance long-term economic resilience.

</details>


### [212] [COCOLogic-V2: Identifying Logical Inconsistencies via Truly Hard-Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28194)
*David Steinmann,Antonia Wüst,Kristian Kersting,Wolfgang Stammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COCOLogic-V2是一个面向真实图像视觉归纳推理的对象中心数据集，覆盖一阶逻辑的广泛子集，通过细粒度样本分类（正样本、近边界负样本、远边界负样本）诊断模型问责性，揭示现有模型在近边界样本上的失败及视觉归纳推理仍为开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释模型（如概念瓶颈模型和程序合成方法）的评估多局限于简单任务，对真实世界图像上的复杂推理缺乏探索，亟需一个能够细粒度诊断模型问责性的基准数据集。

Method: 构建COCOLogic-V2数据集，将样本分为正样本（positive）、近边界负样本（near-boundary NB）和远边界负样本（far-from-boundary FB）三类，覆盖一阶逻辑的广泛子集，用于评估视觉归纳推理中的模型表现，并在少样本设置下分析感知噪声和大规则搜索空间的影响。

Result: 模型通常能较好区分正样本和远边界负样本，但在近边界负样本上表现失败；感知噪声和大规则诱导的搜索空间在少样本设置下带来额外挑战。

Conclusion: 视觉归纳推理仍是一个开放挑战，COCOLogic-V2为推进该方向的方法研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: While interpretable models such as concept bottleneck models (CBMs) and program synthesis methods enable verification of model decisions, their evaluation is typically limited to simple tasks, leaving complex reasoning on real-world images largely unexplored. We introduce COCOLogic-V2, an object-centric dataset for visual inductive reasoning on real-world images covering a broad subset of first-order logic. By categorizing samples into positive variants, near-boundary (NB), and far-from-boundary (FB) negatives, COCOLogic-V2 enables fine-grained diagnosis of model accountability. Our evaluations show that models tend to separate positive and FB samples well but fail on NB samples, while perceptual noise and large rule-induced search spaces pose additional challenges in few-shot settings. Together, these results highlight that visual inductive reasoning remains an open challenge and COCOLogic-V2 provides a concrete foundation for advancing methods in this direction.

</details>


### [213] [Towards Value-Constrained Credit Assignment in Fully Delegated AI Cooperatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28217)
*Young Yoon,Jimin Kim,Soyeon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于遍历学习（Traversal Learning）的奖励分配框架，用于完全委托的AI合作社场景。核心思路是通过价值条件化梯度过滤，仅对符合各委托人价值档案的更新进行奖励，利用去中心化梯度路径实现比联邦学习更精细的贡献归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在完全委托的AI合作社中，人类通过代理贡献数据并参与模型更新，但不同委托人存在异质性价值约束。现有联邦学习聚合式方法难以精细溯源每个参与者的贡献，导致奖励分配缺乏公正性和透明度。需要一种能够在去中心化训练中精确归因边际贡献的分配机制。

Method: 以遍历学习（TL）为底层训练范式，利用其保留显式遍历路径和梯度路径的特性，实现去中心化反向传播。在此基础上构建三个核心机制：(1) 价值条件化梯度过滤——对照各委托人的价值档案筛选可接受的更新；(2) 在线边际贡献信号——实时追踪每次更新的边际贡献；(3) 累积收益结算——将贡献信号转化为最终奖励分配。

Result: 论文将该框架与四个相关方向进行了对比定位：数据估值、联邦贡献估计、个性化联邦学习以及多元对齐，论证了该框架在归因精细度和价值对齐方面的优势。

Conclusion: 遍历学习为去中心化协作训练提供了比FedAvg式联邦学习更精细的归因基底，结合价值条件化过滤和在线边际贡献信号，能够实现更公正透明的奖励分配，适用于异质性价值约束下的AI合作社场景。

Abstract: We propose a framework for reward allocation in fully delegated AI cooperatives where humans are represented by agents that contribute data and participate in model updates under heterogeneous value constraints. The key idea is to credit only those updates that remain admissible after screening them against each principal's value profile. We formulate value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate. TL is especially attractive here because it performs decentralized backpropagation without the quality loss associated with aggregation-centric distributed learning and, we argue, offers a finer attribution substrate than FedAvg-style federated learning by preserving explicit traversal and gradient paths. The framework is positioned against data valuation, federated contribution estimation, personalized federated learning, and pluralistic alignment.

</details>


### [214] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Transfer Learning for State Estimation in Lithium-Ion Batteries using the Single Particle Model with Electrolyte](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28220)
*Gift Modekwe,Qiugang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单粒子电解质模型（SPMe）的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）迁移学习框架。通过预训练学习通用电化学动力学，再将权重迁移至目标电池进行微调，该方法在保持电化学一致性的同时，显著减少了训练时间并实现了跨电池的高效泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs从零开始针对不同电池化学体系或工况进行训练，过程耗时长、收敛慢。需要一种能够复用已有知识、加速训练并保证物理一致性的方法。

Method: 提出SPMe-PINNs的迁移学习框架：首先对模型进行预训练以学习通用电化学动力学；然后通过权重迁移、冻结选定网络层、微调剩余参数（包括关键电化学变量的估计），将模型适配到目标电池。

Result: 利用PyBaMM进行验证，结果表明该方法能准确预测电压，保持了电化学一致性，同时缩短了训练时间，实现了不同电池间的高效泛化。

Conclusion: 迁移学习可有效克服SPMe-PINNs训练成本高、收敛慢的问题，在保证物理一致性的前提下实现快速适配与泛化，为锂离子电池电化学建模提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs), including battery electrochemical models. They typically en-force conservation laws within the loss function to ensure physically consistent solutions. Tradi-tional numerical methods such as finite difference, finite volume, and finite element techniques, re-ly on discretization and can be computationally expensive for nonlinear systems. To address this challenge, PINNs offer improved scalability, particularly for reduced-order models like the single particle model with electrolyte (SPMe). The SPMe describes lithium-ion battery dynamics through coupled diffusion, transport, reaction kinetics, and voltage equations. Despite these advantages, training SPMe-based PINNs from scratch for different battery chemistries or operating conditions is demanding and often leads to slow convergence. To overcome this limitation, this work introduces a transfer learning framework for SPMe-PINNs. The model is first pretrained to learn general elec-trochemical dynamics and then adapted to a target battery by transferring weights, freezing se-lected layers, and fine tuning the remaining parameters, including estimating key electrochemical variables. Validation using PyBaMM demonstrates accurate voltage prediction, indicating that the proposed approach preserves electrochemical consistency while reducing training time and ena-bling efficient generalization across batteries.

</details>


### [215] [Estimation--Prediction Tradeoff in Causal Probabilistic Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28225)
*Aniq Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示了时序链接预测中参数估计与预测精度之间的内在权衡：最大化Fisher信息以改善参数恢复的条件同时也导致最高熵，使个体预测本质上更困难。作者提出一个概率因果框架来生成具有已知因果结构的时序图，并推导了Cramér-Rao界以验证这一权衡，主张评估应区分可约模型误差与内在过程不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时序链接预测通常仅以对未见边的预测性能作为评估标准，但在概率时序图中，这一标准可能将模型误差与不可约的内在不确定性混为一谈，导致无法判断模型是否真正学到了底层因果机制。

Method: 1. 在二值logistic模型框架下，刻画了参数估计（Fisher信息量）与预测（熵）之间的权衡关系；2. 提出概率因果框架生成具有瞬态边和已知真实因果结构的时序图；3. 针对二值logistic参数化，推导Cramér-Rao下界，从理论上量化参数估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡。

Result: 验证了参数估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡：最大化Fisher信息以改善参数可恢复性的参数区域，同时也是熵最高的区域，即便在完美参数恢复条件下，个体预测也因高内在不确定性而更加困难。

Conclusion: 仅靠预测准确率不能反映模型是否学到了底层因果机制。应建立能区分可约模型误差与内在过程不确定性的基准评估方法。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is usually evaluated by predictive performance on unseen edges, but in probabilistic temporal graphs this criterion can conflate model error with irreducible uncertainty. We study this issue by characterising an inherent estimation--prediction tradeoff in binary logistic models where regimes that maximise Fisher information and improve parameter recoverability are also those with the highest entropy, making individual predictions intrinsically harder even under perfect parameter recovery. We propose a probabilistic causal framework for generating temporal graphs with transient edges and known ground-truth causal structure, allowing temporal link prediction to be evaluated jointly with causal parameter recovery. For the proposed binary logistic parametrisation, we derive the Cramér--Rao bound and validate the tradeoff between parameter estimation error and irreducible predictive loss. Our results show that predictive accuracy alone may not reflect whether a model has learned the underlying causal mechanism, motivating benchmarks that distinguish reducible model error from intrinsic process uncertainty.

</details>


### [216] [Parameter Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT) for Network Traffic Prediction via Dynamic Urban Congestion Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28274)
*Abdolazim Rezaei,Mehdi Sookhak,Mahboobeh Haghparast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEHT 是一种面向蜂窝网络流量预测的框架，通过将城市移动性和拥塞信息融入 Transformer 架构，并利用 LoRA 降低可训练参数量，结合多模态融合策略，在保持高精度的同时实现了参数高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络流量预测受复杂移动模式、拥塞动态和异构用户行为影响，现有方法难以有效整合这些外部信息，且模型参数量大、训练成本高。

Method: 提出 PEHT 框架：(1) 将主网络通信特征与辅助城市移动特征分离；(2) 在 Transformer 编码器中引入 LoRA 以减少可训练参数；(3) 通过多模态融合策略将外部移动和拥塞特征注入解码器，提升预测性能。

Result: 在 Telecom Italia Milan 数据集及多个合成拥塞场景上的实验表明，PEHT 在 RMSE、MAE 和 R² 指标上均优于当前最优基线方法。

Conclusion: PEHT 通过参数高效的混合 Transformer 架构，成功融合移动性和拥塞等外部信息，实现了准确且高效的蜂窝网络流量预测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate network traffic prediction is a critical element for efficient resource allocation in dynamic urban cellular networks. However, prediction remains challenging because network demand is influenced by complex mobility patterns, congestion dynamics, and heterogeneous user behavior. This paper introduces the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT), a network traffic prediction framework that integrates urban mobility and congestion information into a Transformer-based architecture. PEHT separates primary network communication features from secondary urban mobility features and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into the Transformer encoder to reduce the number of trainable parameters while maintaining high predictive accuracy. A multimodal fusion strategy then injects external mobility and congestion features into the decoder to improve traffic forecasting. Experiments on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and multiple synthetic congestion scenarios show that PEHT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of RMSE, MAE, and $R^2$. The implementation is available in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [217] [Democratic ICAI: Debating Our Way to Steering Principles from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28294)
*Kevin Kingslin,Anish Natekar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Savita Bhat,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Democratic ICAI通过结构化角色辩论收集多种竞争性理由，生成更全面的偏好原则，从而更忠实地建模人类偏好结构，在创意偏好基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的对齐方法难以捕捉人类判断背后的推理过程。Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) 虽然能将偏好总结为自然语言原则，但其单次解释无法充分反映复杂决策中的细微差别。因此需要一种能获取更丰富、更具竞争性理由信号的方法。

Method: 提出Democratic ICAI：通过结构化角色辩论（structured persona debate）收集多种相互竞争的理由，从这些更丰富的信号中推导出更清晰、更全面的引导原则（steering principles），并将其用于指导LLM和决策树两种判断器的决策建模。

Result: 在MuCE-Pref和LiTBench等多个创意偏好基准上进行实验，Democratic ICAI相比深思式提示（deliberative prompting）和基于原则的基线方法，在多项创意任务类别中提升了平均偏好预测准确率，且生成的宪法（constitutions）更受LLM标注者青睐。

Conclusion: Democratic ICAI通过引入多角色竞争性辩论机制，显著提升了对人类偏好结构的建模忠实度，证明了多元理由聚合在偏好对齐中的有效性。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment often struggles to capture the reasoning that underlies human judgments. Many evaluations rely on multiple interacting criteria, yet pairwise labels reveal only the final choice rather than the considerations that shape preferences. Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) improves interpretability in decision making by summarizing preferences into natural-language principles, but its single-pass explanations miss much of the nuance involved in complex decisions. We introduce Democratic ICAI, a novel approach that gathers multiple competing rationales through structured persona debate, offering a broader and more expressive account of the factors influencing each comparison. From these richer signals, we derive clearer and more comprehensive steering principles and use them to guide decision modeling through both LLM-based and decision-tree judges. Experiments on creative preference benchmarks, MuCE-Pref and LiTBench, across multiple creative task categories show that Democratic ICAI yields a more faithful preference structure. It improves average preference prediction across tasks relative to deliberative prompting and principle-based baselines, while producing constitutions that LLM annotators prefer.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Directed Graph Topology Inference via Graph Filter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27455)
*Rasoul Shafipour,Andrei Buciulea,Santiago Segarra,Antonio G. Marques,Gonzalo Mateos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究从线性扩散动力学产生的节点测量数据中推断有向网络拓扑的问题，提出了一种联合识别图滤波器与图拓扑的交替优化算法，在有向图和非白激励的挑战性场景下实现了网络推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图信号处理的网络推断方法大多假设无向图和白噪声激励，使得图移位算子和观测协方差矩阵可同时对角化。然而现实场景中网络往往是有向的，且输入信号的节点分量可能任意相关，导致两者不可同时对角化，因此需要新的方法来解决这一更具挑战性的有向图推断问题。

Method: 观测信号建模为图卷积滤波器（未知系数的多项式）在未知有向图移位算子上、由任意相关节点分量的独立图信号激励的输出。方法分两步：(1) 利用输出信号测量和输入先验统计信息，通过求解带有Stiefel流形约束的二次矩阵方程组来识别扩散滤波器，在输入协方差的谱多样性假设下可辨识；(2) 在给定滤波器估计后，寻找一个稀疏且结构上容许的、与该滤波器可交换的移位算子，强制滤波器成为图移位算子的多项式，从而恢复网络拓扑。进一步提出联合交替优化算法，使两步相互增强，提升样本复杂度。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提算法在合成有向图和真实数据案例中的有效性，并展示了其在城市交通流动性分析和投资组合优化中的潜在应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文突破了传统方法对无向图和白噪声激励的限制，在有向图和非白激励条件下建立了图滤波器和拓扑的联合辨识框架，提出的交替优化算法具有良好的恢复性能，并成功应用于城市交通和金融投资组合等实际问题。

Abstract: We address the problem of inferring a directed network from nodal measurements generated by linear diffusion dynamics on the sought graph. Observations are modeled as the outputs of a graph convolutional filter, i.e., a polynomial (with unknown coefficients) of a local diffusion graph-shift operator encoding the latent graph topology, excited with an ensemble of independent graph signals with arbitrarily-correlated nodal components. Unlike prior efforts that considered undirected graphs and white signal excitations, here the graph-shift operator and the observations' covariance matrix are not simultaneously diagonalizable. In this challenging context, we first rely on measurements of the output signals along with prior statistical information on the inputs to identify the diffusion filter. Such system identification problem involves solving a system of quadratic matrix equations, which we show is identifiable under spectral-diversity assumptions on the input covariances. For algorithmic purposes we recast it as a smooth quadratic minimization subject to Stiefel manifold constraints. Subsequent identification of the network topology given the graph filter estimate boils down to finding a sparse and structurally admissible shift that commutes with the given filter, thus, forcing the latter to be a polynomial in the sought graph-shift operator. A joint graph filter and topology identification algorithm is also proposed, which alternates between the aforementioned steps in a mutually reinforcing fashion to offer improved sample complexity. Numerical tests corroborate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in recovering synthetic digraphs and real-data case studies, and illustrate their potential utility on urban mobility analyses as well as portfolio optimization.

</details>


### [219] [The Decision Geometry of Covariance Estimation for the Global Minimum-Variance Portfolio under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27462)
*Xavier Fonseca*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了全局最小方差投资组合(GMVP)中协方差估计误差到决策次优性的精确映射关系，证明了决策遗憾仅依赖于估计误差在组合权重上的投影，并受组合集中度和真实协方差条件数的影响，揭示了GMVP遗憾对(p-1)维误差投影的不变性，并将该框架应用于重尾收益情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍采用矩阵范数损失来评估协方差估计量，但这并非投资组合决策所依赖的目标函数。作者旨在填补协方差估计误差如何转化为GMVP决策次优性这一理论空白，为"决策聚焦"方法提供精确的估计几何和一致性理论。

Method: 推导精确的遗憾恒等式和非渐近界，将决策遗憾分解为估计误差在组合权重上的作用，并证明其受组合集中度和真实协方差条件数缩放。利用投影几何刻画GMVP遗憾的不变性结构，随后将框架应用于尾部指数κ∈(2,4)的重尾收益，推导算子范数速率隐含的遗憾收敛速率，并在偏t/t-Copula模拟设计中进行预注册分析验证。

Result: 证明了GMVP遗憾对(p-1)维误差投影的不变性，协方差尺度方向不变性为其特例。决策聚焦的优势体现为更尖锐的常数和集中度折扣，而非更快的速率；报告了速率预测在高度不良条件下的诚实边界。

Conclusion: 该工作为近期决策聚焦学习方法提供了它们所欠缺的精确估计几何和一致性理论，表明在投资组合优化中评估协方差估计量应转向决策聚焦的评价准则，而非传统的矩阵范数损失。

Abstract: The global minimum-variance portfolio (GMVP) is the canonical decision built from an estimated covariance matrix, yet covariance estimators are universally evaluated by matrix-norm loss, which is not the object the decision depends on. We characterise exactly how covariance-estimation error maps into GMVP suboptimality. We prove an exact regret identity and a non-asymptotic bound showing decision regret depends on the estimation error only through its action on the portfolio weights, scaled by portfolio concentration and the conditioning of the true covariance. From this we derive the decision geometry: GMVP regret is invariant to a (p-1)-dimensional projection of the p^2-dimensional error matrix, with invariance to the covariance-scale direction as an exact special case. We then apply the framework to heavy-tailed returns (tail index kappa in (2,4)), establishing the regret convergence rate implied by the centred operator-norm rate, and confirm the theory on a skew-t/t-copula simulation design with pre-registered analysis. The decision-focused advantage is a sharper constant and a concentration discount rather than a faster rate; we report an honest high-conditioning boundary of the rate prediction. The results complement recent decision-focused learning approaches by supplying the exact estimation geometry and consistency theory they lack.

</details>


### [220] [Adversarial Contamination Meets Hard Thresholding: An Iterative Algorithm with Signal Adaptivity and Minimax Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27685)
*Shixiang Liu,Hanming Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段对抗性污染抵抗迭代硬阈值（AC-IHT）算法，用于高维回归中数据污染场景下的鲁棒估计。该非凸算法通过不同阈值的迭代更新系数向量和污染向量，实现了 minimax 近最优估计，并具有信号自适应性和强神谕性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际高维回归问题中，数据普遍存在测量误差、异常值或对抗性污染，传统方法缺乏足够的鲁棒性。因此需要开发既能抵抗污染、又能实现最优估计和推断的鲁棒统计方法。

Method: 提出两阶段AC-IHT非凸算法：第一阶段通过迭代硬阈值交替更新系数向量与污染向量，两者采用不同的阈值尺度；第二阶段利用信号条件进一步精炼估计。算法不依赖凸松弛，直接处理非凸优化问题。

Result: AC-IHT估计量达到minimax近最优估计率（至多对数因子差距）；在适当信号条件下自适应获得更锐利的估计率和更准确的支撑恢复；具备强神谕性质，为渐近推断奠定理论基础。数值实验验证了有限样本下的优越性，方法可扩展至广义线性模型和重尾噪声场景。

Conclusion: AC-IHT算法在抵抗对抗性污染的同时实现了统计最优性、信号自适应性和强神谕性质，兼具理论保证与实用价值，并可推广到更广泛的模型与噪声设定中。

Abstract: Pervasive data contamination -- stemming from measurement errors, outliers, or adversarial corruption -- has motivated the development of robust statistical methods. In this context, we propose a two-stage Adversarial Contamination-resistant Iterative Hard Thresholding (AC-IHT) algorithm for high-dimensional regression with contamination. Our nonconvex algorithm achieves minimax near-optimal (up to logarithmic terms) estimation by iteratively updating the coefficient vector and the contamination vector with different thresholding scales. We further demonstrate that our AC-IHT estimator is signal-adaptive: under proper signal conditions, it adaptively attains a sharper estimation rate and more accurate support recovery. Moreover, it enjoys the strong oracle property, laying a theoretical foundation for asymptotic inference. Numerical experiments confirm its superior finite-sample performance. Finally, we discuss theoretical extensions of the proposed procedure to generalized linear models and to heavy-tailed noise settings.

</details>


### [221] [Local Fokker--Planck Geometry for Score Estimation: Heat-Ball Mean-Value Representations and Exact High-Dimensional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27954)
*Jiayao Bai,Lang Deng,Yi Du,Yifei Jia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种局部Fokker-Planck几何框架，用于估计非线性扩散过程的得分函数。通过引入累积方差坐标变换，将变系数Fokker-Planck方程转化为标准热方程，并利用热球单调性方法导出得分函数、密度、对数密度和熵密度的精确局部均值表示。同时引入κ-测度及其因子化采样器以实现高维蒙特卡洛计算，并证明该局部框架是全局FP残差方法的单参数推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典得分估计方法仅在线性漂移下有闭式解；对于一般的非线性状态依赖漂移，现有方法（如去噪得分匹配DSM和全局Fokker-Planck残差惩罚）采用全局平均，在低密度区域估计误差显著膨胀，而低密度区域恰恰是精度最关键的地方。因此需要一种局部化方法来克服此局限。

Method: 1) 通过时间变换至累积方差坐标，将变系数Fokker-Planck方程化为标准非齐次热方程；2) 推广Evans的热球单调性方法，建立得分函数及其关联量的精确局部均值表示，并在显式的维度依赖漂移预算下证明局部适定性；3) 引入κ-测度，推导其精确因子化采样器（单位样本权重、χ²₂径向集中），用于高维热球积分的蒙特卡洛计算；4) 取r→0极限，证明热球残差逐点恢复Fokker-Planck残差，揭示局部框架是全局FP残差方法的单参数推广，且DSM总体极小化解在任意尺度下对热球约束可行。

Result: 在二维结构化数据、256维MNIST数据以及专用采样器研究上验证了框架的有效性，并确认了浓度定律。

Conclusion: 所提出的局部Fokker-Planck几何框架通过局域抛物线平均替代全局条件化，克服了非线性扩散下现有得分估计方法在低密度区域精度不足的问题，且统一了全局FP残差方法与点估计视角，为基于得分的生成模型提供了更精确的理论与算法基础。

Abstract: Score-based generative models and Langevin samplers rely on estimating the score function $\nabla_x\log p_t(x)$ of a forward diffusion. Classically this is tractable when the drift is linear: the marginal density is Gaussian and the score is a global conditional expectation. For a general nonlinear, state-dependent drift the marginal density has no closed form, and existing methods--denoising score matching and global Fokker--Planck residual penalties--resort to global averaging that inflates estimation error in low-density regions precisely where accuracy is most critical. We address this by developing a local Fokker--Planck geometric framework that replaces global conditioning with local parabolic averaging. Our approach rests on three contributions. First, a time change to the cumulative-variance coordinate reduces the variable-coefficient Fokker--Planck equation to a standard inhomogeneous heat equation, on which we extend Evans' classical heat-ball monotonicity method to derive exact local mean-value representations for the score $\nabla_x\log p$ together with the density, log-density, and entropy density; local well-posedness is established under an explicit dimension-dependent drift budget. Second, for high-dimensional Monte Carlo evaluation of the resulting heat-ball integrals, we introduce the $κ$-measure and derive its exact factorized sampler with unit per-sample weight, $χ^2_2$ radial concentration. Third, the $r\to0$ limit of the heat-ball residual recovers the pointwise Fokker--Planck residual, showing that the local framework is a one-parameter generalization of global FP-residual methods, and that the DSM population minimizer is feasible for the heat-ball constraint at every scale. We validate the framework on 2D structured data on 256-dimensional MNIST, and on a dedicated sampler study confirming the concentration laws.

</details>


### [222] [Surprises in Proper Positive-Only Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28309)
*Shai Ben-David,Farnam Mansouri,Anay Mehrotra,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了长期未决的正样本仅学习（positive-only learning）中proper学习的刻画问题：一个概念类可被properly PAC学习，当且仅当其具有有限VC维且满足一个称为"均匀外部可分性"（uniform exterior separability）的新组合条件。该结果揭示了与标准PAC学习截然不同的丰富图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正样本仅学习的PAC模型可追溯至Natarajan (1987)，其中improper学习的刻画早已为人熟知，但proper学习的组合刻画却长期悬而未决。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，彻底解决proper positive-only学习的可学习性条件问题。

Method: 作者引入了一个新的组合条件——均匀外部可分性（uniform exterior separability），并证明了该条件与有限VC维共同构成proper正样本仅学习的充要条件。此外，还引入了若干新的组合维度（combinatorial dimensions）作为分析工具，并通过多个分离结果（separation results）展示不同学习范式之间的差异。

Result: 完整刻画了proper正样本仅学习：充要条件是有限VC维+均匀外部可分性。进一步揭示了：(1) proper学习与improper学习被分离；(2) 随机化proper学习与确定性proper学习被分离；(3) 存在某些类上ERM不是学习器；(4) 有限VC维甚至不足以保证非均匀学习（non-uniform learning）。

Conclusion: 正样本仅学习的proper可学习性具有比标准PAC学习显著更复杂的理论图景。均匀外部可分性这一新组合条件精确刻画了proper学习的边界，而新引入的组合维度有望对更广泛的学习理论问题产生价值。

Abstract: Binary classification from positive-only samples is a variant of PAC learning in which the learner receives i.i.d. samples from the positive region of an unknown target concept, but is evaluated under the original distribution (which places mass on both positive and negative regions). This model dates back to Natarajan [1987, STOC], and the characterization of improper learning is well-known -- it even appears in textbooks. The characterization of proper positive-only learning, however, has long remained open. In this work, we revisit and settle this question: a concept class is properly learnable from positive-only samples if and only if it has finite VC dimension and satisfies a new combinatorial condition, which we call uniform exterior separability. Together with several separation results, this characterization reveals a surprisingly rich landscape that differs sharply from standard PAC learning: proper and improper learning are separated, randomized and deterministic proper learning are separated, there are classes for which no ERM is a learner, and finite VC dimension does not suffice even for non-uniform learning. Along the way, we introduce new combinatorial dimensions that we believe can be of broader interest in learning theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“世界人工智能模型网络（AI-ModelNet）”的概念、愿景与体系架构，借鉴互联网发展思路，旨在通过建立模型之间的通路，实现异构模型的互联互通、能力共享与协同推理，以解决大模型训练成本高、部署复杂及异质模型间协作困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型实际应用受限于高昂的训练成本与部署复杂性，推动领域向轻量化、私有化、领域专用模型转型。然而，异构模型的广泛分布导致它们之间有效交互与协作成为关键瓶颈，亟需一种新的范式来解决这一问题。

Method: 借鉴互联网发展的理念，提出AI-ModelNet的概念、愿景与分层体系架构；通过原型系统与多样化应用案例验证框架的可行性，并初步探讨未来研究方向。

Result: 构建了AI-ModelNet的分层架构原型系统，并在多种应用案例中验证了该框架的可行性。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet作为一种实现模型互联互通与协作推理的新型范式，具备解决异质模型协同瓶颈的潜力，为未来人工智能模型网络化发展提供了参考方向。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [224] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多智能体系统中，通过操纵大语言模型（LLM）的人格特质（特别是宜人性高低）对团队任务表现的影响。研究发现，人格效应高度依赖于任务结构：在结构化编码任务中，低宜人性导致沟通风格显著变化但对里程碑完成影响甚微；而在开放式协作和竞争性谈判任务中，同样的操纵会显著降低表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已表明人格提示（personality prompting）会改变LLM的沟通风格（如低宜人性产生对抗性语言，高宜人性带来合作行为），但沟通风格转变与客观任务结果之间的关系尚未在多个领域被系统考察。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索人格组合是否影响多智能体团队的实际表现。

Method: 作者选取前沿LLM，在三种不同任务领域（结构化编码、开放式研究协作、竞争性谈判）中操纵智能体的人格特质（主要围绕宜人性维度），观察沟通行为变化与任务完成效果之间的关系，进行跨领域系统比较。

Result: 人格效应严重依赖于任务结构：（1）编码任务中，低宜人性引发大幅沟通转变，但对里程碑完成几乎没有影响；（2）开放式协作和谈判任务中，同样的低宜人性操纵显著降低了表现。

Conclusion: 人格操纵的效果并非普适，而是受任务结构调节。这对多智能体系统设计具有重要启示：不能简单套用单一人格策略，需根据任务类型审慎选择智能体的人格配置，并认识到人格操纵的局限性。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [225] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体在长期任务中缺乏前瞻性规划能力的问题，提出通过训练单一自回归模型来内部化未来感知规划（文本化的Q值模拟）。核心发现是存在“格式-能力鸿沟”：仅通过监督微调会让模型表面模仿而缺乏真正的预测基础。为此，作者设计了三阶段训练范式：世界模型智能体中期训练（WM-AMT）注入潜在预测能力、格式引导SFT（FE-SFT）结构化该能力、前瞻条件强化学习（FC-RL）校准模拟效用。在搜索和数学推理任务上，该方法一致优于其他基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在序列决策中表现强大但本质上是反应式的，缺乏人类那种“what-if”先验推演能力——即缺乏内部世界模型来模拟未来结果。现有后训练方案仅对前瞻轨迹做微调会导致表面模仿，缺乏真正的预测基础（格式-能力鸿沟）。

Method: 提出三阶段训练范式：(1) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT)：在中期训练阶段注入潜在的世界模型预测能力；(2) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT)：通过监督微调将注入的能力结构化为显式的“状态前瞻滚动 + 计划条件成功估计”（文本化Q值）；(3) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL)：利用强化学习进一步校准生成模拟的校准度和决策效用。

Result: 在搜索任务和数学推理任务上进行评估，所提出的三阶段训练方法在各项指标上一致优于其他训练基线（包括直接SFT前瞻轨迹等方法），验证了“能力优先”训练管线的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的LLM智能体内部世界建模需要“能力优先”的训练管线：先注入潜在预测能力，再结构化格式，最后通过RL校准。这样方能获得有基础且校准良好的前瞻能力，而非表面的格式模仿。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [226] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ODYSSEY 是一个范畴论框架，通过将基础模型构建为“铸造厂”（foundries）的组合来实现可验证的、局部保真的基础模型。铸造厂是有组织的知识层（sheaf），包含论证组件，通过左右 Kan 扩展进行形式化构造，并使用 FSQL 查询语言与 TICKET 认证机制。该系统已完整实现并在多种场景下得到测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础模型缺乏可验证的、局部可保证的真理维护机制，难以在异构来源中保持一致性、可解释性与可信性。本文旨在提供一个统一的形式化框架，使得基础模型能够通过局部的、可组合的架构件来保证真理的局部保持与全局一致性。

Method: 提出 ODYSSEY 范畴论框架：(1) 将铸造厂（foundry）定义为有组织的知识层（sheaf），内部包含论证组件；(2) 具体铸造厂由证据/论证、操作决策、制度/金融、市场语义、科学挑战、研究计划、助理构建、评估工具等通用铸造厂构建；(3) 通用铸造厂学习（UFL）通过左 Kan 扩展将局部工件卷成候选铸造厂，右 Kan 扩展强制实施限制、粘合、障碍与论证条件；(4) FSQL 查询语言用于切片铸造厂工件；(5) TICKET 认证利用因果 Kan 扩展变换器将外部模型纳入 ODYSSEY 持久化状态。

Result: ODYSSEY 已完整实现并在广泛的具体铸造厂中测试，验证了同一范畴论机制能够支持领域构建、工件重放、层诊断、基于 Toulmin 模型/局部 LLM 的审查、残差障碍账本以及异构来源间的因果声明提取等任务。

Conclusion: ODYSSEY 展示了范畴论工具（特别是层论与 Kan 扩展）在构建可验证、可组合的基础模型中的实际可行性，为可信 AI 系统提供了一个统一的形式化架构。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [227] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DysLexLens，一个低资源LLM框架，用于从Reddit论坛帖子中分析阅读障碍学习者使用AI工具的体验。该框架通过词典驱动过滤、知识图谱推理、可验证查询响应生成及多维度评估，实现了从噪声社交媒体数据中提取结构化洞察的端到端流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阅读障碍学习者越来越多地使用AI工具辅助学习，但其真实使用体验缺乏系统性研究。现有的社交媒体数据分析方法在低资源论坛场景下面临噪声大、相关性弱的挑战，且缺乏可追溯、可验证的分析框架。

Method: DysLexLens采用四阶段端到端架构：(1)词典驱动过滤，从Reddit构建聚焦阅读障碍与AI的高质量语料库；(2)LLM语义分析结合知识图谱查询推理，挖掘有意义的模式；(3)使用RAGAS和查询鲁棒性等定量指标评估LLM生成响应；(4)提供结构化定性验证指南，重点评估幻觉和证据对齐。研究使用Reddit数据及30个问题验证框架有效性。

Result: DysLexLens在阅读障碍相关Reddit数据上展现了有效性，能够从噪声帖子中提取高质量洞察，其定量和定性评估方法可有效衡量LLM生成响应的质量，并展示了向其他低资源论坛数据场景泛化的潜力。

Conclusion: DysLexLens为分析低资源论坛中特定群体（如阅读障碍学习者）的AI使用体验提供了可复现、可追溯的端到端框架，其模块化设计具有良好的泛化能力。代码、样本数据及评估结果已开源以支持复现。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [228] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文发现多模态情感识别中显式推理未必提升准确率——"快思考"（直接回答）在召回率上优于"慢思考"（深思推理），而慢思考在精确率上更优。基于此提出MER-R1框架，通过双目标解耦和快慢置信度校准，将召回率与精确率联合优化而非权衡，最终实现推理对情感识别的真正增益，在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于推理的多模态大模型（MLLMs）在情感识别中面临一个反直觉现象：显式推理虽使预测更可解释，但并未转化为更好的准确率——直接给出答案的"快思考"反而常优于经过深思熟虑推理的"慢思考"。论文旨在揭示这一现象背后的机制，并设计方法使推理真正为情感识别带来正向收益。

Method: 提出MER-R1强化学习框架，核心包含两个机制：（1）双目标解耦（Dual-objective Disentanglement），将召回率与精确率分离为两个独立优化信号，实现联合优化而非相互权衡；（2）快慢置信度校准（Slow-fast Confidence Calibration），将慢思考的最终答案与快思考的直觉判断对齐，强化正确情感、抑制错误情感。此外还提供了该方法缓解优化过程中方差诱导干扰的理论证明。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion两个基准数据集上取得最先进（state-of-the-art）性能，验证了MER-R1使推理真正有益于情感识别。

Conclusion: 快思考有利于召回（广泛且高置信度预测），慢思考有利于精确（通过保守过滤错误类别），两者具有互补性。通过MER-R1的双目标解耦与快慢置信度校准，可将这种互补性显式转化为优化目标，使推理对多模态情感识别产生真正增益，而非仅仅提升可解释性。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [229] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于证据树的层次化推理框架ToE（Tree of Evidence），通过强化学习驱动的多源检索代理、证据评估代理和论证树聚合算法，实现可解释的自动化事实核查，在对抗性输入上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假信息快速传播，尤其是生成引擎优化（GEO）下AI生成的对抗性错误信息可被检索系统系统性曝光并污染LLM推理，现有自动事实核查方法对此类对抗性输入的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出ToE框架：(1)将每条声明建模为动态扩展的论证树；(2)使用强化学习驱动的多源检索代理进行证据收集；(3)证据评估代理对检索结果进行可信度评估；(4)论证树聚合算法迭代分解、检索并验证声明，形成可解释的证据链。同时给出检索过程的理论分析，推导出形式化误差界，证明学习策略收敛到信息论最优策略的邻域。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种骨干LLM上的实验表明，ToE相较竞争基线提升4至24个百分点，在对抗性污染输入上的增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: ToE通过层次化证据推理框架有效提升了自动事实核查的准确性和鲁棒性，尤其在对抗性GEO污染场景下表现优越，理论分析也为其检索策略的收敛性提供了保证。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [230] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种符号反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架，通过自然语言映射、符号验证器和计划识别器，提升大语言模型在长时域规划中的鲁棒性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长时域决策任务中常产生不可行或错误的规划方案，其鲁棒性和可靠性存在严重安全隐患，亟需一种机制来提升LLM在复杂规划中的表现。

Method: 设计了三组件协同框架：(1)自然语言提示机制，将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述以捕捉任务约束和语义；(2)符号验证器，识别错误并转化为LLM可理解的修正指令；(3)计划识别器，推断目标可达性以引导规划朝向正确目标。整体采用迭代自优化策略。

Result: 在长时域规划任务上，所提框架在可行性和正确性两个维度均取得持续提升，验证了其对LLM规划可靠性的增强效果。

Conclusion: 符号反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架能有效提升LLM在长时域规划中的鲁棒性与可靠性，为构建更可信的AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [231] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了图世界模型（GWM）中的长期 rollout 误差问题，提出统一框架分析拓扑与模型对误差放大的影响，并设计 Error-Aware GWM 方法防止长期发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多规划环境并非向量或图像，而是由智能体、工具、技能、路线等构成的图。在图世界模型中进行长期 rollout 时，局部预测误差可能局限或沿图传播，尤其当边也被预测时，失败模式会进一步变化。现有研究缺乏对图结构下 rollout 误差传播的系统理论分析。

Method: 提出统一的固定边和动态边 GWM 框架，引入动作节点支持节点级、边级和图级决策；推导图值 rollout 误差界，将拓扑诱导放大与模型诱导放大分离；引入联合节点-边算子处理动态边 rollout；基于分析提出 Error-Aware GWM，结合谱正则化、rollout 一致性和关键节点加权。

Result: 在合成拓扑和异构智能体图测试平台上，rollout 误差与规划遗憾随 horizon 增长；当图结构演化时需要动态边训练；Error-Aware GWM 能防止长期发散同时保持预测精度。真实世界图基准测试表明，GWM 最适用于动态图 rollout 和智能体规划，而专用图模型在静态或稀疏预测任务上仍具优势。

Conclusion: 图世界模型的核心价值在于动态图场景下的长期 rollout 与规划，Error-Aware GWM 通过理论指导的正则化策略有效控制了误差传播，为图结构环境中的基于模型的规划提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [232] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种用于语言智能体的世界模型（基于智能体的柔性推理 vs. 参数化模型的可度量训练），提出GILP框架，将小型参数化骨干与LLM推理结合，通过一致性门控机制将幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035，成功率从0.668提升至0.838。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两类世界模型各有优劣：基于智能体的世界模型灵活但会产生难以度量的幻觉状态变化；参数化世界模型误差可度量（如NodeMSE、delta accuracy）但规划能力弱。需要一种结合二者优势的方法，在保持灵活推理的同时，有效降低幻觉并提升规划可靠性。

Method: 提出Grounded Iterative Language Planning (GILP)：仅训练一个小型参数化骨干网络，负责提供有效动作、预测状态增量、风险和估值；LLM负责草拟动作和想象的状态变化；引入一致性门控（consistency gate），当LLM与参数化骨干的预测不一致时触发修正。整体在四个图结构规划基准上评估，并引入操作化幻觉度量指标。

Result: 在真实GPT-4o-mini调用中，GILP将幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035；在校准模拟器消融实验中，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，且仅增加约22%的额外LLM调用。

Conclusion: GILP成功融合了参数化世界模型的可度量性与LLM的灵活推理能力，通过在两者不一致时进行迭代修正，显著抑制了幻觉并提升了规划成功率，且额外计算开销可控。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [233] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ATOD（Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation），一种混合在线蒸馏算法，通过退火式的OPD-RL调度和轮次级分歧-不确定性重加权（T-DUR）机制，结合行为克隆与强化学习的优势，在长程交互任务上超越了教师模型和现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练小语言模型智能体执行长程交互任务时，在线策略蒸馏（OPD）早期收敛快但性能饱和于教师水平，而强化学习（RL）虽能超越教师但早期学习效率低下。现有方法未能有效结合二者的互补优势。

Method: 提出ATOD算法，包含两个核心设计：(1) 退火式OPD-RL调度策略——训练早期以OPD为主快速逼近教师行为，随后逐步增强RL权重以驱动基于奖励的探索；(2) 轮次级分歧-不确定性重加权（T-DUR）——软性放大高价值轮次，改善长轨迹中的稠密监督信号。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个基准上的实验表明，ATOD在三种学生模型规模下均一致优于基线：相比OPD平均成功率提升3.03个百分点，相比GRPO提升23.62个百分点，并超越对应教师模型2.16个百分点。

Conclusion: ATOD通过退火调度和轮次重加权机制，成功融合了OPD的快速模仿能力与RL的探索优化能力，实现了对教师模型的超越，为小语言模型智能体的后训练提供了有效的混合范式。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [234] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NormAct是一个评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在具身环境中理解和遵循隐含社会规范的基准测试。实验显示，现有顶尖模型在明确目标达成上表现尚可(67.3%)，但在隐含规范遵守上表现很差(26.4%)。作者提出了NormPerceptor方法，通过上下文条件化的提示生成，将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身规划评估通常只关注显式目标达成或直接规范知识，很少评估模型能否在行动序列中推断并应用隐含的社会规范约束。隐含规范在常规任务中无处不在，模型若忽视这些规范，即使在物理层面完成任务，社会层面也可能是失败的。因此需要一个专门评估"目标达成+规范遵守"综合能力的基准。

Method: 作者构建了NormAct基准，将隐含社会规范嵌入日常任务中，评估三个维度：目标达成(Goal Achievement)、规范遵守(Norm Compliance)和整体任务成功(Task Success)。测试了GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3 Pro等顶尖MLLMs。进一步通过线索条件实验(cue-condition experiments)诊断性能差距的根源。最后提出NormPerceptor——一种上下文条件化的线索生成器，在规划前先推断场景相关的规范，再将规范作为约束整合到行动规划中。

Result: 实验发现：(1)模型在67.3%的案例中达成显式目标，但仅在26.4%的案例中遵守了隐含规范；(2)性能差距的根源不在于缺乏一般性社会知识，而在于难以在具体情境中激活和锚定相关规范；(3)NormPerceptor将整体任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。

Conclusion: 具身智能体需要具备主动检测隐含规范、将其扎根于视觉证据、并作为行动规划约束加以整合的能力。NormPerceptor的显著提升验证了这一思路的有效性，但46.7%的成功率仍表明该领域存在巨大的提升空间。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [235] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SD-GPS 是一个求解器驱动的神经符号几何推理框架，通过将符号求解器作为执行预言机贯穿形式化与演绎两阶段，解决了自形式化解耦不足和定理预测中演绎僵局两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经符号几何推理框架存在两个核心瓶颈：(1) 自形式化被当作静态多模态翻译任务，与下游求解器兼容性脱节；(2) 定理预测阶段因固定规则库导致求解器频繁陷入演绎僵局。

Method: 提出 SD-GPS 框架，包含两大组件：(1) 求解器驱动的自形式化（Solver-Driven Autoformalization），基于 QwenVL3-2B，将有监督形式语言适应与可解性引导的强化学习统一为单一模块，以可执行性作为核心训练信号；(2) 验证式定理提议（Verified Theorem Proposing），引入一个僵局感知代理，从当前证明状态提出局部辅助引理，并通过符号验证过滤所有提议以确保可靠性。

Result: 在 Geometry3K 和 PGPS9K 数据集上，SD-GPS 在标准补全、多项选择和跨模态参考三种评测范式下均一致优于现有 MLLM、纯神经网络和神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 将多模态感知与符号执行形成闭环能够显著提升几何推理能力，并为神经代理如何通过形式系统获得可验证的问题求解能力提供了深刻洞见。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor.
